cols-3
The single write-path for the platform. Implements the 11-step governance chain. Verdict generation: AUTO_EXECUTE / PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM / REQUIRE_APPROVAL / BLOCK. Idempotency, saga compensation, outbox pattern.
OPA Â· Sentinel Â· WORM Â· SQL Â· Redis
Agent Orchestrator
8-step context assembly for every AI call. PHI tokenization at LLM boundary. Tool dispatch to Action Gateway. Conversation memory, persona injection, context-window budget.
Azure OpenAI Â· Thread Intel Â· KV
Thread Intelligence
Sole writer to Practice Snapshot (Redis, 30s stale max). Compresses context window. Maintains Practice Memory: outcome / pattern / preference / anomaly records. Drives autonomy expansion.
Redis Â· SQL Â· Event Bus
Sentinel Â· PRS
6-dimensional Practice Risk Score: Articulation Â· Adjustment Â· Behavioral Â· Cash Chain Â· Payroll Â· Vendor. Floor conditions raise verdicts when PRS > 70. Hysteresis prevents flapping.

Delivery Service

OPA Policy Engine
Open Policy Agent sidecar to Action Gateway. .rego policy files per persona Ã CI-type Ã action. Per-tenant policy overlays. Fail-closed default.
Sidecar to Action Gateway
Immutable hash-chain audit trail. SHA-256 chained per record. 7-year retention via Azure Immutable Blob (compliance-mode lock). HIPAA, SOC2, RAIVS patent.
Storage Account Â· Key Vault
Outbox Relay
Transactional outbox pattern for guaranteed event delivery. Drains SQL outbox table to Service Bus. Survives crashes, prevents lost events.
SQL Â· Service Bus
Service Bus topics: oscar.operational Â· oscar.governance Â· oscar.telemetry. Subscription filters generated from manifests. 33 canonical event types, 15-field envelope.
Service Bus Â· manifest-to-sb-filters
Internal event observer. Subscribes to operational events and triggers conditions/cards: appointment-unconfirmed, slot-opened, claim-aged-without-response, lab-case-overdue, sterilizer-test-due. Drives Quick Fill and similar reactive workflows. Note: payer-portal scraping is a SEPARATE sibling product (the Payer Portal), not Watcher.

}

const REQ_STATE = {
  moduleId: null,
  subId: null,
  sortKey: 'phaseStatus',  
  sortDir: 'asc',
  filterStatus: 'all',
  filterPhase: 'all',
  searchTerm: '',
};

const STATUS_ORDER = { 'Live Verified': 0, 'Code Present, Untested': 1, 'In Progress': 2, 'Not Started': 3 };
const PHASE_ORDER  = { 'P1': 0, 'P2': 1, 'P3': 2 };

function applyReqSort(reqs) {
  const arr = [...reqs];
  const key = REQ_STATE.sortKey;
  const dir = REQ_STATE.sortDir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
  arr.sort((a, b) => {
    let av, bv;
    if (key === 'reqId') { av = a.reqId; bv = b.reqId; }
    else if (key === 'phase') { av = PHASE_ORDER[a.phase] ?? 99; bv = PHASE_ORDER[b.phase] ?? 99; }
    else if (key === 'status') { av = STATUS_ORDER[a.status] ?? 99; bv = STATUS_ORDER[b.status] ?? 99; }
    else if (key === 'phaseStatus') {
      
      av = (PHASE_ORDER[a.phase] ?? 99) * 10 + (3 - (STATUS_ORDER[a.status] ?? 99));
      bv = (PHASE_ORDER[b.phase] ?? 99) * 10 + (3 - (STATUS_ORDER[b.status] ?? 99));
    }
    else { av = a[key] || ''; bv = b[key] || ''; }
    if (av < bv) return -1 * dir;
    if (av > bv) return  1 * dir;
    return 0;
  });
  return arr;
}

function applyReqFilter(reqs) {
  return reqs.filter(r => {
    if (REQ_STATE.filterStatus !== 'all' && r.status !== REQ_STATE.filterStatus) return false;
    if (REQ_STATE.filterPhase !== 'all' && r.phase !== REQ_STATE.filterPhase) return false;
    if (REQ_STATE.searchTerm) {
      const t = REQ_STATE.searchTerm.toLowerCase();
      const hay = (r.reqId + ' ' + r.requirement + ' ' + r.source + ' ' + r.evidence).toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(t)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}

function renderRequirementsTable(reqs) {
  const filtered = applyReqFilter(reqs);
  const sorted = applyReqSort(filtered);
  const sortKey = REQ_STATE.sortKey;
  const dir = REQ_STATE.sortDir === 'asc' ? 'â' : 'â';
  const arrow = (k) => sortKey === k ?
sort-arrow
:
;
  const headerCls = (k) => sortKey === k ? 'sorted' : '';

  return
req-table-wrap
req-table
reqId
${headerCls('reqId')}
phase
${headerCls('phase')}
status
${headerCls('status')}
<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center; color: var(--muted); padding: 20px;">No requirements match the current filters</td></tr>
<tr>
                <td class="req-id">${r.reqId}</td>
                <td class="req-text">${inlineMd(r.requirement)}</td>
                <td class="req-source">${inlineMd(r.source)}</td>
                <td class="req-phase">${r.phase}</td>
                <td><span class="status-badge ${statusToClass(r.status)}">${r.status}</span></td>
                <td class="req-evidence">${inlineMd(r.evidence)}</td>
              </tr>
;
}

  const lv = s['Live Verified'] || 0;
  const pct = (n) => Math.round((n / total) * 100) + '%';

  const featuresHtml = subData.features && subData.features.length ?

    ${featuresHtml}
    ${oqHtml}
  `;

  
  const tableMount = document.getElementById('req-table-mount');
  const searchEl = detail.querySelector('.req-search');
  const countEl = document.getElementById('req-result-count');

  function rerenderTable() {
    const filtered = applyReqFilter(subData.requirements);
    countEl.textContent = `${filtered.length} of ${subData.requirements.length}`;
    tableMount.innerHTML = renderRequirementsTable(subData.requirements);
    wireSortHeaders();
  }

  function wireSortHeaders() {
    tableMount.querySelectorAll('th[data-sort]').forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const k = th.dataset.sort;
        if (REQ_STATE.sortKey === k) {
          REQ_STATE.sortDir = REQ_STATE.sortDir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
          REQ_STATE.sortKey = k;
          REQ_STATE.sortDir = 'asc';
        }
        rerenderTable();
      });
    });
  }
  wireSortHeaders();

  detail.querySelectorAll('.filter-pill').forEach(p => {
    p.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const f = p.dataset.filter;
      const v = p.dataset.value;
      if (f === 'status') REQ_STATE.filterStatus = v;
      if (f === 'phase')  REQ_STATE.filterPhase  = v;
      
      detail.querySelectorAll(`.filter-pill[data-filter=
]`).forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
      p.classList.add('active');
      rerenderTable();
    });
  });

  if (searchEl) {
    searchEl.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      REQ_STATE.searchTerm = e.target.value;
      rerenderTable();
    });
  }

  
  countEl.textContent = `${subData.requirements.length} of ${subData.requirements.length}`;
}





function navigate(id) {
  const overview = document.getElementById('overview');
  const detail   = document.getElementById('detail');
  const crumb    = document.getElementById('breadcrumb');

  if (!id || id === 'overview') {
    overview.classList.add('active');
    detail.classList.remove('active');
    crumb.innerHTML = '<a class=
data-nav=
>Overview</a>';
    location.hash = '';
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    return;
  }

  
  const l3Match = id.match(/^(m\d+)-(\d+\.\d+)$/);
  if (l3Match) {
    const moduleId = l3Match[1];
    const subId = l3Match[2];
    
    if (REQ_STATE.subId !== subId || REQ_STATE.moduleId !== moduleId) {
      REQ_STATE.filterStatus = 'all';
      REQ_STATE.filterPhase = 'all';
      REQ_STATE.searchTerm = '';
      REQ_STATE.sortKey = 'phaseStatus';
      REQ_STATE.sortDir = 'asc';
    }
    renderSubmoduleDetail(moduleId, subId);
    overview.classList.remove('active');
    detail.classList.add('active');

    
    const subData = getSubmoduleData(moduleId, subId);
    const moduleDetail = DETAIL[moduleId];
    const moduleCrumb = moduleDetail ? moduleDetail.crumb[moduleDetail.crumb.length - 1] : moduleId.toUpperCase();
    const subName = subData ? subData.name : subId;
    crumb.innerHTML =
      '<a class=
>Overview</a>' +
      '<span class=
>/</span>' +
      `<a class=
>${moduleCrumb}</a>` +
      '<span class=
>/</span>' +
      `${subId} Â· ${subName}`;

    location.hash = '#' + id;
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    return;
  }

  
  renderDetail(id);
  overview.classList.remove('active');
  detail.classList.add('active');

  const d = DETAIL[id];
  const trail = d ? d.crumb : ['Drill-down'];
  crumb.innerHTML =
    '<a class=
>Overview</a>' +
    '<span class=
>/</span>' +
    trail.map((c, i) => i === trail.length-1 ? c : `<span>${c}</span><span class=
#
click
[data-nav]
hashchange

## subModules

## 1.1
id: 1.1
name: Patient Demographics
purpose: Legal name, preferred name, DOB, sex/gender identity, race/ethnicity per USCDI v3, language, decedent flag. Patient photo for operatory recognition. Duplicate-record merge/unmerge. **This is the origin record for every other reference to a patient throughout OSCAR.** Patient-merge operations cascade through M3 (appointments), M4 (chart), M5 (imaging), M6 (ledger), M7 (comms history), M8 (documents).
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Patient` (v2/v3 — D1 §P.2), `DPIM_PatientDemographics` (D1 §P.3), `DPIM_PatientPhoto` (v3 §1.6 — property-doc-pending), `DPIM_DuplicatePatientCandidate` (v3 §3.12 governance — property-doc-pending), `DPIM_PatientStatusChange` (governance — D1 implied), `DPIM_AuditEntry` (M11 audit chain).
externalIntegrations: USPS Address Validation (subject to M1.2 contact), ID-document OCR (e.g., Onfido / IDology — vendor TBD), photo-capture device (operatory webcam / kiosk camera).

## requirements

### [0]

Candidates above match-score threshold SHALL surface as ActionCard to office_manager for manual review.

### [15]
reqId: M1-DEM-EXT-016
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support patient-record merge: a manual review path triggered by M1-DEM-EXT-015. **Merge SHALL cascade across modules** — single canonical patient record after merge: (a) M3 reassigns appointments to surviving record; (b) M4 reassigns chart entries; (c) M5 reassigns DICOM studies; (d) M6 reassigns ledger entries + claims + payments; (e) M7 reassigns CommLog and ConversationThread; (f) M8 reassigns FormInstance and Attachment; (g) all events emit `dpim.person.patient.merged` with both surviving and absorbed patient IDs (tokenized at SDK gate). Merge SHALL be auditable in WORM with full delta.
source: v3 LOCKED §1 (PatientStatusChange linkage); HIPAA §164.526 (Right to Amendment); cross-module cascade
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Merge cascade engine absent; **single largest cross-module operation in M1**

### [16]
reqId: M1-DEM-EXT-017
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support patient-record unmerge — reverse a prior merge if it was performed in error. Unmerge SHALL restore the original two records and reverse the cascade. Unmerge SHALL require `office_manager` or higher persona authorization.
source: HIPAA §164.526 implication
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [17]

source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; cross-module with PS-7 WORM, PS-9 Event Routing

### [18]

requirement: Patient-record creation SHALL go through `ctx.governed_write` with action_class `person.write`, action_type `patient.create`. Verdict matrix SHALL be enforced (per DPIM v3 §10): dentist_clinical=AUTO_EXECUTE, hygienist=BLOCK, front_office=AUTO_EXECUTE, billing=BLOCK, office_manager=AUTO_EXECUTE, owner=AUTO_EXECUTE.

source: HIPAA §164.526; D1 §P.0 audit-level=full-delta
phase: P1

## 1.2
id: 1.2
name: Patient Contact
purpose: Multiple addresses (USPS validated), phones with per-number SMS consent (TCPA), emails with channel-specific marketing/clinical/financial opt-in (CAN-SPAM). Quiet hours per TCPA. **TCPA consent at the per-phone-number level, not per-patient.** CAN-SPAM at the per-email-address level with per-purpose opt-in.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_PatientContact` (D1 §P.4 — to be confirmed in v3 update), `DPIM_TCPAConsent` (v3 §8.5 — class lives in M7 Communication; consent grant origin for new patients lives in M1.2), `DPIM_OptOut` / `DPIM_SuppressionList` (v3 §8 — M7 enforcement layer), `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (v3 §8 — preference store).
externalIntegrations: USPS Address Validation API, M7 Communications (consent enforcement at outbound-message gateway), regulatory frameworks (TCPA, CAN-SPAM).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support multiple addresses per patient with type discriminator: home, work, billing, mailing. Each address SHALL be validated via USPS Address Validation API; failed validation SHALL be flagged for staff review (not blocking). Sensitivity: pii.
source: DPIM D1 §P.3 (PatientDemographics → addresses); USPS API
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [1]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support multiple phone numbers per patient: mobile, home, work. Each phone number SHALL have its own consent state per `DPIM_TCPAConsent` keyed by `(phoneNumber, channel, purposeCategory)` where channel ∈ {sms, voice, both} and purposeCategory ∈ {appointment_reminder, recall_outreach, marketing, clinical_followup, financial, general}. **Consent state is per-phone-number, not per-patient.**
source: DPIM v3 §8.5 (TCPAConsent); TCPA 47 USC 227; FCC TCPA orders
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM v3 class defined; M1-side consent grant flow not built

### [2]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL capture TCPA consent at intake via signed acknowledgement. **`expressWritten=true` SHALL be required for `purposeCategory=marketing`** (TCPA mandate for marketing). Consent record SHALL include the verbatim disclosure language displayed at signing — locked at consent for legal evidence. Witness signature timestamp + IP + device fingerprint SHALL be captured.
source: TCPA 47 USC 227; 47 CFR 64.1200; DPIM v3 §8.5 props 8.5.6, 8.5.14, 8.5.8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM v3 §8.5 specifies the structure; M1 capture flow not built

### [3]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support multiple email addresses per patient with per-channel-per-purpose opt-in: clinical communications (e.g., post-op instructions), financial communications (e.g., statements), marketing communications (e.g., newsletter). **CAN-SPAM 15 USC 7701 requires affirmative opt-in for marketing** and an unsubscribe mechanism on every marketing email.
source: CAN-SPAM 15 USC 7701; DPIM v3 §8 CommunicationPreference
phase: P1

### [4]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL store `preferredContactMethod` (code: phone / email / sms / mail / portal) and `preferredContactTime` (code: morning / afternoon / evening / anytime). **Agent MUST honor these for all outbound communications** (per DPIM D1 §P.2 agentic-constraint annotation on prop 2.18). Failure to honor is a compliance issue.
source: DPIM D1 §P.2 props 2.18, 2.19
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: D1 explicitly marks this as agentic-constraint

### [5]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce TCPA quiet hours (default 8am–9pm patient-local time per TCPA + state expansions like FL/OK 8am–8pm). Quiet-hours enforcement SHALL block outbound SMS/voice initiated outside the window — failure to honor is a per-call TCPA violation. Quiet-hours window SHALL be configurable per-tenant for stricter state regulations.
source: TCPA 47 USC 227(b)(1)(B); FCC TCPA Order; per-state restrictions
phase: P1
status: Not Started

### [6]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce TCPA frequency caps — per-patient per-day SMS frequency limit (default 3/day) and per-patient per-week voice-call cap (default 7/week). Configurable per-tenant. Caps SHALL apply across `purposeCategory` boundaries (a marketing SMS plus an appointment reminder count toward the same daily cap).
source: FCC TCPA Order (per-recipient limits)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M7

### [7]
reqId: M1-CONT-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL process inbound SMS keywords STOP / END / QUIT / UNSUBSCRIBE / OPT OUT — automatic transition of the matching `DPIM_TCPAConsent` to `revoked` status with revocation timestamp + revocation method + keyword used. **Revocation SHALL be honored within seconds; future SMS sends to that phone for that purposeCategory SHALL BLOCK at the gateway.**
source: TCPA 47 USC 227; FCC TCPA Order; DPIM v3 §8.5 props 8.5.10, 8.5.11, 8.5.12
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M7 (handles inbound-SMS revocation processing)

## openQuestions

### [0]
id: OQ-1.2.1
text: TCPA enforcement (M1-CONT-EXT-006, 007, 008) is **HARD-required for legal SMS sends.** No M7 communication can fire without it. Critical-path P1 — pin to the same Phase 0 commitment that covered RCM `pms.*` and M3 missing appointment events.

### [1]
id: OQ-1.2.2
text: Inbound-SMS keyword revocation (M1-CONT-EXT-008) requires a webhook handler at the SMS provider boundary (Twilio + Azure Communication Services). Architectural decision: where does the inbound-SMS handler live? M7 Delivery (logical — it's where outbound originates), M1.2 (logical — it's the consent origin), or split (M7 handles the SMS payload, M1 receives the consent-revocation event). Recommend M7 owns the webhook, emits a `consent.revoke_request` event to M1 which authoritatively flips `DPIM_TCPAConsent`. ---

## 1.3
id: 1.3
name: Responsible Party / Guarantor
purpose: Self / parent / guardian / spouse / other relationship type. Independent demographic record (may not be a patient). HIPAA designated representative authorization (consent on behalf of). Financial responsibility assignment per child / dependent. Power of attorney / legal-guardian documentation. Court-order documentation for split families. **Distinct from 1.4 Family / Household Linking** — 1.3 is who pays / who can give consent; 1.4 is the household grouping.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_ResponsibleParty` (D1 §P.5 — to confirm in v3 update), `DPIM_Guarantor` (D1 §P.6), `DPIM_Insurable` (v3 abstract — Patient + RP can be subscribers), `DPIM_ConsentRecord` (M8 — RP-as-designated-rep authorization), `DPIM_FinancialAgreement` (v3 §7.8 — RP signs).
externalIntegrations: M6 RCM (financial responsibility), M8 Documents (legal-doc storage for POA / guardianship / court orders).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support 0..* `DPIM_ResponsibleParty` records per patient. RP relationship type SHALL be: self / parent / guardian / spouse / sibling / other. RP record SHALL be independent of `DPIM_Patient` — an RP may exist without being a patient (e.g., parent of pediatric patient who is not themselves a patient at the practice).
source: DPIM D1 §P.5; common dental PMS pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined; relationship-type discriminator + non-patient-RP support

### [1]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL capture HIPAA designated representative authorization on the `DPIM_ResponsibleParty` record. Authorization SHALL be signed via `DPIM_Signature` (M8) with explicit scope: discuss-PHI / receive-records / sign-on-behalf / all. Per HIPAA §164.510(b) and §164.502(g) for personal representatives.
source: HIPAA §164.510(b), §164.502(g); DPIM v3 §8 (Signature, ConsentRecord)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [2]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support 1..* `DPIM_Guarantor` records per patient (financial-responsibility role — distinct from RP-as-consent-authority). Guarantor SHALL link to a patient account in M6.9 (DPIM_PatientAccount).
source: DPIM D1 §P.6; cross-module with M6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [3]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support per-dependent financial-responsibility assignment for split families. **Use case:** divorced parents where insurance flows through one parent and out-of-pocket flows through another, or court-ordered cost-sharing. Cost-sharing percentages SHALL be configurable per dependent per RP.
source: Common dental practice scenario; cross-module with M6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [4]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL store legal documentation linking RP to patient in `DPIM_Attachment` (M8): power-of-attorney documents, legal-guardian orders, court orders for custody, divorce decrees with cost-sharing terms. Documents SHALL be retention-tagged per state requirements (typically 7+ years post-relationship-end).
source: HIPAA §164.526; state legal-document retention rules
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M8

### [5]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.responsible_party.created`, `.updated`, `.changed` events when RP relationships are added, modified, or terminated. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.** Specifically `responsible_party.changed` SHALL fire when financial-responsibility-share changes (driving M6.7 A/R re-allocation) or when designated-rep authorization scope changes.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; cross-module with M6 + M8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Same `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap pattern

### [6]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.subscriber_relationship.changed` event when patient↔subscriber linkage changes (dependent ages out, divorce changes financial responsibility, employer plan change affects dependent coverage). **M6 SHALL invalidate cached eligibility results on this event** (per the M1↔M6 boundary clarification).
source: M1↔M6 boundary per checkpoint 2026-05-08; cross-module with M6.1
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: New event type — needs DPIM event-taxonomy registration + AG ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT mapping

### [7]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL detect minor patients (age <18 derived from DOB) and SHALL require at least one `DPIM_ResponsibleParty` with `parent` or `guardian` type **before any encounter-related governed write succeeds.** Verdict matrix SHALL gate encounter creation on RP presence for minors.
source: HIPAA §164.502(g)(2); state minor-consent rules
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [8]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL handle aged-out dependent transitions automatically: when a patient turns 18, system SHALL flag the RP-required gate as no-longer-required and SHALL emit `dpim.person.subscriber_relationship.changed` (per M1-RESP-EXT-007) so M6 can re-verify eligibility (insurance dependent coverage typically ends at 19 or 26 depending on plan).
source: Insurance industry standard; cross-module with M6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [9]
reqId: M1-RESP-EXT-010
requirement: RP creation SHALL go through `ctx.governed_write` with action_class `person.write`, action_type `responsible_party.create`. Verdict matrix SHALL be enforced (verdict matrix entry needed in registry).
source: DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: New action_type — verdict matrix entry missing in `dpim-v3-registry.json`

## 1.4
id: 1.4
name: Family / Household Linking
purpose: Family head-of-household designation, family ledger view aggregating all linked patients, shared insurance subscription model, family appointment booking, sibling/family discount eligibility, move-individual-out-of-family / re-link. **Distinct from 1.3 Responsible Party** — 1.4 is the household grouping; 1.3 is who pays / who can give consent.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Family` (D2 — under Family domain), `DPIM_FamilyLink` (D2), `DPIM_Patient` (linked via familyRef), `DPIM_PatientAccount` (M6 — family ledger view).
externalIntegrations: M6 RCM (family ledger view, shared subscription), M3 Scheduling (family-block bookings), M7 Communications (family communication digests).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support `DPIM_Family` records — the household grouping. A family SHALL have one head-of-household designation. Patients SHALL link to a family via `DPIM_FamilyLink` records with role discriminator (head, spouse, child, dependent, other). A patient SHALL belong to 0..1 families at any time.
source: DPIM D2 §Family domain; common PMS pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined; family-link engine not built

### [1]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL provide a family ledger view aggregating all linked patients' patient accounts. The view SHALL be readable by office_manager / billing / front_desk personas; patients SHALL see their own ledger plus aggregated view of dependents (per HIPAA §164.502(g) personal-representative rules, parents see minor children's records).
source: HIPAA §164.502(g); cross-module with M6.9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [2]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support shared insurance subscription — a single `DPIM_Coverage` (M6) record SHALL be linkable to multiple patients in a family (subscriber + dependents). Subscriber SHALL be designated; dependents SHALL have dependent codes. **Subscription change events SHALL emit `dpim.person.subscriber_relationship.changed` per M1-RESP-EXT-007.**
source: DPIM v3 Coverage (M6); cross-module with M6.1
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [3]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support family-block appointment booking — Sarah books multiple family members in adjacent or same-time slots in a single workflow. Family-block bookings SHALL be visible as a group on the calendar.
source: SCH-NP-006 (Family Scheduling Coordinator); cross-module with M3.1
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [4]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support sibling / family discount eligibility — practice-configurable rule set (e.g., "10% discount on second child's services in same visit"). Discount application SHALL flow through M6.12 Adjustment Tracking (Adjustment subclass: CourtesyWriteOff).
source: Practice marketing pattern; cross-module with M6.12
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [5]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support family deconfliction when family members have separate insurance vs shared subscription. Specifically: the family-link DOES NOT imply shared insurance — each patient maintains their own `DPIM_Coverage` records. Family ledger aggregates patient accounts; insurance plans are independent unless explicitly linked via subscriber-relationship.
source: M1↔M6 boundary clarification; common split-family scenario
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Critical for divorced families

### [6]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support move-individual-out-of-family and re-link operations (e.g., adult child moves out, divorce, remarriage). Move-out SHALL: (a) remove `DPIM_FamilyLink` for that patient; (b) preserve patient's individual record and history; (c) emit `dpim.family.family_link.removed` event; (d) NOT delete or modify ledger entries already attributed to the patient.
source: Common life-event scenario; HIPAA §164.526 implication
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [7]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support family-level communication digests — combined email/SMS for multiple family-member appointments (e.g., "Reminder: Sarah, Tommy, and Emma have appointments tomorrow"). Digest SHALL respect each individual patient's communication preferences (M1.2) and TCPA consents.
source: DPIM v3 §8 (Family Communication M7.14); cross-module with M7
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [8]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit family-domain events: `dpim.family.family.created`, `dpim.family.family_link.added`, `dpim.family.family_link.removed`, `dpim.family.head_of_household.changed`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.**
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap

### [9]
reqId: M1-FAM-EXT-010
requirement: Family creation and family-link operations SHALL go through `ctx.governed_write`. Verdict matrix entries needed for new action_types: `family.create`, `family_link.add`, `family_link.remove`.
source: DPIM v3 §10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Verdict matrix entries missing

## 1.5
id: 1.5
name: Patient Risk Profile
purpose: Caries risk score, perio risk score, medical complexity score, treatment-acceptance propensity, no-show risk, financial risk, behavioral flags. Feeds Sentinel PRS dimensions and AI-ranked worklists. **Different from M5 Imaging risk and M4 Clinical risk — this is the patient-level composite that aggregates clinical, behavioral, and financial risk for cross-module use.**
dpimClasses: `DPIM_PatientRiskProfile` (D1 §P.7 — to confirm), `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3 — for AI-derived risk scoring), `DPIM_AIRecommendation` (M11 — risk-driven recommendations), `DPIM_PracticeRiskScore` (Sentinel — patient-risk inputs roll up).
externalIntegrations: PS-2 Agent Orchestrator (AI risk scoring), PS-4 Sentinel (Behavioral dimension consumes patient no-show / financial-risk signals), M3.13 (no-show risk feeds AI overbooking), M6.7 (financial risk feeds A/R worklist priority).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PatientRiskProfile` per patient with the following composite scores: caries risk (CAMBRA-aligned), periodontal risk (AAP/EFP 2017-aligned), medical complexity (ASA-aligned), treatment-acceptance propensity, no-show risk, financial risk, behavioral flags. **Sensitivity: phi.**
source: HTML m1 §1.5 description; DPIM D1 §P.7; CAMBRA / AAP/EFP standards
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined; scoring engine not built

### [1]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-002
requirement: Caries and periodontal risk scores SHALL be derived from M4 Clinical (future-stub) — chart conditions, perio probings, prior-treatment patterns. **In Phase 1 (M4 future-stub), scores SHALL be manually-entered placeholders;** Phase 2 introduces AI-derived scoring.
source: Phase 1 fallback per M4 future-stub status
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 Clinical is future-stub

### [2]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-003
requirement: Medical-complexity score SHALL be derived from `DPIM_MedicalHistory` (1.7) using ASA classification (I–VI). Score updates SHALL emit `dpim.person.patient_risk_profile.updated` event. **Event payload SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** (subscribers receive risk-score and patient-token, not raw medical history).
source: ASA Physical Status Classification System; cross-ref M1.7
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [3]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-004
requirement: No-show risk score SHALL be the per-patient component of the M3.13 SCH-NS-001 ML model output. **M1.5 stores the score; M3.13 produces it.** Score SHALL be readable by M3.13 and feed AI gap-fill candidate ranking (M3.13 SCH-GAP-003 weighting).
source: M3 SCH-NS-001 + SCH-NS-006; cross-module with M3.13
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M3.13 ML model not built

### [4]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-005
requirement: Financial risk score (collection probability) SHALL be derived from M6.7 RCM-AR-006 collection-probability scoring. **M1.5 stores the patient-level aggregate; M6.7 produces per-claim probabilities.** Score SHALL feed M6.7 worklist prioritization and statement-cadence escalation.
source: M6 RCM-AR-006; cross-module with M6.7
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M6.7 not started

### [5]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-006
requirement: Treatment-acceptance propensity SHALL be derived from historical treatment-plan acceptance vs presentation patterns (cross-module with M4 TreatmentPlan). Phase 1 fallback: simple ratio (accepted$ / presented$ over trailing 24 months); Phase 2 introduces AI propensity model.
source: Common dental analytics; cross-module with M4 + M6.10
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 future-stub limits Phase 1

### [6]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-007
requirement: Behavioral flags SHALL be a code-set tag list: `sedation_needed`, `dental_anxiety`, `special_needs`, `language_assistance_required`, `transportation_assistance`, `behavior_management_pediatric`, `cooperation_concern`, `litigation_risk`. Flags SHALL be staff-set with audit chain. **Flags SHALL feed M3.11 reminder content personalization (M3-CFM-006) and M3.13 scheduling intelligence.**
source: HTML m1 §1.5; common dental practice patterns
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [7]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-008
requirement: Risk profile SHALL feed Sentinel PS-4 PRS scoring — specifically the **Behavioral** dimension (no-show rate, cancellation rate, behavioral flags) and the **Articulation** dimension (treatment-acceptance propensity). Sentinel PS-4 stays upstream of M12; M12.10 Embezzlement Risk Score consumes Sentinel output, not patient-risk directly.
source: DPIM v3 §13 PracticeRiskScore; cross-module with PS-4 + M12
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with PS-4 Sentinel

### [8]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-009
requirement: Risk profile changes SHALL emit `dpim.person.patient_risk_profile.updated` events. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.** Subscribers (M3.13, M6.7, PS-4) receive the score updates without raw PHI context.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap

### [9]
reqId: M1-RISK-EXT-010
requirement: Risk profile SHALL be visible only to clinical, billing, office_manager, and owner personas. Patient SHALL NOT see their own risk profile in cleartext (per agentic-access=`context-only` for this CI).
source: DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; HIPAA principle of minimum-necessary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Verdict matrix entry needed

## 1.6
id: 1.6
name: Patient Portal Identity
purpose: Account creation/invitation/claim, password / passkey / MFA, linked-patient roster (parent viewing children), per-feature portal permissions, login audit. **Patient portal identity is its own auth domain — separate from staff auth.** Patient uses Microsoft Entra B2C (consumer); staff uses Entra workforce.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_PatientPortalAccount` (D1 §P.9 — Identity domain), `DPIM_UserAccount` (D1 §P.8 — distinguish staff vs patient), `DPIM_OAuthGrant` (v3 — for SSO with Apple/Google/Microsoft consumer), `DPIM_AuditEntry` (login audit), `DPIM_ConsentRecord` (M8 — portal terms acceptance).
externalIntegrations: Microsoft Entra B2C (patient auth), Apple ID / Google / Microsoft consumer SSO providers, MFA provider (TOTP / passkey / WebAuthn).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL provide patient-portal account creation via three paths: (a) **invitation** — staff sends link, patient claims; (b) **self-registration** — patient initiates from public website with email + DOB + last-name verification; (c) **linked-patient claim** — parent claims minor child's portal account and links to own account.
source: DPIM D1 §P.9; common patient portal pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [1]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL authenticate patients via **Microsoft Entra B2C** (consumer identity) — SEPARATE from staff Entra workforce identity. **Cross-domain authentication is BLOCKED.** A patient SHALL NOT be able to authenticate to staff console; staff SHALL NOT authenticate to patient portal with workforce credentials.
source: HIPAA §164.312(d) authentication; defense-in-depth principle
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [2]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL require multi-factor authentication for patient portal: TOTP / passkey (WebAuthn) / SMS-OTP. **Passkey SHALL be the preferred mechanism per OSCAR Phase 1 stance** (post-quantum-ready, phishing-resistant). SMS-OTP SHALL be available as fallback but flagged with security warning per NIST SP 800-63B AAL2 guidance.
source: NIST SP 800-63B; HIPAA §164.312(d)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [3]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support consumer SSO with Apple ID, Google, Microsoft (consumer Microsoft Account, not Entra workforce) — `DPIM_OAuthGrant` records issued per SSO provider per patient. **SSO grants SHALL be revocable independently** (revoke Google but keep Apple).
source: DPIM v3 §1 Identity (OAuthGrant); patient UX standard
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined; SSO integration P2

### [4]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support linked-patient roster on portal accounts — a parent's portal account SHALL be linkable to 0..* minor children's records. **Per HIPAA §164.502(g)(2) and §164.524(a)(3)(i), parental access to minor children's PHI is gated by state law and provider judgment.** Linked-children access SHALL respect state-of-practice age-of-majority rules and adolescent-specific privacy carve-outs (e.g., reproductive health, mental health, substance abuse — typically state-specific 12+ age).
source: HIPAA §164.502(g)(2); state minor-confidentiality laws
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: State-specific rules engine needed

### [5]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-006
requirement: Per-feature portal permissions SHALL be configurable: appointment-self-service, statement-view, payment-make, form-complete, message-staff, family-roster-view, document-download. Permissions SHALL be granular per linked-patient (e.g., parent can pay for minor child but cannot view minor child's mental-health notes per state carve-out).
source: HIPAA minimum-necessary principle
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [6]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain a login audit trail per portal account: timestamp, IP, device, geolocation, success/failure, MFA method used. Audit entries SHALL be retained for 6 years (HIPAA + Cures Act). Failed login attempts SHALL trigger `DPIM_ComplianceEvent` after configurable threshold (default: 5 fails in 15 min) with optional account-lockout.
source: HIPAA §164.312(b); 21st Century Cures Act access logs
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [7]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support portal-account suspension and termination. Suspension SHALL preserve account state but block login; termination SHALL be recoverable for 30 days then permanently delete the portal-side account record (the underlying `DPIM_Patient` is unaffected). All suspend / terminate events SHALL emit to `oscar.governance` topic with hash-chained audit.
source: HIPAA §164.524 right-of-access (cannot delete underlying records); Cures Act
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [8]
reqId: M1-PORT-EXT-009

## 1.7
id: 1.7
name: Medical History
purpose: Chronic conditions (SNOMED-CT), pregnancy/lactation, premedication requirements per ADA/AHA prophylaxis guidelines, ROS, surgical history, re-attestation cadence. **Re-attestation cadence is a regulatory requirement — most states require re-attestation at every visit or at minimum annually.**
dpimClasses: `DPIM_MedicalHistory` (D1 §P.X — under PatientAggregate v3 abstract), `DPIM_PregnancyEpisode` (v3 new — phi-sensitive), `DPIM_AuditEntry` (re-attestation chain).
externalIntegrations: SNOMED-CT (terminology binding), ADA/AHA prophylaxis guidelines (premedication rules engine).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_MedicalHistory` per patient with: chronic conditions (SNOMED-CT bound), surgical history, hospitalizations, family history (SNOMED-CT for genetic-relevant conditions), review-of-systems (patient-reported). **Sensitivity: phi.**
source: DPIM v3 §1.7 (PatientAggregate child); SNOMED-CT
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined

### [1]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track pregnancy/lactation status as `DPIM_PregnancyEpisode` (v3 new — phi-sensitive). Episodes SHALL have estimated due date, episode-active flag, complications (SNOMED-CT). **Sensitivity: phi-sensitive.** Drug-prescribing (M1.9) and imaging (M5) SHALL hard-stop on active pregnancy without provider override.
source: DPIM v3 §1.7 (PregnancyEpisode); ADA/AHA guidance
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined

### [2]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL evaluate premedication-required flag based on ADA/AHA prophylaxis guidelines: artificial joint < 2 years, prosthetic heart valve, history of infective endocarditis, congenital cardiac conditions, cardiac transplant w/ valvulopathy. Flag SHALL be a hard-stop on procedures requiring prophylaxis (per `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` rules in M1.10).
source: ADA/AHA Premedication Guidelines
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Premedication-evaluation engine not built

### [3]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-004
requirement: Medical history re-attestation SHALL fire per configurable cadence: per-visit (default for high-risk patients), every 12 months (default for standard patients), or per-state regulatory minimum. Re-attestation SHALL be patient-acknowledged via portal/SMS link or staff-assisted at check-in. **Each re-attestation SHALL emit `dpim.person.medical_history.attested` event with attestation timestamp + attestation method (patient-portal / sms / staff-assisted).**
source: State minor-consent rules (varies); industry standard
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap

### [4]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track medical-history version chain — every re-attestation produces a new version; differences from prior version SHALL be highlighted to clinical staff (delta view). Audit-level: full-delta with WORM hash chain.
source: HIPAA §164.526; D1 audit-level=full-delta
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [5]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.medical_history.updated`, `dpim.person.medical_history.attested`, `dpim.person.pregnancy_episode.created`, `dpim.person.pregnancy_episode.ended`. **All events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.** Pregnancy events SHALL flow with extra phi-sensitive marking.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [6]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface medical-history alerts on chart-open per `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` (M1.10). Alerts SHALL be acknowledged by provider before encounter writes proceed.
source: Cross-ref M1.10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [7]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support medical-history templated capture (intake form structure) — system-supplied baseline questionnaire with practice-customizable additions. Form SHALL be usable in patient-self-service (portal) and staff-assisted (kiosk / iPad) modes.
source: Common dental intake pattern; cross-module with M8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M8

### [8]
reqId: M1-MED-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support medical-history import from external sources via `DPIM_DataExportRequest`-inverse pattern: patient brings records from another provider, OSCAR ingests via FHIR R4 (Patient Summary IPS where available) or scanned-document OCR.
source: Cures Act interoperability
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence:

## 1.8
id: 1.8
name: Allergies & Adverse Reactions
purpose: Drug (RxNorm), material (latex, nickel, dental materials per SNODENT), environmental, with severity / onset / source. Hard-stop on prescribe.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Allergy` (D1 §P.X — PatientAggregate child), `DPIM_Material` (M4 — clinical-domain material catalog), `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` (M1.10 — surfaced alerts).
externalIntegrations: RxNorm (drug allergens), SNODENT (dental material allergens), SNOMED-CT (environmental allergens).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Allergy` records per patient with: substance (RxNorm for drugs, SNODENT for dental materials, SNOMED-CT for environmental), severity (mild / moderate / severe / anaphylactic), onset date, last-verified date, source (patient-reported / verified-by-clinician / pharmacy-import). **Sensitivity: phi.**
source: DPIM v3 §1.8; RxNorm; SNODENT; SNOMED-CT
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined

### [1]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL hard-stop drug-prescribing (M1.9 / M4 future-stub e-prescribe path) when patient has an active allergy to the prescribed drug or drug class. Override SHALL require provider explicit override with reason + documentation; override SHALL emit `DPIM_ComplianceEvent` to governance topic.
source: RxNorm interaction; HIPAA principle of safety
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [2]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL hard-stop operatory-material setup when patient has an active material allergy (latex → no latex gloves; nickel → no nickel-bearing alloys). Operatory check-in SHALL display the allergy alert per M1.10 hard-stop rule.
source: SNODENT material allergy; HTML m1 §1.8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M1.10 + M9 (operatory equipment)

### [3]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-004
requirement: Allergy records SHALL track verification status: `unverified` / `patient_reported` / `clinician_verified`. Unverified allergies still hard-stop but with "Patient-reported, unverified — please confirm" displayed.
source: Clinical safety best practice
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [4]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.allergy.added`, `.updated`, `.resolved` events. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.** Subscribers (M4 prescribe, M9 operatory setup) receive allergen identifier + severity, not patient identity in cleartext.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap

### [5]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface allergy alerts on chart-open per M1.10 hard-stop rule. Alerts SHALL be unmissable (banner-level, color-coded by severity).
source: Cross-ref M1.10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [6]
reqId: M1-ALG-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support latex allergy operatory routing — when patient with severe latex allergy is checked in, OSCAR SHALL alert front-desk to route to latex-free operatory. **Cross-module with M2.4 Operatory** (operatories tagged `latex_free=true`) and M3.15 check-in flow.
source: Common dental safety pattern
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module

## 1.9
id: 1.9
name: Medications
purpose: Active medication list (RxNorm), reconciliation each encounter, drug-drug interaction screening, anticoagulant/bisphosphonate/immunosuppressant flags, source attribution.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Medication` (D1 §P.X — PatientAggregate child), `DPIM_Prescription` (M4 — outbound prescribing), `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` (M1.10).
externalIntegrations: RxNorm, Surescripts (e-prescribing — Phase 2/3), drug-drug interaction service (e.g., First Databank, Medi-Span — vendor TBD), PDMP (state controlled-substance registry — Phase 2).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain active-medication list per patient with: drug (RxNorm), dose, frequency, route, prescribing provider, prescribing date, source (patient-reported / pharmacy-imported / Surescripts-fetched / e-prescribed-by-OSCAR). **Sensitivity: phi.**
source: DPIM v3 §1.9; RxNorm
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [1]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL perform medication reconciliation at every encounter — patient confirms / updates / removes medications via portal pre-visit (M3.12) or staff-assisted at check-in. Reconciliation SHALL emit `dpim.person.medication.reconciled` event.
source: Joint Commission medication-reconciliation standard; cross-module with M3.12
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [2]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL flag clinically-significant medication classes that affect dental care: anticoagulants (warfarin, DOACs, antiplatelet — bleeding-risk during procedures), bisphosphonates (osteonecrosis-of-jaw risk for extractions), immunosuppressants (infection-risk + premedication), insulin (sedation timing), beta-blockers (epinephrine considerations). Flags SHALL surface as `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` (M1.10).
source: Common dental safety patterns
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [3]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL perform drug-drug interaction screening before any e-prescribed medication (M4 future-stub e-prescribe path). Screening SHALL use a clinical decision-support service (vendor TBD). Severity SHALL be displayed (contraindicated / major / moderate / minor). Contraindicated interactions SHALL hard-stop prescribe.
source: Common e-prescribing requirement
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: DDI service vendor TBD; M4 future-stub limits Phase 1

### [4]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL perform drug-allergy screening at prescribe-time (M1.8 cross-ref). Hard-stop applies.
source: Cross-ref M1.8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [5]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.medication.added`, `.updated`, `.removed`, `.reconciled` events. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.**
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [6]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate Surescripts to fetch medication history (Phase 2) — patient gives consent at intake; OSCAR fetches from Surescripts; medications imported with source=`surescripts_fetched`. **Surescripts requires DEA-registered identity for fetch.**
source: Surescripts API; DEA EPCS
phase: P2
status: Not Started

### [7]
reqId: M1-MEDS-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate state PDMP (Prescription Drug Monitoring Program) for controlled-substance prescribe queries (Phase 2). Provider-initiated query SHALL be logged.

phase: P2

## 1.10
id: 1.10
name: Patient Clinical Alerts
purpose: Banner alerts surfaced clinic-wide on chart open: premedication required, latex allergy, pacemaker, special needs. **Hard-stop vs advisory distinction is critical.** Premedication-required is hard-stop on procedures requiring prophylaxis; latex allergy is hard-stop on operatory setup; "patient is anxious" is advisory.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` (D1 §P.X — PatientAggregate child), `DPIM_AuditEntry` (provider acknowledgment chain).
externalIntegrations: None directly (alert sources are internal: M1.7, M1.8, M1.9, plus staff-set flags).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ClinicalAlert` records per patient with: alert text, alert source (medical_history / allergy / medication / staff_flag / system_derived), severity (advisory / warning / hard_stop), expiration date (optional), provider acknowledgment audit chain. **Sensitivity: phi.**
source: DPIM v3 §1.10; HTML m1 §1.10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [1]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface clinic-wide banner alerts on chart open — banner-level UI (not subtle). **Hard-stop alerts SHALL block encounter writes** until acknowledged by provider with reason. **Advisory alerts SHALL display but not block.**
source: Common clinical safety pattern; HTML m1 §1.10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [2]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL distinguish hard-stop categories: (a) **prescribing** — drug allergy + drug-drug interaction; (b) **operatory** — latex/nickel allergy; (c) **procedural** — premedication-required; (d) **anesthesia** — sedation contraindications (e.g., severe sleep apnea + IV sedation). Each hard-stop category SHALL be enforced by the relevant downstream module (M4 prescribe, M2.4 operatory, M4 anesthesia).
source: Common dental safety patterns
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module enforcement points

### [3]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-004
requirement: Provider acknowledgment of any clinical alert SHALL be auditable: who acknowledged, when, with what reason. Acknowledgment SHALL be per-encounter (don't silence a hard-stop forever). Override of hard-stop SHALL emit `DPIM_ComplianceEvent` to governance topic.
source: HIPAA §164.312(b); patient safety
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [4]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support staff-set advisory flags: "anxiety / sedation needed", "transportation assistance", "language assistance", "behavioral notes (pediatric)". Staff-set flags SHALL be auditable and removable with audit trail.
source: HTML m1 §1.10 + common practice
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [5]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support alert expiration / re-validation — for example, premedication-required alerts based on artificial-joint-within-2-years SHALL auto-expire after 2 years and prompt clinical re-evaluation.
source: ADA/AHA premedication guidelines (time-bound)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [6]
reqId: M1-ALERT-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.clinical_alert.flagged`, `.acknowledged`, `.expired`, `.resolved` events. Hard-stop overrides SHALL emit additional `dpim.person.clinical_alert.override_executed` event with override reason + provider identity. **All events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.**
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap

## 1.11
id: 1.11
name: Patient Status & Lifecycle
purpose: Lead → New → Active → Inactive → Archived → Deceased lifecycle with reasons. Reactivation campaign eligibility flag, status-change audit. **The "Inactive" status (>18 months no visit) is the trigger for M7 Reactivation Campaigns.**
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Patient.patientStatus` (property), `DPIM_PatientStatusChange` (governance domain — D1 §P.X), `DPIM_Lead` (v3 new — pre-patient leads), `DPIM_ReactivationCampaign` (M7 §8).
externalIntegrations: M7 Communications (reactivation campaign trigger), M11 Practice Ops & Analytics (lifecycle distribution KPIs).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Patient.patientStatus` with values: `lead` (pre-patient — see M1-LIFE-EXT-002), `new` (registered, not yet seen), `active` (seen within configurable window), `inactive` (>18 months no visit by default), `archived` (manual archive), `deceased` (terminal).
source: DPIM D1 §P.2 prop 2.2
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [1]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Lead` records (v3 new) for pre-patient leads — inbound calls captured by AI receptionist (M3.10 SCH-DS-005), marketing-attributed contacts, website inquiries. Leads SHALL be promotable to Patient via standard registration flow. **Lead → Patient promotion SHALL emit `dpim.person.lead.promoted_to_patient`.**
source: v3 LOCKED §1.10 (Lead class — property-doc-pending)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM class defined; lead-promotion flow not built

### [2]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL transition Active → Inactive automatically when patient's `lastVisitDate` exceeds the configurable inactive-window (default 18 months). Practice-tier customizable. **Transition SHALL emit `dpim.person.patient.status_changed` with old/new status.**
source: Common dental practice pattern; cross-module with M7
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [3]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-004
requirement: Inactive patients SHALL be flagged eligible for M7 Reactivation Campaigns. **Reactivation eligibility SHALL be tagged on the patient and consumable by M7 segment-builder** (DPIM v3 §8.7 PatientSegment).
source: Cross-module with M7 §8.13 ReactivationCampaign
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M7

### [4]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support archive operation — staff manually archives patient (e.g., moved away, transferred to another practice). Archive SHALL preserve all records for retention period; future scheduling SHALL be blocked; archive SHALL be reversible.
source: HIPAA retention requirements
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [5]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-006
requirement: Deceased status SHALL be set via M1-DEM-EXT-012 (deceasedDate field). Deceased patients SHALL block all outbound communications (no marketing / no recall) within hours of status change. M7 SHALL honor deceased status across all communication paths.
source: HIPAA §164.502(f); cross-module with M7
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M7 suppression

### [6]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PatientStatusChange` records — one per status transition with old status, new status, reason, actor (staff or system). Records SHALL be hash-chained in WORM audit.
source: DPIM v3 §3 (PatientStatusChange under Governance)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [7]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `dpim.person.patient.status_changed` event on every transition. **Event SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate.** Subscribers (M7 reactivation, M11 analytics, PS-4 Sentinel Behavioral) receive status code + patient-token.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` gap

### [8]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track lifecycle transitions for analytics (M11) — patient acquisition rate, activation rate (lead → active), retention rate, attrition rate (active → inactive), reactivation rate (inactive → active). M11 SHALL roll up to fleet view for DSO.
source: Cross-module with M11 + M2 DSO
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [9]
reqId: M1-LIFE-EXT-010
requirement: Status change SHALL go through `ctx.governed_write` with action_class `person.write`, action_type `patient.status_change`. Verdict matrix SHALL be enforced — `archived` and `deceased` transitions require `office_manager` or higher persona.
source: DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Verdict matrix entry needed

## m3
moduleId: m3

## subModules

## 3.1
id: 3.1
name: Appointment
purpose: Patient + provider + operatory + procedure(s) + duration. Multi-procedure / multi-provider appointments. Status lifecycle: scheduled → confirmed → checked_in → in_chair → checked_out → completed (or no_show / cancelled / rescheduled). The procedure-first constraint chain (procedure drives operatory drives provider drives time slot) is enforced at booking and through every lifecycle change.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Appointment`, `DPIM_AppointmentProcedure`, `DPIM_PreVisitChecklist`, `DPIM_BookingHold` (v3 new), `DPIM_OperatoryFlowEvent` (v3 new — event CI), `DPIM_AppointmentSeries` (v3 new — for ortho recurring).
externalIntegrations: NexHealth Synchronizer (PMS bidirectional sync), Azure Service Bus (event publishing).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-APT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL require procedure type selection as the first step in appointment creation. The selected procedure SHALL determine valid operatories, qualified providers, and auto-calculated duration. SHALL enforce the constraint chain procedure→operatory→provider→time slot.
source: Req Spec §3 SCH-APT-001 (Critical); Excel Indiv. Practice rows 4–5
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: `modules/scheduling/app/writer.py` (main); migration 022_module_scheduling_tables.sql; `modules/scheduling/manifest.yaml`

### [1]
reqId: M3-APT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL query provider and operatory availability via NexHealth Synchronizer with <15-second response time. Results SHALL be filtered by procedure type, patient preferences, and operatory qualifications. Search SHALL return ranked slots with date/time, provider, operatory, AI match score.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-002 (Critical); Excel row 4
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: NexHealth integration on `feat/hexhealth` (Allan Crawford); not on main

### [2]
reqId: M3-APT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support drag-and-drop rescheduling, preserving all metadata: procedure codes, notes, duration, sooner-if-possible flag, confirmation status, Quick Fill associations. SHALL validate resource availability before confirming. SHALL sync to PMS within 15 seconds via NexHealth. SHALL audit original/new times, reason, user.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-003 (High); Excel row 7
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Drag-drop UI surface not yet built; cross-module with i2 Workstation Web

### [3]
reqId: M3-APT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL present structured cancellation prompt with 3 options: Cancel only, Send to Quick Fill (DEFAULT), Add to Waitlist. Cancellation reason SHALL be structured data. SHALL trigger gap detection, update production tracking, optionally initiate Quick Fill outreach within 60 seconds.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-004 (Critical); Excel rows 7, 8
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: Cancellation primitive in writer.py (main); structured reason capture + Quick Fill auto-trigger absent

### [4]
reqId: M3-APT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL manage complete appointment status lifecycle: Scheduled, Confirmed, Checked In, In Progress, Complete, Cancelled, No-Show, Rescheduled. Each transition SHALL trigger downstream events: Confirmed→pre-visit task check; Checked In→eligibility re-verification (M6 RCM-ELIG-008); Complete→claim generation + next-appt prompt; No-Show→automated follow-up + Quick Fill activation.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-005 (Critical); CAIS Module Design §4.4 (events list)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: manifest.yaml declares scheduling.write action class; lifecycle transitions partial. Per CAIS M-01 §4.4, 10 events published — only `appointment.book/cancel/confirm` mapped in AG `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` per Phase 0 status doc (no `appointment.checked_in`, `completed`, `no_show`)

### [5]
reqId: M3-APT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support structured patient check-in via checklist: eligibility verified, demographics confirmed, forms completed, balance collected or payment plan confirmed. SHALL auto-trigger eligibility re-verification if original check >24 hours old or treatment value >$500 (bridges to M6 RCM-ELIG-008). Patient SHALL appear in provider queue upon completion.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-006 (Critical); cross-module with M6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No check-in flow code; depends on M6 eligibility re-verification (Code Present, Untested in modules/rcm)

### [6]
reqId: M3-APT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL display visit summary at checkout (procedures completed, charges, payment due), collect payment via M6 Payment Processing, prompt next-appointment scheduling based on completed procedures. AI SHALL recommend follow-up interval. Family scheduling prompt SHALL appear if family members are due.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-007 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M6.8 Payment Processing (Not Started) and M1 Family

### [7]
reqId: M3-APT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL capture patient scheduling preferences as structured data: preferred days, preferred times (AM/PM/specific hours), preferred provider (required vs preferred), sooner-if-possible flag, communication method. Preferences SHALL feed gap-fill AI matching and recall outreach.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-008 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM `DPIM_Patient` patient-preference fields needed; cross-module with M1

### [8]
reqId: M3-APT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain configurable procedure-driven appointment type taxonomy: New Patient Exam, Hygiene variants, Restorative, Emergency, custom. Each type maps to required operatory type, qualified provider types, default duration, production category, CDT codes. Practices SHALL configure 10–20 procedure variants per provider type.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-009 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Migration 006/007 has CDT + fee schedule; appointment-type → CDT mapping in migration 022

### [9]
reqId: M3-APT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce operatory constraints: hygiene operatories accept only hygienist procedures; doctor operatories accept only dentist procedures. Each operatory SHALL track equipment, provider assignments, availability windows. Resource conflicts SHALL be detected real-time during booking. SHALL support shared operatories with time-based provider rotation.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-010 (Critical); Excel row 14 (Operatory Constraint Engine)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: DPIM `DPIM_Operatory` (M2 §2.4) defines operatory type; constraint enforcement in writer.py partial

### [10]
reqId: M3-APT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL provide unified schedule visibility across multiple practice locations. Sarah SHALL see availability across all locations a patient is willing to visit. SHALL prevent cross-location double-booking.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-011 (High); Excel "DSO" row 5
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-tenant RLS exists; cross-location patient sharing not built

### [11]
reqId: M3-APT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain immutable audit trail for every appointment action: creation, modification, rescheduling, cancellation, status change, confirmation. Each entry SHALL record action type, timestamp, user, previous value, new value, reason. SHALL use chained cryptographic checksums consistent with RCM M6.14 audit pattern. **SHALL feed fraud detection patterns (M11 IntegrityAlerts surfacing → M12 12.7 User Behavior Analytics investigation).**
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-012 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: WORM hash chain on main; appointment-event coverage requires emitter wiring per event type

### [12]
reqId: M3-APT-013
requirement: OSCAR AI SHALL rank available slots beyond chronological order using weighted criteria: patient preference match (35%), production goal alignment (25%), provider schedule balance (20%), operational efficiency (20%). Sarah SHALL see match score indicator (Best Match / Good Fit / Available).
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-013 (High)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: AI ranking algorithm not built; depends on PS-2 Orchestrator

### [13]
reqId: M3-APT-014
requirement: OSCAR SHALL continuously observe all scheduling activity across the practice and synthesize proactive intelligence pushed to each persona: Sarah/gap-fill, Linda/production, Dr. Michael/exec briefings, Rachel/fleet analytics. Intelligence SHALL update real-time.
source: Req Spec SCH-APT-014 (High)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with PS-2 Orchestrator + PS-3 Thread Intelligence

## openQuestions

### [0]
id: OQ-3.1.1
text: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` map at `services/action-gateway/app/main.py:59-68` covers `appointment.book/cancel/confirm` only. Per CAIS M-01 §4.4, M3 publishes 10 events including `appointment.checked_in`, `appointment.completed`, `appointment.no_show`. **The seven missing event mappings are critical-path P1 work.** Surface this to the same Phase 0 critical-path commitment that covered the missing `pms.*` action types.

### [1]
id: OQ-3.1.2
text: Drag-and-drop rescheduling (M3-APT-003) is UI-layer (i2 Workstation Web). Confirm: M3 owns the *constraint validation*; i2 owns the *UI*. The split is similar to RCM 6.16 persona-view ownership. ---

## 3.2
id: 3.2
name: Appointment Type
purpose: Type catalog (NPE, prophy, perio maint, crown seat, etc.) with default duration per provider, default operatory eligibility, color coding, procedures auto-attached per type. The taxonomy underlies 3.1's procedure-first constraint chain.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_AppointmentType`, `DPIM_Procedure` (CDT linkage), `DPIM_Operatory`, `DPIM_Practitioner`.
externalIntegrations: ADA CDT code library (annual updates).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-APT-009-A
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain the appointment-type taxonomy with per-type required-operatory + qualified-provider + default-duration + production-category + CDT-code mapping. Practices SHALL configure 10–20 procedure variants per provider type.
source: Req Spec §3 SCH-APT-009 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Migration 022_module_scheduling_tables.sql; CDT seed in sql/cdt/

### [1]
reqId: M3-TPL-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support two duration modes: procedure template defaults and provider-specific overrides per procedure type or per individual appointment. Duration history SHALL be tracked to identify template accuracy. AI SHALL recommend template adjustments when actual durations deviate.
source: Req Spec §4 SCH-TPL-005 (Medium)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: No duration-history tracker

### [2]
reqId: M3-2-EXT-01
requirement: Each appointment type SHALL have a configurable color for calendar rendering. Color choice SHALL be per-tenant.
source: HTML m3 §3.2 description; UI/UX spec
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: UI-layer concern; data model field exists

### [3]
reqId: M3-2-EXT-02
requirement: Procedures SHALL auto-attach to an appointment when the appointment-type is selected, populating the line items required for downstream RCM 6.3 claim generation at appointment.completed.
source: HTML m3 §3.2 + cross-module M6 RCM-CLM-001
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: Auto-attach logic in writer.py partial; full procedure population deferred to M4 Clinical (future-stub)

## 3.3
id: 3.3
name: Provider Schedule & Availability
purpose: Working hours, recurring weekly templates, per-day overrides, multi-location split schedules, lunch/break, CE/vacation/time-off. **Per HTML m3 §3.3 integration, license expiry from M10 HR Compliance Tracker auto-blocks scheduling availability** (RCM-CLM-016 pattern applied to scheduling).
dpimClasses: `DPIM_ProviderSchedule`, `DPIM_TimeBlock`, `DPIM_BlockoutPeriod`, `DPIM_ScheduleTemplate` (v3 new), `DPIM_Practitioner`, `DPIM_License` (v3 — for license-gate), `DPIM_DEARegistration` (v3), `DPIM_HospitalPrivilege` (v3 — for surgical-privilege gating where applicable).
externalIntegrations: M10 HR Compliance Tracker (license expiration feeds availability), iCal/Google/Outlook calendar feeds (provider personal calendars).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-TPL-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support configurable weekly schedule templates per provider with time blocks: patient hours, admin time, lunch, blocked time. Templates SHALL enforce minimum buffer between appointments (configurable, default 5 min). Linda SHALL create/clone/modify. Provider-specific production goals SHALL be embedded in templates.
source: Req Spec §4 SCH-TPL-001 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Migration 022 has provider_schedule + template tables

### [1]
reqId: M3-TPL-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce scheduled breaks, lunch, and inter-appointment buffers within templates. Buffers SHALL be configurable by appointment type (longer buffers after complex procedures). SHALL alert when bookings attempt to override protected blocks. Emergency override SHALL require Linda approval.
source: Req Spec SCH-TPL-002 (High)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: Buffer enforcement in writer.py partial; emergency-override workflow not built

### [2]
reqId: M3-TPL-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support Perfect Day scheduling templates defining optimal procedure mix per provider per day. Templates SHALL specify procedure type distribution targets, production value targets by time block, appointment type sequencing. SHALL alert when booking deviates.
source: Req Spec SCH-TPL-003 (High)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Perfect Day engine not built; depends on PS-2 Orchestrator

### [3]
reqId: M3-TPL-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support daily and monthly production goals per provider and per practice, tracking scheduled vs. goals real-time throughout the day. Goals SHALL support different targets per day-of-week. Monthly goals SHALL auto-calculate from daily targets adjusted for working days.
source: Req Spec SCH-TPL-004 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Production-goal store not in migration 022; cross-module with M11

### [4]
reqId: M3-TPL-006
requirement: OSCAR AI SHALL analyze historical scheduling patterns and production outcomes to recommend template improvements: procedures consistently over/under-allocated, suboptimal sequencing, provider bottleneck patterns, underutilized time blocks. Recommendations SHALL include projected production impact.
source: Req Spec SCH-TPL-006 (Medium)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: No analytics engine

### [5]
reqId: M3-TPL-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain a matrix mapping providers to qualified procedure types with competency levels. Matrix SHALL support credentialing requirements, scope-of-practice rules, practice-specific restrictions. New procedure types SHALL require explicit qualification assignment before booking is allowed. Changes SHALL be audited with effective dates.
source: Req Spec SCH-TPL-007 (Critical); cross-module with M10 RCM-CLM-016 credentialing
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Provider-procedure qualification matrix absent; depends on `DPIM_License` (v3)

### [6]
reqId: M3-TPL-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support appointments requiring multiple providers (hygiene exam with doctor check). System SHALL coordinate provider availability to ensure both are available during required overlap window. Doctor-check time blocks SHALL be configurable per hygienist schedule. SHALL alert when coordination conflicts arise.
source: Req Spec SCH-TPL-009 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-provider coordination logic absent

### [7]
reqId: M3-3-EXT-01
requirement: License expiration via M10 HR Compliance Tracker (M10.4) SHALL auto-block availability for the affected provider. Per HTML m3 §3.3 integration callout.
source: HTML m3 §3.3; cross-module with M10 RCM-CLM-016 pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `DPIM_License` (v3) defined; cross-module gate not implemented

### [8]
reqId: M3-3-EXT-02
requirement: Multi-location providers SHALL have per-location split schedules. Cross-location double-booking SHALL be prevented.
source: HTML m3 §3.3 + Excel "DSO" row 5
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: RLS supports multi-tenant; cross-location split-schedule logic absent

### [9]
reqId: M3-3-EXT-03
requirement: Provider personal calendar feeds (iCal / Google / Outlook) SHALL be supported as read-only conflict sources. Personal calendar conflicts SHALL surface as advisories, not hard blocks.
source: HTML m3 §3.3 external integrations
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: No calendar-feed integration

## 3.4
id: 3.4
name: Time Blocks (Block Scheduling)
purpose: Reserved time blocks per appointment type (e.g., NPE only between 9–11am). AI-driven block release rules, provider-specific templates. **Distinct from 3.3 Provider Schedule (which defines availability) and 3.13 Schedule Optimization (AI) (which dynamically optimizes).** Block scheduling is the *static* reservation policy; AI optimization can release blocks dynamically.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_TimeBlock`, `DPIM_AppointmentType`, `DPIM_ProviderSchedule`, `DPIM_ScheduleTemplate` (v3).
externalIntegrations: PS-2 Orchestrator (AI block-release).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-4-EXT-01
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support time blocks reserved per appointment type with provider-specific templates (e.g., new-patient blocks between 9–11am for Provider X). Block templates SHALL be per-provider, per-day-of-week.
source: Req Spec §4 SCH-TPL-001 / SCH-TPL-003 (partial); HTML m3 §3.4
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Time-block table in migration 022

### [1]
reqId: M3-4-EXT-02
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support AI-driven block release rules: blocks SHALL auto-release for general booking when configurable lead-time threshold is reached without an appointment-type-matching booking (e.g., release NPE block 24h before if not booked).
source: HTML m3 §3.4 description; CAIS M-01 §4.6 (WATCH-003 trigger logic)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: AI release logic absent; depends on PS-2 Orchestrator + WATCH-003

### [2]
reqId: M3-4-EXT-03
requirement: Block-release decisions SHALL be auditable with reason (manual override / auto-release / time-threshold) and SHALL emit `gap.detected` event when a block is released to general booking.

phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Event taxonomy defined; emitter not built

### [3]
reqId: M3-4-EXT-04
requirement: Block templates SHALL support holiday / closure overlays from 3.5 Blockout Periods. Blockouts SHALL trump block-template rules.
source: HTML m3 §3.4 + §3.5
phase: P1
status: Not Started

### [4]
reqId: M3-4-EXT-05
requirement: Multi-tenant block-template policies SHALL be per-practice with optional DSO-brand-level inheritance.
source: Excel "DSO" row 7 (Standardized Workflow Templates Across Locations)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: DSO-policy layer absent across all M3
