## 3.8
id: 3.8
name: Recall Schedule
purpose: Recall types (prophy 6-mo, perio maint 3-mo, OE annual, BWX, FMX) with due dates per patient, pre-block recall slots, conversion analytics. **Phase 1 target: 80% recall rate.** Recall is the *cadence* (when patients are due); 3.11 Confirmations & Reminders is the *operational delivery*. Don't conflate.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_RecallSchedule`, `DPIM_Patient` (recallType field), `DPIM_Coverage` (insurance-benefits-expiring integration), `DPIM_Notification` (outreach delivery via M7).
externalIntegrations: M7 Communications (PS-5 Delivery for outreach), M6 RCM-ELIG-016 (Expiring Benefits Intelligence).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-RCL-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL assign recall type at visit completion based on clinical outcomes: Healthy Adult (6-mo prophy + exam), Periodontal Maintenance (3–4 mo perio + exam), Child/Pediatric (6-mo child prophy + fluoride + exam), High Risk (3–4 mo prophy + exam + monitoring), Custom intervals configurable per practice. Recall type SHALL drive due date calculation, procedure codes, outreach cadence.
source: Req Spec §7 SCH-RCL-001 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M4 Clinical (future-stub) for clinical-outcome assignment

### [1]
reqId: M3-RCL-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL continuously monitor recall lists and categorize patients: Coming Due (within 30/60/90 days configurable), Currently Due (within recall window), Overdue (past recall window). Each category SHALL trigger different outreach intensity. SHALL work three months forward and three months back. Lists SHALL update daily.
source: Req Spec SCH-RCL-002 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM `DPIM_RecallSchedule` defined; daily monitoring service absent

### [2]
reqId: M3-RCL-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL send automated recall reminders via patient-preferred channel. Default sequence: 90 days before (email + booking link), 60 days (SMS + 1-click booking), 30 days (SMS), at due date (urgent SMS), 30 days overdue (escalation SMS). Sequence timing and content SHALL be configurable per practice. Each touchpoint SHALL include direct booking link.
source: Req Spec SCH-RCL-003 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M7 + 3.11 + 3.10 (1-click booking)

### [3]
reqId: M3-RCL-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL escalate to reactivation campaign for patients overdue 30+ days: automated outreach with personalized messaging, then manual call tasks for Sarah. Contact attempts SHALL be tracked. After configurable max attempts (default 5 over 90 days), patients SHALL be flagged for review as potentially inactive.
source: Req Spec SCH-RCL-004 (High); cross-module with M7 ReactivationCampaign
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M7

### [4]
reqId: M3-RCL-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track the percentage of patients who leave with their next hygiene appointment scheduled, per hygienist. Target: 85–90% reappointment rate. SHALL calculate metric real-time and surface to Linda. Top-performing hygienists SHALL be identified as benchmarks.
source: Req Spec SCH-RCL-005 (High)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M11 KPIs

### [5]
reqId: M3-RCL-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL automatically cross-reference the recall due list when a hygiene gap opens, finding patients whose recall window overlaps with the gap date. These patients SHALL be prioritized for outreach. **Recall-to-Gap Bridge.**
source: Req Spec SCH-RCL-006 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with 3.13 (Gap-Fill AI)

### [6]
reqId: M3-RCL-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL provide recall performance metrics: recall volume by type and status, reactivation rate (target >40%), average time from due date to scheduled appointment, recall completion rate, unscheduled patient aging. Dashboard SHALL show trends and provider comparisons. Rachel SHALL see fleet-wide recall health.

status: Not Started

### [7]

### [8]

requirement: OSCAR AI SHALL analyze patient booking behavior to optimize recall outreach timing. If a patient historically books within 24 hours of receiving a text, SHALL time outreach for optimal response.

status: Not Started

## 3.9
id: 3.9
name: Wait List
purpose: Patient priority by clinical urgency / revenue value, auto-fill on cancellation, patient self-add via portal, wait-list aging. The Quick Fill queue (cancellation-sourced) and the wait list (rescheduling-sourced) are operationally distinct surfaces but share the matching-engine logic with 3.13.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_WaitList`, `DPIM_Patient`, `DPIM_AppointmentType`, `DPIM_Notification`.
externalIntegrations: M7 Communications (outreach), i4 Patient Portal (self-add), NexHealth (continuing care / ASAP list sync).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-QF-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL preserve all metadata when cancelled appointments are routed to Quick Fill: original date/time, duration, procedure codes, appointment type, patient contact, availability preferences, sooner-if-possible flag, notes, cancellation reason. Metadata SHALL enable one-click rebooking.
source: Req Spec §6 SCH-QF-001 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: Cancellation primitive in writer.py partial; metadata-preservation logic incomplete

### [1]
reqId: M3-QF-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL run automated consistency audits on the Quick Fill queue: stale items (>30 days) flagged for review, duplicate entries merged, missing data flagged for completion, auto-remove when patient scheduled elsewhere, max attempts (3+ failed contacts) moves to Cold list.
source: Req Spec SCH-QF-002 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No audit job

### [2]
reqId: M3-QF-003
requirement: OSCAR AI SHALL prioritize Quick Fill entries based on production value of needed procedure, patient response likelihood, time sensitivity (insurance benefits expiring, treatment urgency), availability match to current gaps. Priority scores SHALL update dynamically.
source: Req Spec SCH-QF-003 (High)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: No prioritization AI

### [3]
reqId: M3-QF-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain a separate waitlist for patients who want to reschedule but no immediate slot is available. Waitlist SHALL capture: desired appointment type, availability windows, provider preference, urgency level, date added. When matching slots become available, SHALL notify waitlisted patients automatically. **First-to-respond booking SHALL prevent double-booking.**
source: Req Spec SCH-QF-004 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM `DPIM_WaitList` defined; first-respond locking mechanism not built

### [4]
reqId: M3-QF-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL continuously match Quick Fill and waitlist entries against current schedule openings considering procedure type and duration compatibility, operatory type, provider qualifications, patient availability preferences, production goal alignment. Matches SHALL surface to Sarah as one-click booking opportunities. In Auto mode, SHALL send booking offers without manual intervention.
source: Req Spec SCH-QF-005 (Critical)

## 3.10
id: 3.10
name: Online Booking
purpose: Patient-facing booking with real-time availability, eligibility verification at booking, new-patient form completion before slot lock. **73% of appointments are booked after hours per Spec §11 narrative.** Includes Google Business Profile booking, AI receptionist (voice + SMS/chat), and self-service rescheduling/cancellation.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Appointment` (booking creates), `DPIM_BookingHold` (v3 new — pre-form-completion), `DPIM_Coverage` / `DPIM_Eligibility` (real-time check), `DPIM_FormInstance` (intake completion), `DPIM_Lead` (v3 new — pre-patient leads from AI receptionist).
externalIntegrations: NexHealth (real-time availability), Google Business Profile API (Book Now button), Azure AI (voice receptionist + chat), Twilio + Azure Communication Services (SMS booking), HIPAA-compliant video conferencing (telehealth scheduling).

Portal SHALL show real-time availability via NexHealth (updates within 15 seconds). Booking SHALL trigger automatic confirmation, intake form delivery, eligibility verification. Portal SHALL be mobile-optimized, HIPAA-compliant, practice-branded.

## 3.11
id: 3.11
name: Confirmations & Reminders
purpose: Multi-channel SMS / email / voice / push with cadence configuration, two-way confirm-cancel-reschedule via SMS reply, channel fall-through. **TCPA-enforced send windows per `DPIM_TCPAConsent` with `purposeCategory=appointment_reminder`.** This is the *operational delivery* layer; the *cadence rules* live in 3.8 Recall (for recall) and configured per-practice (for confirmations).
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Notification`, `DPIM_TCPAConsent` (v3 — required for SMS), `DPIM_MessageTemplate`, `DPIM_CommLog`, `DPIM_ConversationThread` (for two-way SMS), `DPIM_OptOut` / `DPIM_SuppressionList`.
externalIntegrations: PS-5 Delivery Service, Twilio (SMS + voice), SendGrid / Azure Communication Services (email + SMS), CAIS internal `outreach_sender_acs_direct` tool (Phase 1; replaced by MCP Communication Hub in Phase 2 per CAIS M-01 §4 seam note).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-CFM-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL manage multi-channel appointment reminder sequences configurable per practice. Default sequence: 7 days before (email + confirm/reschedule links), 2 days (SMS C/R/X reply), 1 day (SMS final), optional 2 hours before (same-day SMS). Timing, channel, content SHALL be configurable per appointment type. **SHALL respect patient communication preferences and opt-out status.**
source: Req Spec §8 SCH-CFM-001 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: Per CAIS M-01 §3.2 (`batch_confirmation_outreach` workflow) declared in manifest; runtime not exercised. WATCH-005 trigger logic specified.

### [1]
reqId: M3-CFM-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL process patient SMS responses real-time: C/CONFIRM/YES/Y auto-confirms and logs timestamp; R/RESCHEDULE sends reschedule link and creates front-desk task; X/CANCEL/NO cancels and triggers Quick Fill + gap detection. Unrecognized responses SHALL create manual follow-up tasks. **All responses SHALL update confirmation status within 30 seconds.**
source: Req Spec SCH-CFM-002 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Two-way SMS handler not built

### [2]
reqId: M3-CFM-003

phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Dashboard absent

## 3.12

externalIntegrations: M8 Documents (forms), M6 RCM (eligibility re-verify, balance), M1 Patient (medical history), telehealth screening platform (optional).

## 3.13
id: 3.13
name: Schedule Optimization (AI)
purpose: AI-driven production target tracking, hygiene fill, no-show prediction with overbooking suggestions, same-day add-on suggestions. **Phase 1 differentiator vs incumbent dental PMS.** Phase 1 target: 95% schedule utilization.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_AIRecommendation`, `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3), `DPIM_PracticeRiskScore` (v3 — Behavioral / Articulation dimensions), `DPIM_Forecast` (v3), `DPIM_BookingHold` (v3), `DPIM_AnomalyEvent` (v3 — for unusual scheduling patterns).
externalIntegrations: PS-2 Agent Orchestrator (8-step context assembly), PS-3 Thread Intelligence (4 watchers, 6 Practice Snapshot fields per CAIS M-01 §4.6, §4.7), PS-4 Sentinel (no-show prediction signal), PS-5 Delivery (PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM UI callbacks), Azure OpenAI (gpt-4o-mini for ranking, narrative briefings).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M3-GAP-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL continuously monitor all provider schedules for gaps: cancellations, no-shows, unfilled time blocks exceeding configurable minimum duration (default 30 min). Gaps SHALL be categorized by operatory type, time until gap, production value at risk, provider. **Gap detection SHALL trigger within 60 seconds of cancellation or schedule change.**
source: Req Spec §5 SCH-GAP-001 (Critical); CAIS M-01 §4.6 (WATCH-003)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: WATCH-003 declared in manifest; runtime not exercised

### [1]
reqId: M3-GAP-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL draw from seven intelligent sources to fill gaps: (1) Sooner If Possible list, (2) Recall Wizard patients due/overdue, (3) Quick Fill Queue cancelled appointments, (4) Treatment Plan Pending unscheduled treatment, (5) Prior Month Audit incomplete treatment, (6) Specialist Return Tracker, (7) Proactive Treatment Coordination matching incomplete treatment to upcoming visits.
source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-002 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-source candidate aggregation absent

### [2]
reqId: M3-GAP-003
requirement: OSCAR AI SHALL rank candidates from all seven sources using weighted algorithm: Production Match (35%), Availability Match (25%), No-Show Risk (20%), Clinical Need (20%). Rankings SHALL refresh as patient responses arrive. Each candidate SHALL display ranking score with reasoning.
source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-003 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with PS-2 Orchestrator

### [3]
reqId: M3-GAP-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce time-based production protection: same-day gaps accept any matching procedure type; 1–3 day gaps prefer high-production but accept medium if no match; 3+ day gaps only fill with procedures meeting or exceeding original production value. **Golden Rule: SHALL never break a high-production appointment to insert a low-production unless explicitly approved by Linda.**
source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-004 (Critical)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Production-protection rule engine absent

### [4]
reqId: M3-GAP-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply intelligent source selection based on gap type: hygiene gaps prioritize Recall Due/Overdue then Quick Fill then Sooner-If-Possible; doctor gaps prioritize Treatment Plan Pending then Sooner-If-Possible then Specialist Returns; same-day emergencies accept any. Source priority SHALL be configurable per practice.
source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-005 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: 

### [5]
reqId: M3-GAP-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL initiate outreach per practice configuration: Suggest Only (presents to Sarah), Auto-Text with Approval (SMS pending Linda approval), Full Auto (SMS with one-click booking links). Outreach messages SHALL include date/time, procedure description, estimated patient cost. Responses SHALL be tracked with timestamps.
source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-006 (High); CAIS M-01 §3.1 (quick_fill_campaign workflow)
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: quick_fill_campaign workflow declared with PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM verdict; runtime not exercised

### [6]
reqId: M3-GAP-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL display gap-fill intelligence as visual overlay on schedule calendar. Each gap SHALL show #1 recommended patient, priority score, reason, one-click actions (text/call/book/snooze/mark-unreachable). Next-best alternatives SHALL be accessible with single click. **Do-not-break guardrails SHALL be visually indicated.**
source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-007 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: UI surface

### [7]
reqId: M3-GAP-008

source: Req Spec SCH-GAP-008 (High)
phase: P1
status: Not Started

## openQuestions

### [0]
id: OQ-3.13.1
text: 3.13 is the single largest sub-module by requirement count (24 reqs across spec §5 §9 §12 + CAIS M-01 EXT). It also exercises the broadest platform surface (PS-2, PS-3, PS-4, PS-5). Recommend treating 3.13 as its own sprint sequence (likely 4–6 weeks of focused work) rather than parallel-running with the rest of M3.

### [1]
id: OQ-3.13.2
text: Per CAIS M-01 §1, Smart Scheduling is the **reference implementation** for the entire OSCAR module system. Patterns established here (workflow declarations, tool declarations, watcher registration, event surface, SLCO, PHI boundary) become canonical for every subsequent module. Implies 3.13 quality bar is higher than other sub-modules.

### [2]
id: OQ-3.13.3
text: No-Show ML model (M3-NS-001) and CSA model (M3-NS-002) overlap conceptually. Decide: single model with multiple outputs, or two distinct models? Spec is ambiguous. ---

## 3.14
id: 3.14
name: No-Show / Cancellation Mgmt
purpose: Tracking per patient, late-cancellation policy enforcement (fees), auto-blacklist after N no-shows (with override), late-cancel chair filling. **Anomaly signals (no-show pattern abuse, cancellation gaming) surface to M11 IntegrityAlerts → M12 12.7 User Behavior Analytics for investigation.** Operational deployment of no-show prediction (3.13) plus policy enforcement.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_NoShowPolicy` (v3 new), `DPIM_CancellationPolicy` (v3 new), `DPIM_Patient` (no-show count, blacklist flag), `DPIM_AnomalyEvent` (v3 — for pattern surfacing), `DPIM_LateCancellationFee` (v3 new — under DPIM_Service).
externalIntegrations: M6 RCM (late-cancellation fee posting), M7 Communications (no-show follow-up), M11 IntegrityAlerts (anomaly surfacing), M12 (investigation), PS-4 Sentinel (Behavioral dimension).

## 3.15
id: 3.15
name: Patient Check-In / Check-Out
purpose: Front-desk and patient self check-in (kiosk / mobile), check-out with charge posting, next-appointment scheduling, copay collection. Operatory wait timer / patient flow timer. The bridge between scheduling (3.1) and clinical encounter (M4 — future-stub) on the in-bound side, and to RCM 6.8 Payment + 6.5 ERA on the outbound side.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Appointment` (status transitions), `DPIM_Encounter` (created at check-in), `DPIM_OperatoryFlowEvent` (v3 — wait/in-chair/dismissed timestamps), `DPIM_Eligibility` (re-verify trigger), `DPIM_PatientPayment` (copay), `DPIM_FormInstance` (forms completion verification).
externalIntegrations: i7 Kiosk (self check-in), M6 RCM (eligibility re-verify, copay collection, claim trigger on completion), M4 Clinical (provider queue — future-stub).

## m6

## subModules

## 6.1
id: 6.1
name: Eligibility Verification
purpose: Real-time and batch insurance benefit checks via X12 270/271. OSCAR interprets benefits, predicts cost exposure, detects network anomalies, and pushes actionable intelligence to every persona before the patient arrives. This is OSCAR's first always-connected intelligence touchpoint.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Coverage`, `DPIM_BenefitInstance`, `DPIM_BenefitVerification`, `DPIM_Eligibility`, `DPIM_X12_270Request` (v3), `DPIM_X12_271Response` (v3), `DPIM_TreatmentEstimate` (v3 — for cost calc), `DPIM_PracticeKPI` (eligibility rate), `DPIM_InsuranceIntelligence` (payer scoring), `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3 — OCR + trend prediction).
externalIntegrations: Stedi (270/271 primary), Vyne ClearCoverage (failover + leased-network detection), Azure AI (OCR), Azure OpenAI (trend prediction), Azure Communication Services (SMS estimate to Ben).

## 6.2
id: 6.2
name: Pre-Determination
purpose: Eliminates financial surprises for patients and practices by managing the entire pre-determination lifecycle: auto-prompting on high-value procedures, tracking payer responses, notifying patients, locking estimates, and generating Good Faith Estimates per the No Surprises Act. **Pre-D status checking primarily routes through the Payer Portal sibling product** because direct payer APIs for pre-D status are weak/absent.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Predetermination`, `DPIM_PredeterminationLine`, `DPIM_TreatmentEstimate` (v3), `DPIM_GoodFaithEstimate` (v3 — NSA), `DPIM_FinancialAgreement` (v3 — patient signs), `DPIM_TreatmentPlan`, `DPIM_TCPAConsent` (v3 — for SMS), `DPIM_Notification` (SMS), `DPIM_Attachment` (radiographs/narratives).
externalIntegrations: Stedi/Vyne (837D pre-D submission), **Payer Portal sibling product** (REST API for status checks), Azure Communication Services (TCPA-compliant SMS), Azure OpenAI (smart pre-estimate AI), Azure Blob (attachment store).

## 6.3
id: 6.3
name: Claims Generation & Scrubbing
purpose: Automated 837D claim generation at checkout, validated against payer-specific rules, cross-referenced with clinical documentation. OSCAR is the only system that simultaneously sees clinical chart, account ledger, and claim data — enabling both 98% clean-claim rate and embezzlement detection. Phase 2 introduces Clinical Documentation Integrity audit and autonomous self-healing.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Claim`, `DPIM_ClaimLine`, `DPIM_ClaimAttachment`, `DPIM_X12_837Submission` (v3), `DPIM_FeeSchedule`, `DPIM_FeeItem`, `DPIM_Procedure`, `DPIM_Encounter`, `DPIM_DentalChart`, `DPIM_PatientAccount`, `DPIM_Practitioner` (NPI), `DPIM_NPI` (v3 — Type 1 / Type 2 lifecycle), `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3 — narrative + image AI), `DPIM_AIRecommendation` (v3 — credentialing pre-validation).
externalIntegrations: Stedi/Vyne (837D wire format), Azure OpenAI (CDI audit, self-healing, narrative generation), Azure Blob (imaging store for attachments), provider credentialing database, NPI registry (NUCC).

## 6.4
id: 6.4
name: Claims Submission
purpose: OSCAR submits validated claims through dual clearinghouse infrastructure (Stedi primary, Vyne failover) with intelligent routing and automatic failover. Monitors claims in transit, processes acknowledgments, and proactively manages the submission pipeline. Phase 2 adds Shadow Adjudicator (predicts payment within seconds of submission) and **Payer Portal AI Agents** for portal-first payers where EDI is incomplete.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_X12_837Submission` (v3), `DPIM_X12_277CA` (v3), `DPIM_X12_999Ack` (v3), `DPIM_Claim`, `DPIM_Clearinghouse`.
externalIntegrations: Stedi REST (270/271/837D/276/277/835), Vyne SFTP + ClearCoverage REST, **Payer Portal sibling product REST API** (40+ dental payer portals, browser automation), Azure Service Bus (queue management), Azure OpenAI (Shadow Adjudicator).

## 6.5
id: 6.5
name: ERA Processing
purpose: Converts payer remittances (X12 835) into posted revenue. OSCAR auto-retrieves 835 data, matches to claims, calculates postings, and presents Carlos with a review-and-approve workflow. Three-way matching (claim ↔ ERA ↔ deposit) feeds embezzlement detection. **PMS Write-Back during the bridge period writes payment postings back to legacy PMS via NexHealth/OpenDental APIs**, deprecated when practice fully transitions.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_X12_835Remittance` (v3), `DPIM_EOB`, `DPIM_InsurancePayment`, `DPIM_Adjustment` (v3 abstract), `DPIM_CourtesyWriteOff` / `DPIM_ContractualWriteOff` / `DPIM_BadDebtWriteOff` (v3), `DPIM_LedgerEntry`, `DPIM_PatientAccount`, `DPIM_PaymentBatch` (v3), `DPIM_BankDeposit` (v3), `DPIM_ClaimDenial` (v3 — when CARC/RARC indicates denial).
externalIntegrations: Stedi/Vyne (835 retrieval via webhooks or polling), Azure AI (paper EOB OCR), Fiska + bank reconciliation (deposit matching), **NexHealth API** (Dentrix/Eaglesoft write-back), **OpenDental native API** (write-back).

## 6.6
id: 6.6
name: Denial Management
purpose: OSCAR AI delivers the highest impact in denial management. OSCAR categorizes denials instantly, generates appeal letters in <60 seconds, predicts success probability, and learns from every outcome. **Critical principle: OSCAR NEVER auto-writes-off.** All write-offs require human approval. OSCAR can autonomously refile correctable denials in Phase 2.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_ClaimDenial` (v3), `DPIM_ClaimAppeal` (v3), `DPIM_AppealLetter` (v3), `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3 — appeal-letter drafting + success scoring), `DPIM_BadDebtWriteOff` (v3), `DPIM_AuditEntry`, `DPIM_GovernanceVerdict` (write-off two-tier approval).
externalIntegrations: Azure OpenAI (appeal letter generation, AI triage, success-probability scoring, denial-prevention learning), CARC/RARC code database, Workflow Engine (SLA enforcement).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: RCM-DEN-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL categorize denials using CARC/RARC into: Missing Info, Coding Error, Medical Necessity, Timely Filing, Duplicate, Non-Covered, Frequency.
source: Req Spec §8 RCM-DEN-001 (Critical); Feature Spec DM-01
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No denial-categorization service. CARC/RARC parsing exists in 6.5 era_parser but routing to denial domain absent

source: Req Spec RCM-DEN-002 (Critical); Feature Spec DM-02 (AI)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No AI triage code

## 6.8
id: 6.8
name: Payment Processing
purpose: Patient financial experience via Fiska — text-to-pay, card-on-file, payment plans, terminal integration. Auto-post to ledger via webhooks; settlement reconciliation feeds three-way matching for embezzlement detection. **Fiska revenue-share aligned with Canopy business model** — payment processing is a strategic asset, not a cost center.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_PaymentMethod` (v3), `DPIM_CardOnFile` (v3), `DPIM_PatientPayment`, `DPIM_PaymentPlan`, `DPIM_PaymentBatch` (v3), `DPIM_BankDeposit` (v3), `DPIM_LedgerEntry`, `DPIM_FinancialAgreement` (v3 — recurring authorization), `DPIM_TCPAConsent` (v3 — for SMS payment links), `DPIM_FinancingApplication` (v3 — Phase 2).
externalIntegrations: **Fiska** (card-present, card-not-present, text-to-pay, tokenization, payment plans, settlement, merchant onboarding), Azure Communication Services (SMS payment links), Apple Pay / Google Pay, ACH, HSA/FSA card support, Bank API (deposit reconciliation), CareCredit/Sunbit/Cherry/Proceed (Phase 2 financing pre-qual).

## 6.9
id: 6.9
name: Fee Schedules
purpose: Per-payer contracted fee tables, UCR (Usual, Customary, and Reasonable), custom schedules per location/provider. Auto-update on payer notification. The fee-schedule engine underlies eligibility cost calculation (6.1), claim fee validation (6.3), pre-treatment estimates (6.10), and underpayment detection (6.5).
dpimClasses: `DPIM_FeeSchedule`, `DPIM_FeeItem`, `DPIM_PayerContract` (v3 — adds contract-lifecycle context), `DPIM_InsurancePlan` (per-plan attachment), `DPIM_Procedure` (CDT code lookup).
externalIntegrations: Payer fee-schedule import (CSV, payer portal scrape via Payer Portal sibling), CDT code library (ADA annual updates), Imported per contract.

## 6.10

externalIntegrations: CareCredit / Sunbit / Cherry / Proceed (Phase 2 financing pre-qualification), Azure OpenAI (financing-propensity scoring), ESIGN/UETA-compliant signature provider.

## 6.11
id: 6.11
name: Patient Statements
purpose: Multi-channel patient billing-statement delivery (paper, email, SMS, portal) with aging-driven cadence and Text-to-Pay link injection. Statements are the consumer-facing artifact of the A/R + Payment Processing pipeline; this sub-module is the rendering and delivery layer.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Statement`, `DPIM_PatientAccount`, `DPIM_LedgerEntry`, `DPIM_Notification`, `DPIM_TCPAConsent` (v3 — for SMS), `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (v3 — channel preference).

## 6.12
id: 6.12
name: Adjustment Tracking
purpose: Per-insurance adjustment codes (DELTA-PPO, UHC, CIGNA, METLIFE), PPO analysis, write-off tracking. Adjustments are the bridge between billed amount and contracted amount; their categorization (courtesy vs contractual vs bad debt) matters for profitability analytics and embezzlement detection. This sub-module owns the lifecycle of `DPIM_Adjustment` subclasses. **Embezzlement / fraud signals derived from adjustment patterns route to M12 Embezzlement & Fraud (12.2 Adjustment Pattern Detection) for investigation; M11 IntegrityAlerts is the surfacing layer.**
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Adjustment` (v3 abstract), `DPIM_CourtesyWriteOff` (v3), `DPIM_ContractualWriteOff` (v3), `DPIM_BadDebtWriteOff` (v3), `DPIM_LedgerEntry`, `DPIM_FeeSchedule` (variance source), `DPIM_GovernanceVerdict` (approval), `DPIM_AuditEntry`, `DPIM_IntegrityAlert` (M11 surfacing — investigation workflow lives in M12 Embezzlement & Fraud, sub-module 12.2 Adjustment Pattern Detection).
externalIntegrations: None directly; consumes 6.5 ERA Processing output and 6.6 Denial Management write-off queue.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: RCM-ADJ-EXT-01

phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM v3 defines abstract `DPIM_Adjustment` and 3 subclasses; no implementation

status: Not Started
evidence: No payer-adjustment-code mapping table

M11 surfaces the alert; **M12 Embezzlement & Fraud (12.2 Adjustment Pattern Detection) owns the investigation workflow.**
source: DPIM v3 §11 IntegrityAlert; Feature Spec AC-08; v1.1 HTML M12 sub-module 12.2
phase: P1

## 6.14
id: 6.14
name: Compliance & Audit
purpose: OSCAR treats security and compliance as non-negotiable foundations. The immutable audit trail with chained cryptographic checksums serves triple duty: HIPAA compliance, PCI compliance, and embezzlement detection. OSCAR sees every financial action by every user across every practice.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_AuditEntry`, `DPIM_GovernanceVerdict`, `DPIM_PolicyRule`, `DPIM_PolicyDocument` (v3), `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement` (v3), `DPIM_BreachIncident` (v3), `DPIM_DataExportRequest` (v3 — Cures Act), `DPIM_SecurityRiskAssessment` (v3 — annual SRA), `DPIM_Permission`, `DPIM_PersonaDefinition`.
externalIntegrations: Azure Key Vault (PHI tokenization), Azure Entra ID (MFA, step-up auth), WORM Storage (immutable audit blob), OPA Policy Engine (`.rego` rules), Microsoft Defender (anomaly detection).

reqId: RCM-AUD-001

phase: P1

## 6.15
id: 6.15
name: Integration Architecture
purpose: OSCAR connects everything. Four integration planes (PMS, Clearinghouse, Payment, Communication) plus central event bus form the nervous system. **The Payer Portal sibling product is a peer integration** — OSCAR-RCM consumes its REST API for portal-first payers where direct payer APIs are weak or absent (pre-D status, deep eligibility, claim status fallback, EOB capture).
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Integration` (v3), `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` (v3), `DPIM_RateLimitPolicy` (v3), `DPIM_Clearinghouse`, `DPIM_MerchantConfig` (v3), `DPIM_Pharmacy` (v3 — Surescripts), `DPIM_BankAccount` (v3 — for ACH).
externalIntegrations: Stedi REST, Vyne SFTP + REST, **Payer Portal sibling product REST API**, Fiska, NexHealth (Dentrix/Eaglesoft bridge), OpenDental native API, Azure Service Bus, Azure Communication Services, Azure Blob, Azure Key Vault.

## 6.16
id: 6.16
name: Persona-Aware Intelligence
purpose: OSCAR is not a tool that personas use — OSCAR is an intelligence that works for every persona simultaneously. The same underlying data is surfaced differently to each role: Carlos sees claims detail, Sarah sees check-in readiness, Linda sees KPIs, Ben sees patient-friendly summaries, Dr. Michael sees business health, Rachel sees DSO fleet performance. **OSCAR pushes information proactively — personas do not have to search for it.**
dpimClasses: `DPIM_PersonaDefinition`, `DPIM_Permission`, `DPIM_ActionCard`, `DPIM_AIRecommendation`, `DPIM_DashboardLayout`, `DPIM_PracticeRiskScore` (v3 — drives persona alert prioritization).
externalIntegrations: Agent Orchestrator (PS-2 — context assembly per persona), Sentinel PRS (PS-4 — drives floor-condition alerts), Action Gateway (verdict routing per persona).

## 6.17
id: 6.17
name: Membership Plans
purpose: **In-house dental membership plans** — practice-owned subscription plans for uninsured patients. Distinct from `DPIM_InsurancePlan` (third-party insurance). Includes plan design, recurring auto-billing, member benefit tracking, renewal workflow. Common offerings: $200/year for 2 cleanings + 1 exam + 1 X-ray + 15% off other services.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_MembershipPlan`, `DPIM_PaymentPlan` (recurring), `DPIM_CardOnFile` (v3), `DPIM_Coverage` (membership-as-coverage applied to ledger), `DPIM_FeeSchedule` (membership-discount fee schedule), `DPIM_Notification` (renewal reminders).
externalIntegrations: Fiska (recurring billing), Azure Communication Services (renewal SMS/email).

## requirements

phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: DPIM `DPIM_MembershipPlan` exists; member flag noted in RCM-DAT-006

phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No recurring-billing engine

## m2
moduleId: m2

## subModules

## 2.1
id: 2.1
name: DSO / Group Practice Entity
purpose: DSO legal entity record — parent organization for multi-practice groups. Consolidates reporting, holds portfolio-level analytics, tracks operational maturity and valuation. Distinct from `DPIM_Practice` (M2.2) — a DSO **owns** practices but is not itself a practice. The relationship is many-to-one (`Practice.parentDSO` is optional; independent practices have no parent DSO).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_DSO` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 + DPIM D2 P.14, v2.0.0 — 9 properties) - `DPIM_PracticeValuation` (DPIM D2 P.15, v2.0.0 — 12 properties; entirely computed, no human edits) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (DPIM D2 P.13) — `name`, `identifier[]` (TIN/NPI/DUNS), `address`, `telecom[]`, `orgType`, `isActive`
externalIntegrations: None directly. Downstream consumers: M11 Strategy Zone (Portfolio Uplift recommendations driven by `maturityLevel`); M6 RCM (claim routing affected by `centralizedBilling`).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_DSO` records as the parent entity for multi-practice groups. Each DSO SHALL have legal name, optional doing-business-as name (`dbaName`), Federal TIN (`taxId`), and one or more child practices linked via `Practice.parentDSO`. The DSO entity SHALL be optional — independent practices SHALL operate with `parentDSO = null`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (DSO hierarchy); DPIM D2 P.14; HTML m2 §2.1; Module Catalog §3.2.1
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No `org.dso` table in `sql/migrations/003_org_and_user_tables.sql` — DSO not on main schema

### [1]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support multi-brand identity at the DSO level — a single DSO SHALL host multiple practice brands without requiring separate DSO records. Each child Practice SHALL maintain its own `dbaName` distinct from the DSO's `dbaName`, enabling brand-level reporting alongside corporate consolidation.
source: DPIM D2 P.14.1 + P.16.1; Module Catalog §3.2.1 ("Brand identity")
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Schema absent

### [2]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute and surface DSO-level reporting rollups across all child practices. System-computed properties SHALL include `practices` (Reference list), `practiceCount` (active count), and `totalActivePatients` (aggregate). All cross-practice rollup queries SHALL be subject to M2.11 cross-tenant audit and SHALL respect SQL Row-Level Security boundaries.
source: DPIM D2 P.14.3–P.14.5; HTML m2 §2.1 ("DSO-level reporting rollups")
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics pipeline absent

### [3]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track DSO operational maturity as a four-tier code (`L1` / `L2` / `L3` / `L4`) per `DPIM_DSO.maturityLevel`. Maturity level SHALL drive M11 Strategy Zone (Portfolio Uplift) recommendation tier — L1 receives foundational recommendations, L3+ receives advanced optimization. Mutation SHALL emit `dpim.org.dso.updated`.
source: DPIM D2 P.14.6 + Agentic Notes; DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M11 Strategy Zone consumer absent

### [4]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `consolidatedReporting` and `centralizedBilling` boolean flags on `DPIM_DSO`. When `centralizedBilling = true`, M6 RCM ERA/EFT posting SHALL aggregate to a DSO-level `DPIM_BankAccount` (v3-new); when false, payment posts to each practice's bank account. Flag change SHALL be propagated to M6 via event.
source: DPIM D2 P.14.7, P.14.8 + Agentic Notes; cross-module M6
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M6 RCM today assumes practice-level only

### [5]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support `sharedFeeSchedules` flag on `DPIM_DSO` — when true, practices inherit a DSO-level `DPIM_FeeSchedule`; when false, each practice maintains its own. Fee-schedule resolution at billing time SHALL traverse practice → DSO when the practice has no override.
source: DPIM D2 P.14.9; cross-module RCM-DAT-006 (fee schedules)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M6 today resolves per-practice; DSO inheritance not implemented

### [6]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support cross-practice patient sharing within a single DSO. Cross-link SHALL require explicit Office Mgr consent + WORM audit entry per M2.11. Cross-DSO patient linking (between different DSOs, or DSO ↔ independent practice) SHALL be prohibited and SHALL be rejected at the SDK validation gate.
source: HTML m2 §2.1 ("cross-practice patient sharing rules"); Module Catalog §3.2.1; M2.11 tenancy isolation rules
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M1 (patient identity) + M2.11

### [7]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PracticeValuation` entity per DSO — entirely computed, no human edits. Required properties: `valueScore` (0–100), `ebitda`, `ebitdaMargin`, `ebitdaMarginTrend` (`expanding` / `stable` / `contracting`), `sameStoreGrowth`, `revenueTrailing12`, `patientValueAvg`, `impliedMultiple`, `valuationDate`, `valuationNarrative` (AI-generated by M11 Strategy Zone Portfolio Uplift agent per OQ-2.1.3 resolution). API resource SHALL be read-only.
source: DPIM D2 P.15 (12 properties); HTML m2 §2.1 ("practice valuation tracking"); OQ-2.1.3 resolution
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics + AI narrative pipeline absent

### [8]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute `DPIM_PracticeValuation.ppoFfsMix` as `{ppoPercent, ffsPercent, medicaidPercent, membershipPercent}` from M6 RCM ledger and M2.6 InsurancePlan classifications. Threshold-driven "Improve Mix" action cards SHALL surface in M11 Strategy Zone when PPO percentage exceeds the per-practice configured upper bound.
source: DPIM D2 P.15.7 + Agentic Notes ("Improve Mix")
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M6 + M11

### [9]

source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; DPIM D2 P.14 audit annotations
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

### [10]
reqId: M2-DSO-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL restrict DSO record create/update to a dedicated `dso_admin` role. DSO records SHALL be invisible to practice-staff roles outside the DSO's child practices. RBAC enforcement SHALL occur at the OPA policy layer (PS-6) with DPIM v3 §10 verdict-matrix annotations for `dpim.org.dso.*` action_type. Cross-tenant access by `dso_admin` SHALL be WORM-audited per M2.11.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist §1 (RLS + RBAC matrix); DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; OPA policy spec
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: RBAC tables 008/009 have practice-level roles; `dso_admin` role not seeded

## 2.2
id: 2.2
name: Practice
purpose: The primary operational tenant entity. Every record in OSCAR is scoped to a `practice_id`, and `practice_id` is the SQL Row-Level Security anchor on every module-owned table. The Practice owns provider rosters, fee schedules, locations, operatories, and settings. Optionally a child of `DPIM_DSO` (M2.1) for DSO-managed practices; independent practices have `parentDSO = null`.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Practice` (DPIM D2 P.16, v2.0.0 — 13 properties) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (P.13) — `name`, `identifier[]`, `address`, `telecom[]`, `orgType`, `isActive` - Children declared at P.16.1: `DPIM_PracticeLocation`, `DPIM_Operatory`, `DPIM_PracticeSettings`, `DPIM_Practitioner`, `DPIM_Staff`, `DPIM_FeeSchedule` **Status lifecycle (P.16):** `onboarding` → `active` → `inactive` **FHIR alignment:** US Core Organization Profile · ADA 1084 RCDM Practice/Office section
externalIntegrations: None directly. Consumed by every other module via `practice_id` RLS scoping.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Practice` records as the primary operational tenant. Each Practice SHALL have UUID v7 `practice_id` (immutable post-create), legal `name`, optional `dbaName`, Federal TIN (`taxId`), organizational NPI Type 2 (`npiOrg`), IANA `timezone`, `practiceType` code, and `isActive` flag.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Practice hierarchy); DPIM D2 P.16; HTML m2 §2.2; Module Catalog §3.2.2
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: `sql/migrations/003_org_and_user_tables.sql` defines org schema; UUID v7 not yet end-to-end validated

### [1]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce `practice_id` as the SQL Row-Level Security anchor on every module-owned table. Each module-owned table SHALL have a `practice_id` column with a corresponding RLS policy filtering rows to the session's practice context. RLS SHALL be enabled on every module table — verified by `sql/tests/validate_indexes.sql`.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist §1 ("★ RLS enabled — all module tables have practice_id policy"); Architecture Final Draft (RLS anchor); HTML m2 §2.2 ("tenant boundary anchor")
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: `sql/migrations/002_rls_function.sql` + `004_apply_rls_policies.sql` define RLS infra; per-table policies in 022, 023, 028, 030

### [2]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support optional parent-DSO linkage via `DPIM_Practice.parentDSO` (Reference). When set, the Practice SHALL be subject to DSO-level rollups (M2-DSO-EXT-003), shared fee schedules (M2-DSO-EXT-006), and centralized billing (M2-DSO-EXT-005) where corresponding DSO flags are enabled. `parentDSO` mutation SHALL emit `dpim.org.practice.updated` with full-delta audit.
source: DPIM D2 P.16.2 (parentDSO writable, full-delta audit)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `parentDSO` column not in current schema

### [3]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `practiceType` enum: `general` / `pediatric` / `orthodontic` / `periodontic` / `endodontic` / `oral_surgery` / `prosthodontic` / `multi_specialty`. Practice type SHALL drive default operatory-type taxonomy (M2.4), default fee-schedule selection (M2.6), and downstream module behavior (M3 scheduling, M4 clinical, M5 imaging).
source: DPIM D2 P.16.3; cross-module M2.4 + M3 + M4 + M5
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Enum not yet seeded

### [4]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL store `npiOrg` (Type 2 NPI — Organizational) on every Practice record. NPI SHALL be validated against CMS NPPES format (10-digit Luhn checksum) at creation. Type 2 NPI SHALL appear on every X12 837D claim submitted from this practice.
source: DPIM D2 P.16.5; CMS NPPES NPI standards; X12 837D loop 2010AA Billing Provider
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: `npiOrg` field present in `org_and_user_tables`; NPPES validation absent; 837D in `modules/rcm/app/claims_837d/`

### [5]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL store `taxId` (Federal TIN) on every Practice record. Field sensitivity SHALL be `financial`. Audit level SHALL be `full-delta`. Tax ID SHALL appear on every X12 837D and SHALL be required for ERA/EFT enrollment. Practice creation SHALL fail if TIN is missing.
source: DPIM D2 P.16.4; X12 837D loop 2010AA; ERA 835 enrollment requirements
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Field declared; required-field enforcement absent in writer

### [6]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce IANA `timezone` (e.g., `America/New_York`) as a hard agentic constraint per `DPIM_Practice.timezone` Agentic Notes. All agent-generated date/time displays, scheduling logic, reminder timing, and TCPA quiet-hours enforcement SHALL convert to the practice's timezone — never UTC. Multi-timezone DSOs SHALL set per-location timezone overrides on `DPIM_PracticeLocation.timezone` (M2.3).
source: DPIM D2 P.16.7 + Agentic Notes ("Agent MUST use for all date/time display"); cross-module M1-CONT-EXT-006 (TCPA quiet hours)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Timezone-aware datetime enforcement is module-by-module today

### [7]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL link Practice to a default fee schedule via `defaultFeeSchedule` (Reference to `DPIM_FeeSchedule`). M6 RCM fee resolution SHALL traverse insurance-plan-specific schedules first; when none applies, SHALL fall back to the practice's `defaultFeeSchedule` (UCR — Usual, Customary, Reasonable).
source: DPIM D2 P.16.6 + Agentic Notes; cross-module RCM-DAT-006 (fee schedules)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: M6 fee resolution exists in `modules/rcm/`; default-fee fallback path not fully tested

### [8]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain practice-wide production targets — `productionTargetDaily` (Money) and `productionTargetMonthly` (Money). Daily target SHALL drive M11 Practice Pulse "Production X% of Target": `todayProduction / productionTargetDaily * 100`.
source: DPIM D2 P.16.8 + Agentic Notes (Practice Pulse formula); cross-module M11
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M11 Practice Pulse module surfaces this; computation absent

### [9]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute `activePatientCount` and `activePractitionerCount` per Practice. "Active patient" SHALL align with M1 lifecycle status `active` (per M1-LIFE-EXT-009). Counts SHALL be exposed on the Practice dashboard and roll up to DSO aggregate per M2-DSO-EXT-003.
source: DPIM D2 P.16.12, P.16.13; cross-module M1-LIFE-EXT-009 + M2.1
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Active-patient definition needs cross-module alignment with M1 lifecycle

### [10]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL gate the `onboarding → active` Practice status transition behind completion of the M2.11 TenantOnboarding workflow: RLS policy attachment, BAA execution, KV credential provisioning, default seed-data load, manifest validation. Premature transitions SHALL be rejected at the SDK validation gate with verdict `BLOCK`.

phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-module with M2.11 onboarding workflow

### [11]
reqId: M2-PRAC-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit standard Practice lifecycle events: `dpim.org.practice.created`, `dpim.org.practice.updated`, `dpim.org.practice.status_changed`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — Practice records contain no PHI; computed `activePatientCount` is internal sensitivity and SHALL NOT propagate verbatim outside the originating tenant boundary. Practice creation SHALL be restricted to `dso_admin` or `super_admin` roles via OPA — non-admin roles receive verdict `BLOCK`.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; OPA policy spec

### [12]

source: DPIM D2 P.16.10, P.16.11; PMS Mapping docs (`06 - Integration & APIs/PMS Mappings/`)

## 2.3
id: 2.3
name: Practice Location
purpose: Physical site under a Practice. A Practice may operate multiple locations (multi-location single-practice or multi-location DSO). Each Location SHALL have its own address, hours of operation, and timezone — the timezone CAN differ from the parent Practice for chains spanning timezones. Location is the parent of operatories (M2.4).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_PracticeLocation` (DPIM D2 P.17, v2.0.0 — 9 properties) - v3-new related: `DPIM_TaxJurisdiction` (per-location tax — **property-doc-pending**) **FHIR alignment:** US Core Location Profile
externalIntegrations: Address validation (USPS — vendor TBD); geocoding (Azure Maps with BAA — recommended).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PracticeLocation` records as the physical-site entity, child of `DPIM_Practice`. Each location SHALL have `practice` (Reference, immutable), `name` (display), `address` (full Address), `telecom[]` (phone/fax/email), and `isAcceptingPatients` boolean. A Practice SHALL have at least one active location.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4; DPIM D2 P.17; HTML m2 §2.3; Module Catalog §3.2.3
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No `practice_locations` table in current schema; locations modeled inline on practice today

### [1]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support multi-location practices — a Practice MAY have one or more `DPIM_PracticeLocation` children (cardinality 1..*). Schedules (M3), operatories (M2.4), and providers SHALL be assignable per-location. Cross-location double-booking SHALL be prevented at scheduling time per M3-3-EXT-02 + M3-APT-011.
source: DPIM D2 P.16.1 (hasLocation 1..*); HTML m2 §2.3; cross-module M3-APT-011 + M3-3-EXT-02
phase: P2
status: In Progress
evidence: M3 multi-location not on main; documented gap

### [2]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support location-specific NPI Type 2 where applicable — independent locations operating under their own organizational NPI (common in DSO contexts). Location NPI SHALL be optional; when null, the parent practice's `npiOrg` (M2-PRAC-EXT-005) SHALL be used. **Property is currently doc-gap in DPIM D2 v2.0.0** — recommended for D2 v3 update.
source: HTML m2 §2.3 ("location-specific NPI if applicable"); Module Catalog §3.2.3; DPIM D2 P.17 (gap)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Property absent from D2 v2.0.0; flagged for v3 update

### [3]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain per-location `hoursOfOperation` as per-day-of-week BackboneElement: `{dayOfWeek, openTime, closeTime, isClosed}`. Hours SHALL be a hard agentic constraint — no agent SHALL propose appointments outside published hours, including online booking, AI scheduling recommendations, or watcher-triggered fill-gap actions.
source: DPIM D2 P.17.5 + Agentic Notes ("hard scheduling constraint"); cross-module M3 (booking)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: M3 today validates hours per-practice not per-location

### [4]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain per-location IANA `timezone` (P.17.6) — MAY differ from parent Practice timezone for chains spanning timezones (e.g., a DSO with EST and PST locations). All location-scoped agentic logic (booking, reminders, TCPA quiet-hours) SHALL use the **location's** timezone, not the practice's.
source: DPIM D2 P.17.6; cross-module M1-CONT-EXT-006 (TCPA) + M3 + M7
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Per-location timezone not threaded through scheduling / comms

### [5]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `geoCoordinates` (`{latitude, longitude}`) on `DPIM_PracticeLocation` for proximity calculations: nearest-location routing for new patients (DSO multi-location), referral-partner distance ranking (M2.9), and patient-to-location matching when patients see multiple locations. Geocoding SHALL use a vendor with HIPAA BAA — recommended Azure Maps.
source: DPIM D2 P.17.7 + Agentic Notes ("nearest location" logic); HIPAA BAA requirement
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Geocoding vendor selection deferred

### [6]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL link `DPIM_PracticeLocation` to a `DPIM_TaxJurisdiction` (v3-new) for per-location sales-tax computation on OTC sales (M6) and tax-relevant fee items. Where a location lacks an explicit `TaxJurisdiction` link, the practice's default jurisdiction SHALL apply. **`DPIM_TaxJurisdiction` property doc is pending** — flagged for D2 v3 update.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (TaxJurisdiction v3-new); HTML m2 §2.3 ("tax rates"); cross-module M6 OTC tax
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [7]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support `isAcceptingPatients` boolean per location. When false, online-booking and new-patient-registration flows SHALL exclude this location from selection lists. Existing-patient appointments SHALL continue to be servable at the location.
source: DPIM D2 P.17.9; cross-module M1 (registration) + M3 (online booking)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Online-booking selection logic absent

### [8]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit location lifecycle events: `dpim.org.practice_location.created`, `dpim.org.practice_location.updated` (on address mutation, hours mutation), `dpim.org.practice_location.status_changed` (on isAcceptingPatients toggle). **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — locations carry no PHI; subscribers (M3, M7) SHALL refresh cached schedule context on update.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; DPIM D2 P.17 (event triggers on address + hours + status)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

### [9]
reqId: M2-LOC-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL audit address mutations on `DPIM_PracticeLocation.address` at `full-delta` audit level (per D2 P.17.3). Practice address change SHALL trigger payer credentialing re-verification (M2.6) and is a regulatory-relevant event for state dental board notifications. Audit entries SHALL include before/after Address snapshots.
source: DPIM D2 P.17.3 (full-delta audit); state dental board change-of-address rules
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: PS-7 WORM audit infra exists; full-delta on address not specifically tested

## 2.4
id: 2.4
name: Operatory
purpose: Individual treatment room / dental chair. Child of `DPIM_PracticeLocation` (M2.3); each Practice has many operatories spread across one or more locations. Operatory type is the foundation of the procedure-first scheduling constraint chain (M3.1 procedure → operatory → provider → time slot), and operatory-resident hard-stops (M1.8 latex allergy, M1.10 hard-stop categories) are enforced at the operatory level.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Operatory` (DPIM D2 P.18, v2.0.0 — 10 properties) - Cross-module references: `DPIM_Device` (M9 Inventory) for equipment installed; `DPIM_Practitioner` (M10) for assigned providers
externalIntegrations: None directly.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-OP-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Operatory` records as the treatment-room entity, child of `DPIM_PracticeLocation`. Each operatory SHALL have `location` (Reference, immutable), `name` (display, e.g., "Op 1", "Hygiene Bay A"), `operatoryType` (enum), and `isActive` boolean. A Practice SHALL have at least one active operatory per active Location.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Operatory hierarchy); DPIM D2 P.18; HTML m2 §2.4; Module Catalog §3.2.4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: No `org.operatory` table in current schema; operatory referenced by M3 scheduling but not yet a first-class entity

### [1]
reqId: M2-OP-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `operatoryType` enum: `general` / `hygiene` / `surgical` / `ortho` / `pediatric` / `imaging` / `consultation`. Type SHALL be a hard scheduling constraint per M3-APT-010 — hygiene operatories accept only hygienist procedures; surgical operatories accept only surgical procedures; general operatories accept all. Mismatch at booking SHALL be rejected with verdict `BLOCK`.
source: DPIM D2 P.18.3 + Agentic Notes ("operatoryType → appointmentType matching"); cross-module M3-APT-010
phase: P1
status: In Progress
evidence: M3 writer.py partial constraint enforcement; not exhaustive

### [2]
reqId: M2-OP-EXT-003

### [4]

### [5]

Hard-stop SHALL surface a banner alert on operatory check-in screen and BLOCK the operatory-prep action.

Operatory create/update SHALL be restricted to `office_manager` and `owner` roles per OPA.

## 2.5
id: 2.5
name: Practice Settings
purpose: Practice-level configuration singleton — exactly one `DPIM_PracticeSettings` per Practice (cardinality 1..1 per `DPIM_Practice.hasSettings`). Embodies the **Configuration-as-Constraint Pattern** (D2 §1) — 18 properties, **zero event triggers**: settings are loaded once at agent session start and enforced throughout. Owns scheduling constraints, clinical defaults, financial defaults, intelligence thresholds, AI feature toggles, compliance rules, and communication rules. Per scope-confirmation, also the canonical home for v3-new banking + merchant + tax-jurisdiction configuration and the generalized config-mutation audit covering DSO toggle properties.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_PracticeSettings` (DPIM D2 P.19, v2.0.0 — 18 properties; all `change-log` audit; zero event triggers) - v3-new (property-doc-pending, per scope-confirmation #1): - `DPIM_BankAccount` (under `DPIM_Practice`; attachable at DSO when `centralizedBilling=true`) - `DPIM_MerchantConfig` (payment processor configuration) - `DPIM_TaxJurisdiction` (per-practice tax-rate authority; per-location override per M2-LOC-EXT-007)
externalIntegrations: Payment processors (Stripe / Square / Clover — vendor TBD with HIPAA BAA); state tax-rate authorities (TBD).

### [0]
reqId: M2-SET-EXT-001

PracticeSettings SHALL be created automatically on Practice transition `onboarding → active` (M2-PRAC-EXT-011) with system-defined default values. Deletion SHALL be blocked while the parent Practice is `active`.
source: DPIM D2 P.19 (singleton); DPIM v3 §4 hierarchy (`hasSettings 1..1`); cross-module M2.11 onboarding

## 2.6
id: 2.6
name: Insurance Companies & Plans
purpose: The carrier registry and plan-template repository — defines the universe of payers the practice contracts with and the per-plan benefit structure that drives every M6 RCM eligibility check, predetermination, treatment-plan estimate, claim submission, and ERA reconciliation. Per DPIM D2 §3 "Insurance Plan as Rules Engine" pattern, `DPIM_InsurancePlan` is a **declarative rules engine** evaluated in deterministic priority order during estimation. Per DPIM D2 §2 "Computed Intelligence Pattern," carrier-level computed properties (`averageDaysToPayment`, `averageDenialRate`) drive Revenue Flow forecasting and claim-prep risk assessment.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_InsuranceCompany` (DPIM D2 P.20, v2.0.0 — 14 properties) - `DPIM_InsurancePlan` (DPIM D2 P.21, v2.0.0 — 17 properties) - `DPIM_PayerContract` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (P.13) for InsuranceCompany — `name`, `identifier[]`, `address`, `telecom[]`, `orgType=insurance_company`, `isActive` - Distinct from: `DPIM_Coverage` (M6 patient-specific enrollment), `DPIM_BenefitInstance` (M6 patient benefit consumption), `DPIM_Eligibility` (M6 270/271 result)
externalIntegrations: - **CMS NPPES** — payer ID lookup - **Vyne Dental** — primary clearinghouse for X12 270/271/277CA/837D/835 routing per payer (M2.10) - **Stedi** — failover clearinghouse (M2.10) - **NEA (National Electronic Attachments)** — claim-attachment routing for payers with `supportsAttachments=true` - **Change Healthcare / DentalXChange** — alternate clearinghouse paths per payer

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_InsuranceCompany` records as the carrier registry. Each carrier SHALL have UUID v7 record id, `name` (legal entity), `payerId` (electronic payer identifier — primary), optional `payerIdSecondary` (alternate clearinghouse-specific ID), `claimsAddress` (paper claims), `claimsPhone`, `providerRelationsPhone`, `portalUrl`, `isActive` flag. `payerId` SHALL be `full-delta` audit per D2 P.20.1.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (InsuranceCompany hierarchy); DPIM D2 P.20; HTML m2 §2.6; Module Catalog §3.2.6
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: `org` schema in 003 has carrier table; full property set not modeled

### [1]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain per-carrier `claimSubmissionMethod` (`electronic` / `paper` / `both`) and optional `defaultClearinghouse` (Reference to `DPIM_Clearinghouse`). M6 RCM submission pipeline SHALL resolve clearinghouse routing in priority order: (1) per-carrier `defaultClearinghouse`; (2) practice-level `DPIM_PracticeSettings.defaultClearinghouse` (M2-SET-EXT-004); (3) global default (Vyne primary).
source: DPIM D2 P.20.7, P.20.8; cross-module M2.5 + M2.10 + M6
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: M6 routing logic in `modules/rcm/app/clearinghouse/`; per-carrier override path partial

### [2]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute carrier-level intelligence properties (Computed Intelligence Pattern, D2 §2): `averageDaysToPayment` (from M6 claim-payment history) and `averageDenialRate` (from M6 claim-denial history). Both SHALL be `read-only` for humans and agents. Properties SHALL drive: (a) Revenue Flow cash-flow prediction (M11); (b) treatment-plan cost-estimate risk flagging (M6); (c) claim follow-up prioritization for slow payers (M6).
source: DPIM D2 P.20.9, P.20.10 + Agentic Notes; cross-module M11 + M6
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics pipeline absent

### [3]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain payer capability flags: `supportsRealTimeEligibility` (270/271), `supportsERAs` (835), `supportsAttachments` (NEA). Capability flags SHALL gate M6 transaction routing — e.g., `supportsRealTimeEligibility=false` SHALL force eligibility verification to portal-scrape (sib-payerportal sibling product) or manual workflow.
source: DPIM D2 P.20.11–P.20.13; cross-module M6 (eligibility/ERA/attachments); sib-payerportal sibling product fallback
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Capability-driven routing not implemented

### [4]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain a free-text `notes` field on each `DPIM_InsuranceCompany` (sensitivity `internal`, agent access `read-write`). Practice-curated payer intelligence (e.g., "always requires narrative for D2750", "denies bitewings on same-day prophy") SHALL be agent-readable for claim-preparation intelligence. **Notes SHALL NOT contain PHI** — validation gate SHALL reject any patient identifier in notes payload.
source: DPIM D2 P.20.14 + Agentic Notes ("notes valuable for agent intelligence"); SDK `validate_no_phi` boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Free-text validation gate absent

### [5]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_InsurancePlan` records as the per-carrier plan template (cardinality `1..*` plans per InsuranceCompany via `plan.payer` immutable Reference). Each plan SHALL have `planName`, `planType` enum, optional `groupNumber`, optional `feeSchedule` Reference. Plan deletion SHALL be blocked while any patient `DPIM_Coverage` references the plan (M6).
source: DPIM v3 §4; DPIM D2 P.21; HTML m2 §2.6; cross-module M6 Coverage
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Plan template table absent from current schema

### [6]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce `planType` enum: `DHMO` / `DPPO` / `indemnity` / `discount` / `capitation` / `medicaid` / `medicare` / `tricare`. Plan type SHALL determine the estimation-algorithm branch (per D2 P.21.3 Agentic Notes): PPO uses fee schedule + coinsurance; DHMO uses copay schedule; indemnity uses UCR; capitation uses copay only. M6 estimator SHALL branch deterministically on `planType` value.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.3 + Agentic Notes ("planType determines the entire estimation algorithm"); cross-module M6 estimator
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: M6 estimator logic in `modules/rcm/`; plan-type branching partial

### [7]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL link plans to a contracted `DPIM_FeeSchedule` via `feeSchedule` (Reference). M6 fee resolution SHALL traverse: (1) plan-specific schedule; (2) practice `DPIM_Practice.defaultFeeSchedule` (M2-PRAC-EXT-008) when null; (3) DSO-shared schedule when `DPIM_DSO.sharedFeeSchedules=true` (M2-DSO-EXT-006). `feeSchedule` Reference SHALL be `full-delta` audit per D2 P.21.5 (contract-relationship change).
source: DPIM D2 P.21.5; cross-module RCM-DAT-006 + M2-PRAC-EXT-008 + M2-DSO-EXT-006
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: M6 fee resolution exists; full hierarchy traversal not exhaustively tested

### [8]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain plan-level financial caps: `annualMaximum` (Money), `annualMaximumOrtho` (Money — separate ortho lifetime/annual cap), `deductibleIndividual` (Money), `deductibleFamily` (Money), and `deductibleAppliesTo` (code list — `preventive` / `basic` / `major` / `ortho`). M6 estimator SHALL track deductible consumption via `DPIM_BenefitInstance` and cap estimates at remaining annual maximum.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.6–P.21.10; cross-module M6 BenefitInstance
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Eligibility harness in `modules/rcm/eligibility-harness/` extracts caps; cap-tracking + ortho separation absent

### [9]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `benefitCategories` BackboneElement[] on each plan: `{category, coinsurancePercent, copay, waitingPeriodMonths}` per category in `preventive` / `basic` / `major` / `ortho` / `implant`. M6 estimator SHALL select category by procedure-CDT mapping and apply coinsurance/copay accordingly.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.11; cross-module M6 + CDT taxonomy (`sql/migrations/006_cdt_and_fee_schedule_tables.sql`)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: CDT seeded; category-to-procedure mapping in `modules/rcm/payer-rules/`

### [10]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `frequencyLimitations` BackboneElement[] as `{procedureCode, frequency, periodMonths}` (e.g., "D0120 2x per 12 months"). Per D2 P.21.12 Agentic Notes, frequency limits are **the most critical property for estimation accuracy** — M6 estimator SHALL: (a) find all completed procedures of the same code within the frequency period for this patient; (b) when limit met, estimate $0 insurance coverage and surface "limit reached" warning to the provider during treatment-planning.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.12 + Agentic Notes ("most critical for estimation accuracy"); cross-module M6 estimator + M4 treatment plan
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Frequency rules in `modules/rcm/payer-rules/payers/`; cross-patient history lookup partial

### [11]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `ageRestrictions` BackboneElement[] as `{procedureCode, minAge, maxAge}` (e.g., "sealants only for patients under 14"). M6 estimator SHALL evaluate restrictions against `DPIM_Patient.dob` and surface exclusion warnings during treatment planning.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.13; cross-module M6 + M1 (patient DOB)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Age-restriction enforcement absent

### [12]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-013
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `waitingPeriods` BackboneElement[] as `{category, waitingPeriodMonths, effectiveFrom}` per benefit category. M6 estimator SHALL evaluate waiting periods against `DPIM_Coverage.effectiveFrom` (M6) and apply $0 coverage when within waiting period.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.14; cross-module M6 Coverage
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Waiting-period enforcement absent

### [13]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-014
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `coordinationOfBenefits` (`standard` / `non_duplication` / `maintenance_of_benefits` / `carve_out`) and `benefitYearStart` (`calendar_year` / `fiscal_year` / `enrollment_anniversary`). COB method SHALL drive secondary-claim calculation in M6 (when patient has multiple coverages). `benefitYearStart` SHALL drive benefits-remaining math — fiscal-year and anniversary plans require non-calendar reset awareness per D2 P.21.16 Agentic Notes.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.15, P.21.16 + Agentic Notes ("benefitYearStart essential for benefits remaining"); cross-module M6 secondary claims
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: COB calculation + non-calendar reset absent

### [14]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-015
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `missingToothClause` boolean per plan. When `true` and a treatment plan includes prosthodontic procedures (bridge, implant, partial), M6 estimator SHALL evaluate whether the relevant teeth were missing prior to coverage `effectiveFrom` and warn the provider that the claim is at high denial risk per D2 P.21.17 Agentic Notes.
source: DPIM D2 P.21.17 + Agentic Notes ("common source of claim denials"); cross-module M6 + M4 treatment plan
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Missing-tooth detection absent

### [15]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-016
requirement: OSCAR M6 estimator SHALL implement the **Insurance Plan as Rules Engine** evaluation order (D2 §3) in this deterministic priority: (1) within waiting period → $0; (2) frequency limit blocks → $0; (3) age restriction excludes → $0; (4) missing-tooth clause applies → $0; (5) apply deductible to category if applicable; (6) apply coinsurance from benefit category; (7) cap at annual maximum remaining. The estimator SHALL produce auditable trace explaining which specific plan property drove each decision.
source: DPIM D2 §3 Insurance Plan as Rules Engine pattern; cross-module M6 estimator
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Estimator evaluation order not yet codified

### [16]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-017
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PayerContract` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records linking Practice to InsuranceCompany with contract lifecycle: `contractStatus` (`negotiating` / `contracted` / `terminated`), `inNetworkStatus` per Practitioner, `effectiveFrom`, `effectiveThrough`, contracted `feeSchedule` Reference. Contract status SHALL gate `inNetwork` flag on M6 patient encounters and drive billed-vs-allowed amount calculations.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (PayerContract v3-new); cross-module RCM-DAT-006 + M6 + M10 (practitioner credentialing)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending; contract-lifecycle workflow absent

### [17]
reqId: M2-INS-EXT-018
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit InsuranceCompany + InsurancePlan + PayerContract lifecycle events: `dpim.org.insurance_company.created` / `.updated` / `.status_changed`; `dpim.org.insurance_plan.created` / `.updated`; `dpim.org.payer_contract.created` / `.updated` / `.status_changed`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — carrier and plan records contain no PHI; subscribers (M6 Coverage, M11 Revenue Flow analytics, M2.10 routing-table cache) SHALL refresh on update. Mutation SHALL be restricted to `billing` and `office_manager` roles per OPA; `payer_contract.status_changed` to `terminated` SHALL additionally require `owner` role.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3); RBAC matrix not seeded

## openQuestions

### [0]
id: OQ-2.6.1
text: `DPIM_PayerContract` (v3-new) has no property doc. Recommend D2 v3 update with full property set: contractStatus, inNetworkStatus[], effectiveFrom/effectiveThrough, contracted feeSchedule, participatingProviderTypes[], reimbursement schedule, attachment policies. *Counts toward Finding #4.*

### [1]
id: OQ-2.6.2
text: `planType` enum doesn't include `discount_dental_plan` (Careington-style aggregator), state-specific Medicaid variants (MediCal, Texas Medicaid). Recommend D2 v3 add granular state-Medicaid sub-types or document mapping convention.

### [2]
id: OQ-2.6.3
text: Frequency-limitation cross-patient read latency: estimating a 10-procedure plan against 5 frequency rules requires ~50 cross-module reads. Recommend per-patient frequency-state cache in PS-3 Practice Snapshot.

### [3]
id: OQ-2.6.4
text: `coordinationOfBenefits` carve-out is functionally a separate plan, not a COB rule. Recommend clarify whether carve-out belongs in COB enum or in a different plan-type field.

### [4]
id: OQ-2.6.5
text: Plan-level narrative requirements (per-CDT-code) currently captured in carrier `notes`. Recommend lift into dedicated `narrativeRequirements` BackboneElement[] on `DPIM_InsurancePlan` for D2 v3.

## 2.7
id: 2.7
name: Dental Labs
purpose: External dental laboratory registry — vendors fabricating crowns, bridges, dentures, partials, ortho appliances, surgical guides, and night guards. Distinct from M9 `DPIM_LabCaseTracking` (operations side: case state, ship/receive lifecycle) and M4 `DPIM_LabCase` (clinical view: case linked to procedure). M2.7 owns the **lab-vendor catalog**; M9 owns case operations; M4 owns clinical linkage. Per DPIM D2 §2 Computed Intelligence Pattern, `averageTurnaroundDays` and `remakeRate` drive scheduling intelligence (seat appointment slot prediction) and Cost Control supplier-review recommendations.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_DentalLab` (DPIM D2 P.22, v2.0.0 — 10 properties) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (P.13) — `name`, `identifier[]`, `address`, `telecom[]`, `orgType=dental_lab`, `isActive`
externalIntegrations: - **DDX (Dental Data Exchange)** — digital case submission protocol - **LabStar** — lab management platform integration - **Magic Touch / Evident** — alternate lab platforms (TBD) - **ANSI/ADA Dental Lab Standard 130-12** — case-specification interchange - **STL file format** — digital impressions (cross-module M5 Imaging)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_DentalLab` records as the lab-vendor registry. Each lab SHALL have UUID v7 record id, `name`, `shippingAddress` (Address for physical case shipment), `telecom[]`, `contactPerson` (primary lab contact), per-practice `accountNumber`, and `isActive` flag.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (DentalLab hierarchy); DPIM D2 P.22; HTML m2 §2.7; Module Catalog §3.2.7
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `org.dental_lab` table not in current schema; M9 LabCase has lab_id reference but lab table absent

### [1]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `specialties` code list per lab: `crown_bridge` / `removable` / `implant` / `ortho_appliance` / `night_guard` / `whitening_tray` / `surgical_guide`. M9 LabCase creation SHALL match case type to lab specialty — case routing SHALL prefer labs with matching specialty per D2 P.22.1 Agentic Notes.
source: DPIM D2 P.22.1 + Agentic Notes ("Agent matches LabCase type to lab capability"); cross-module M9 LabCase
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: M9 LabCase routing not yet implemented

### [2]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `acceptsDigitalImpressions` boolean per lab (STL file submission via DDX or proprietary protocol). When `true` and an `DPIM_IntraOralScan` (M5 Imaging v3-new) exists for the case, M9 SHALL prefer digital workflow over physical impression shipment. When `false`, OSCAR SHALL fall back to physical impression with shipping label generation.
source: DPIM D2 P.22.3 + Agentic Notes ("considers when recommending workflow"); cross-module M5 + M9
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M5 IntraOralScan v3-new; cross-module digital workflow absent

### [3]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `shadeSystem` code per lab: `vita_classical` / `vita_3d_master` / `ivoclar` / `other`. M4 clinical workflow SHALL reference lab `shadeSystem` when capturing shade selection for prosthodontic procedures so the recorded shade matches the lab's matching system.
source: DPIM D2 P.22.10; cross-module M4 (shade capture during prep)
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 not extracted; shade-system propagation absent

### [4]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute `averageTurnaroundDays` per lab (Computed Intelligence Pattern, D2 §2) from `DPIM_LabCaseTracking` (M9) completion history. M3 scheduling intelligence SHALL use this value to recommend seat-appointment date as `procedureDate + averageTurnaroundDays + 2 buffer days` per D2 P.22.4 Agentic Notes.
source: DPIM D2 P.22.4 + Agentic Notes ("seat appointment scheduling window"); cross-module M3 + M9
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics + M9 case-completion tracking absent

### [5]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute `averageCost` (Money), `qualityRating` (decimal 0.0–5.0), and `remakeRate` (decimal 0.0–1.0) per lab. `remakeRate` above the practice-configured threshold (default 0.05) SHALL trigger a "Review Suppliers" recommendation in M11 Cost Control per D2 P.22.7 Agentic Notes. `qualityRating` SHALL be derived from remake rate + provider feedback (cross-module M11 feedback collection).
source: DPIM D2 P.22.5–P.22.7 + Agentic Notes; cross-module M11
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics + provider feedback collection absent

### [6]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain per-practice `accountNumber` on `DPIM_DentalLab` (sensitivity `internal`). Account number SHALL appear on every lab-case shipping label and prescription RX. Changes SHALL be `change-log` audit.
source: DPIM D2 P.22.9; cross-module M9 LabCase shipping labels
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Lab-case label generation absent

### [7]
reqId: M2-LAB-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit lab-vendor lifecycle events: `dpim.org.dental_lab.created` / `.updated` / `.status_changed`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — lab-vendor records contain no PHI; subscribers (M9 case routing, M11 Cost Control analytics) SHALL refresh on update. Mutation SHALL be restricted to `office_manager` and `owner` roles per OPA.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)
