## 2.8
id: 2.8
name: Suppliers
purpose: Supply vendor registry — Henry Schein, Patterson Dental, Benco Dental, Net32, plus office-supply, equipment, IT-services, and marketing vendors. Distinct from M2.7 Dental Labs (lab-fab vendors) and M2.6 Insurance Companies (payers). Owns vendor identity, account credentials, payment terms, ordering portal URLs, punch-out catalog configuration, and YTD spend rollups. M9 Inventory consumes this registry for purchase orders, par-level reordering, and three-way invoice matching.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Supplier` (DPIM D2 P.23, v2.0.0 — 8 properties) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (P.13) - v3-new related (M9 territory, cross-module): `DPIM_PurchaseOrder`, `DPIM_GoodsReceipt`, `DPIM_VendorInvoice`
externalIntegrations: - **Henry Schein One** — punch-out catalog (cXML protocol) - **Patterson Dental** — punch-out catalog - **Benco Dental** — punch-out catalog - **Net32 Marketplace** — competitive pricing aggregator (REST API) - **Punch-out OCI / cXML standards** — B2B procurement protocols

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Supplier` records as the supply-vendor registry. Each supplier SHALL have UUID v7 record id, `name`, `supplierType` (enum), per-practice `accountNumber`, optional `accountRep` + `repPhone`, optional `orderingUrl` (online portal), `paymentTerms` string (e.g., "Net 30"), and `isActive`. Account credentials (login/password) SHALL NOT be stored on the supplier record — credentials live in Azure Key Vault (IF-4) referenced by handle.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4; DPIM D2 P.23; HTML m2 §2.8; Module Catalog §3.2.8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Supplier table absent from current schema

### [1]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce `supplierType` enum: `dental_supply` / `office_supply` / `equipment` / `it_services` / `marketing`. M9 Inventory routing SHALL match supply category to supplier type — clinical supplies route to `dental_supply` vendors only; equipment maintenance routes to `equipment` vendors.
source: DPIM D2 P.23.1; cross-module M9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: M9 routing absent

### [2]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `orderingUrl` (uri) per supplier for online-portal access. M9 Inventory low-stock alerts SHALL deep-link to the supplier's portal where applicable. URLs SHALL be public-sensitivity (no embedded credentials) per D2 P.23.5.
source: DPIM D2 P.23.5; cross-module M9
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M9 low-stock workflow absent

### [3]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **punch-out catalog integration** for the four major dental-supply vendors — Henry Schein, Patterson Dental, Benco Dental, Net32 — via cXML 1.2 or OCI 4.0 protocols. Punch-out flow SHALL: (a) initiate from M9 PO line item; (b) hand off to vendor catalog; (c) return PunchOutOrderMessage with line items + pricing; (d) re-enter OSCAR for PO completion. Vendor authentication SHALL use Key Vault-stored shared secret per vendor.
source: HTML m2 §2.8 ("catalog punch-out"); Module Catalog §3.2.8; cXML 1.2 / OCI 4.0 standards; cross-module IF-4 + M9
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Punch-out integration absent; vendor SDKs not yet evaluated

### [4]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support per-supplier auto-reorder threshold linkage — when `DPIM_StockLevel` (M9, v3-new) drops below `DPIM_ParThreshold` (M9, v3-new), M9 SHALL generate a `DPIM_PurchaseOrder` (M9, v3-new) routed to the configured supplier per supply item. Auto-reorder SHALL respect `DPIM_PracticeSettings.aiFeatureToggles.actionCards` master toggle (M2-SET-EXT-011).
source: HTML m2 §2.8 ("per-supplier auto-reorder thresholds"); cross-module M9 + M2.5
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M9 par-threshold workflow absent

### [5]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support price-comparison across suppliers for matching SKUs — M11 Cost Control SHALL surface "Net32 cheaper by $X" recommendations when an alternate supplier offers the same SKU at lower price (default threshold 10% savings). Comparison SHALL respect supplier-specific contract pricing (some practices have negotiated rates with Schein/Patterson).
source: HTML m2 §2.8 ("price comparison"); cross-module M9 + M11
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M11 Cost Control not yet extracted

### [6]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute supplier-level spend rollups (Computed Intelligence Pattern, D2 §2): `totalSpendYTD` (Money) and `totalSpendPriorYear` (Money) per supplier. M11 Cost Control SHALL surface "Top 5 Suppliers by Spend" and YoY-trend indicators. Spend SHALL aggregate from `DPIM_VendorInvoice` (M9, v3-new).
source: DPIM D2 P.23.7, P.23.8; cross-module M9 + M11
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M9 vendor-invoice tracking absent

### [7]
reqId: M2-SUPP-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit supplier lifecycle events: `dpim.org.supplier.created` / `.updated` / `.status_changed`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — supplier records contain no PHI; subscribers (M9 ordering, M11 Cost Control) SHALL refresh on update. Mutation SHALL be restricted to `office_manager` and `owner` roles per OPA; supplier credential mutations SHALL be `full-delta` audit.
source: DPIM v3 §6 event taxonomy; DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix

## 2.9
id: 2.9
name: Referral Partners
purpose: External provider/organization registry for referral relationships — specialists (oral surgery, perio, endo, ortho, pedo, prostho), general dentists, physicians, hospitals, plus patient-as-source ("Mrs. Smith referred her sister"). Owns partner identity (NPI Type 1 for individual providers), specialty taxonomy (NUCC), referral-direction tracking (inbound/outbound), conversion analytics, and FHIR digital-referral capability flags. Cross-module with M7 Communications (referral-letter delivery), M11 Strategy Zone (referral ROI), and M12 Embezzlement & Fraud (anti-kickback compliance — surfacing only).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ReferralPartner` (DPIM D2 P.24, v2.0.0 — 10 properties) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (P.13) - Cross-module: `DPIM_OutboundReferral` (Communication domain v2), `DPIM_InboundReferral` (Communication v2)
externalIntegrations: - **CMS NPPES NPI Registry** — NPI Type 1 (individual provider) lookup + validation - **NUCC Healthcare Provider Taxonomy** — specialty code authority - **FHIR Dental Data Exchange Implementation Guide** — digital referral exchange (Referral/Consult Note bundles) - **Stark Law (42 USC 1395nn)** + **Anti-Kickback Statute (42 CFR 411 Subpart J)** — referral compliance framework

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-REF-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ReferralPartner` records as the referral-partner registry. Each partner SHALL have UUID v7 record id, `name`, `partnerType` (enum), optional `practitionerName` (HumanName for individual providers), optional NPI Type 1, optional `specialty` (NUCC CodeableConcept), `address`, `telecom[]`, and `isActive`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4; DPIM D2 P.24; HTML m2 §2.9; Module Catalog §3.2.9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `org.referral_partner` table absent from current schema

### [1]
reqId: M2-REF-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce `partnerType` enum: `specialist` / `general_dentist` / `physician` / `hospital` / `patient` / `other`. Type SHALL drive referral routing per D2 P.24.1 Agentic Notes — agent matches referral need (e.g., endodontic) to partners with matching `specialty` taxonomy code. `patient` type SHALL link to a `DPIM_Patient` record (M1) representing patient-as-referral-source.
source: DPIM D2 P.24.1; cross-module M1 (patient-as-source linkage) + M7 (routing)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Routing absent

### [2]

source: DPIM D2 P.24.2; NUCC taxonomy; cross-module M7 search
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: NUCC taxonomy not seeded; cardinality gap

### [3]
reqId: M2-REF-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL store NPI Type 1 (individual provider NPI) on `DPIM_ReferralPartner` when applicable. NPI SHALL be validated against CMS NPPES format (10-digit Luhn checksum) at creation and OPTIONALLY validated against NPPES live registry at runtime when feature flag `referralNpiLiveValidation` is enabled. **NPI Type 1 (individual) is distinct from NPI Type 2 (organizational)** stored on `DPIM_Practice.npiOrg` (M2-PRAC-EXT-005).

### [4]

Counts SHALL aggregate from M7 `DPIM_InboundReferral` and `DPIM_OutboundReferral` events.
source: DPIM D2 P.24.5–P.24.8 + Agentic Notes; cross-module M7
phase: P2
status: Not Started

### [5]
reqId: M2-REF-EXT-006

Recommendation SHALL respect `DPIM_PracticeSettings.aiFeatureToggles.actionCards` master toggle (M2-SET-EXT-011).

### [6]

When `false`, M7 SHALL fall back to printable referral form (PDF) via M8 Documents or fax via PS-5 Delivery.

M12 Embezzlement & Fraud sub-module 12.9 (Referral & Kickback Compliance) SHALL ingest these flags for compliance monitoring (**M11 IntegrityAlerts surfaces; M12 investigates** per cross-module routing convention §2.5). **Property is currently doc-gap in DPIM D2 v2.0.0.**
source: Stark Law 42 USC 1395nn; Anti-Kickback Statute 42 CFR 411 Subpart J; cross-module M11 (surfacing) + M12 (investigation); D2 v2.0.0 gap

### [9]

Subscribers (M7, M11, M12) SHALL refresh on update. Mutation SHALL be restricted to `office_manager` / `owner` per OPA; `compensationRelationship` mutation SHALL be `full-delta` audit.

phase: P1

## 2.10
id: 2.10
name: Clearinghouses
purpose: Electronic claims-clearinghouse registry — Vyne Dental (primary), Stedi (failover, transitional), plus alternate paths (Change Healthcare, DentalXChange). Owns clearinghouse identity, submission/report endpoints, format support (X12 837D/837P/NEA), credential references (Azure Key Vault), and computed reliability intelligence (submission success rate, processing latency). M6 RCM consumes this registry for every claim submission, ERA receipt, and attachment upload. Per `agenticAccess: blocked` annotations on submission endpoints + login credentials (D2 P.25.2/P.25.3/P.25.5), agents NEVER directly interact with clearinghouse infrastructure — claim submission is platform-pipeline territory.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Clearinghouse` (DPIM D2 P.25, v2.0.0 — 9 properties) - Inherited abstract `DPIM_Organization` (P.13) - v3-new related: `DPIM_Integration` + `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` (Governance domain — host clearinghouse credentials)
externalIntegrations: - **Vyne Dental** — primary clearinghouse (X12 270/271/277CA/837D/835 + NEA attachments) - **Stedi** — failover clearinghouse (transitional, per CAIS Phase 0 Stedi Integration Strategy) - **Change Healthcare** — alternate clearinghouse path - **DentalXChange** — alternate clearinghouse path - **NEA (National Electronic Attachments)** — claim attachments

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-CLR-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Clearinghouse` records as the clearinghouse registry. Each clearinghouse SHALL have UUID v7 record id, `name`, `clearinghouseType` (`primary` / `secondary` / `attachment_only`), `submissionFormat` (`x12_837d` / `x12_837p` / `nea` / `custom`), `submissionEndpoint` (uri, agent access `blocked`), optional `reportEndpoint` (uri, agent access `blocked`), and `isActive`. Endpoints SHALL be `full-delta` audit per D2 P.25.2.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Clearinghouse hierarchy); DPIM D2 P.25; HTML m2 §2.10; Module Catalog §3.2.10
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: `org.clearinghouse` table referenced by `modules/rcm/app/clearinghouse/`; full property set not modeled

### [1]
reqId: M2-CLR-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL designate Vyne Dental as the **primary** clearinghouse and Stedi as the **failover** clearinghouse for X12 837D submission per practice. Designation SHALL be configurable per-tenant via `DPIM_PracticeSettings.defaultClearinghouse` (M2-SET-EXT-004) with carrier-level overrides per `DPIM_InsuranceCompany.defaultClearinghouse` (M2-INS-EXT-002). M6 RCM submission resolution priority: (1) carrier override; (2) practice default; (3) global Vyne primary.
source: HTML m2 §2.10 ("Vyne primary → Stedi failover"); CAIS Stedi Integration Strategy doc; cross-module M2.5 + M2.6 + M6

### [2]

Clearinghouse `loginId` field on `DPIM_Clearinghouse` (D2 P.25.5) SHALL be `agentic-access: blocked` — agents NEVER read credential values. Credential rotation SHALL be tracked via `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` lifecycle and `full-delta` audited.

phase: P1

### [3]
reqId: M2-CLR-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `submissionFormat` per clearinghouse: `x12_837d` (dental claims) / `x12_837p` (professional claims, secondary use) / `nea` (attachments) / `custom`. Routing SHALL match transaction type to clearinghouse format support — 837D-only clearinghouses SHALL NOT receive 837P transactions, and NEA attachments SHALL route only to `attachment_only` or attachment-supporting clearinghouses (per `supportsAttachments` flag, D2 P.25.6).

### [4]

When `true` AND the M6 estimator confidence is below practice-configured threshold AND the carrier supports real-time adjudication (M2-INS-EXT-004), M6 SHALL invoke real-time adjudication during treatment-plan presentation rather than relying on plan-rules-engine estimation alone.
source: DPIM D2 P.25.7; cross-module M2-INS-EXT-004 + M6 estimator

### [5]
reqId: M2-CLR-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute clearinghouse reliability intelligence (Computed Intelligence Pattern, D2 §2): `submissionSuccessRate` (decimal 0.0–1.0, from claim acceptance/rejection history) and `averageProcessingHours` (decimal, submission → ack response time). Below the practice-configured threshold (default 0.95 success), an alert SHALL surface to the billing team per D2 P.25.8 Agentic Notes.
source: DPIM D2 P.25.8, P.25.9 + Agentic Notes; cross-module M11 + M6
phase: P2

### [6]
reqId: M2-CLR-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **automatic failover** from primary clearinghouse to failover clearinghouse when: (a) `submissionSuccessRate` drops below failover-trigger threshold (default 0.80) over a 24-hour window; OR (b) submission endpoint health check fails 3 consecutive times within 5 minutes; OR (c) operator manually invokes failover via admin action. Failover SHALL emit `dpim.org.clearinghouse.failover_triggered` event and SHALL be `full-delta` audited. Recovery to primary SHALL require manual operator confirmation.
source: HTML m2 §2.10 ("primary/failover designation per transaction, health monitoring"); cross-module PS-1 Action Gateway + PS-7 WORM
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Failover automation absent; Stedi as failover noted but not wired

## 2.11
id: 2.11
name: Tenancy & Data Isolation
purpose: The **architectural foundation** that every other sub-module inherits — `practice_id` is the SQL Row-Level Security anchor on every module-owned table, and `tenant_id` is the corresponding Redis key-prefix anchor (`t:{tenant_id}:`) and APIM rate-limit dimension. Owns multi-tenant lifecycle (DSO onboarding, Practice onboarding, Practice offboarding, suspension), cross-tenant access policy (admin-only with WORM audit), data residency, BAA tracking, breach incident management, Cures Act data-export-request fulfillment, and the no-SAS-tokens / managed-identity-only / no-raw-PHI-in-Redis HIPAA control baseline. Per scope-confirmation #2, **DSO + Practice onboarding/offboarding workflows live here** — M2.1 + M2.2 own the entity records; M2.11 owns the lifecycle workflow.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Tenant` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_TenantOnboarding` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) - `DPIM_TenantOffboarding` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) - `DPIM_TenantBilling` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) - `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) - `DPIM_BreachIncident` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) - `DPIM_DataExportRequest` (v3-new, property-doc-pending — Cures Act + HIPAA right-of-access) - `DPIM_RateLimitPolicy` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) - `DPIM_Integration` + `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) **Domain ownership note:** All Tenancy classes live in **Governance domain**, not Organization domain. M2.11 **operates on** these classes (creates/updates them as part of M2 entity lifecycle) but does not **own** them. Per Step 2 boundary call: "M2 reqs will say *consumes from Governance domain* not *owns*."
externalIntegrations: - **Microsoft Entra ID** — JIT PIM (Privileged Identity Management), break-glass account - **Azure Key Vault** (IF-4) — credential storage, RBAC mode - **Azure APIM** — per-tenant rate-limit policy - **Azure Service Bus** + **Azure Web PubSub** — tenant-scoped subscriptions/hubs - **Azure SQL Database** — RLS function + per-table policies - **Microsoft / Azure BAA** — Business Associate Agreement (signed at subscription level)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Tenant` records (Governance domain v3-new) one-per-Practice as the tenant lifecycle anchor. Tenant lifecycle: `created` → `onboarding` → `active` → `suspended` → `offboarding` → `terminated`. State transitions SHALL emit `dpim.governance.tenant.*` events per v3 §6.2 taxonomy. **`DPIM_Tenant` is consumed-from-Governance, not owned-by-M2** — Governance domain owns the class definition.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Tenant v3-new, Governance domain); HTML m2 §2.11; v3-LOCKED §6.2
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Governance class v3-new; property doc pending; tenant table absent

### [1]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **DSO + Practice onboarding workflow** via `DPIM_TenantOnboarding` (Governance v3-new). Workflow SHALL execute these steps in order, atomically: (1) Practice entity created (M2-PRAC-EXT-001) with `status=onboarding`; (2) RLS policy attached to every module-owned table for the new `practice_id`; (3) Azure Key Vault per-tenant secrets seeded; (4) Service Bus subscription filters generated from manifests via PS-9; (5) Azure APIM rate-limit policy applied for the tenant; (6) BAA recorded in `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement`; (7) default seed data loaded (default `DPIM_PracticeSettings` per M2-SET-EXT-001, default fee schedule, default operatory if any); (8) `OnboardingChecklist` events recorded per item; (9) Practice `status` transitions to `active` via M2-PRAC-EXT-011 gate. Failures SHALL roll back atomically. *Per scope-confirmation #2, this workflow is the M2.11 home.*
source: HTML m2 §2.11 ("tenant onboarding/offboarding workflow"); v3-LOCKED §7.2 (`POST .../governance/tenants/{id}/onboard`); HIPAA Verification Checklist (BAA, KV, RLS, APIM); cross-module M2.1 + M2.2 + IF-4 + PS-9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Onboarding workflow scattered across manual scripts; not orchestrated

### [2]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **tenant offboarding workflow** via `DPIM_TenantOffboarding` (Governance v3-new). Workflow SHALL execute: (1) tenant transitions to `offboarding` (read-only, billing paused); (2) Cures Act `DPIM_DataExportRequest` SHALL be fulfilled (full-fidelity patient-record export per 21st Century Cures Act §4002 / 45 CFR 164.524) within state-required timeline (default 30 days); (3) state-mandated retention period observed (typically 7–10 years post-last-encounter for adults; minor + age-of-majority + 7–10 years per M8 Documents NO-DATA research domain); (4) destruction invoked via `DPIM_DocumentDestructionRecord` (Document domain v3-new) with destruction certificate per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.530(c); (5) RLS policies removed; (6) tenant `status=terminated` final state.
source: HTML m2 §2.11 ("tenant offboarding workflow"); 21st Century Cures Act §4002; 45 CFR 164.524 + 164.530(c); v3-LOCKED §7.2 (suspend action); cross-module M8 NO-DATA + Governance domain
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Offboarding workflow + retention policy plumbing absent; M8 retention rules NO-DATA

### [3]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **tenant suspension** as a non-destructive lifecycle state (`status=suspended`). Suspended tenants SHALL: (a) retain all data (no deletion, no offboarding triggers); (b) reject all `ctx.governed_write` and `ctx.admin_write` SDK calls with verdict `BLOCK`; (c) allow `ctx.read` only for super-admin role with WORM-audited cross-tenant access; (d) pause `DPIM_TenantBilling` accrual; (e) transition back to `active` only via super-admin action.
source: v3-LOCKED §7.2 (`POST .../governance/tenants/{id}/suspend`); cross-module SDK Layer 3 + Governance
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Suspension semantics undefined in SDK

### [4]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **cross-tenant access** as a privileged operation: only `super_admin` role MAY query data across `practice_id` boundaries; cross-tenant queries SHALL emit `dpim.governance.cross_tenant_access.invoked` events; every cross-tenant access SHALL be WORM-audited (PS-7) with operator identity, queried tenants, query scope, and justification text. Cross-DSO patient sharing rules (M2-DSO-EXT-007) override this for explicit DSO-scoped sharing. **Cross-tenant access without WORM audit entry SHALL be a `DPIM_BreachIncident`.**
source: HTML m2 §2.11 ("cross-tenant access (admin role only) audited"); HIPAA Verification Checklist; cross-module PS-7 + Governance domain BreachIncident
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cross-tenant query path not bounded; super_admin role not seeded

### [5]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply **per-tenant rate limiting** via Azure APIM `DPIM_RateLimitPolicy` (Governance v3-new). Rate-limit dimension SHALL be `tenant_id`; per-tier defaults SHALL be configurable (Phase 1 default: 1000 req/min/tenant for governed_write; 5000 req/min/tenant for read). Rate-limit-by-key element SHALL be present in APIM policy XML per HIPAA Verification Checklist.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist ("Rate limiting per tenant_id configured"); v3-LOCKED Governance §RateLimitPolicy; cross-module IF-5 APIM
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: APIM policy applied (`apim-oscar-dev`); rate-limit-by-key element status pending verification

### [6]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **per-tenant Redis key prefixing** via the convention `t:{tenant_id}:` on every cache key. PS-3 Thread Intelligence Practice Snapshot, PS-2 Agent Orchestrator session state, and SDK `_circuit_breaker` keys SHALL all use this prefix. **No raw PHI values SHALL appear in Redis** per HIPAA Verification Checklist (★) — keys may include tokenized identifiers but never patient names, DOB, or medical content.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist ("★ No raw PHI values — spot check Redis keys", "All keys follow t:{tenant_id}: prefix"); cross-module PS-2 + PS-3 + Layer 3 SDK
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Convention documented; spot-check evidence in Phase 0 status §2; enforcement is per-service

### [7]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL prohibit **SAS tokens** in any service environment variable, code path, or runtime configuration — managed identity SHALL be the sole Azure-resource access mechanism per HIPAA Verification Checklist (★). Violation SHALL be a `DPIM_BreachIncident` (Governance v3-new) and SHALL surface to the security team within 1 hour. CI gate SHALL scan service definitions for SAS-token URL patterns at build time.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist ("★ No SAS tokens in use — managed identity access only"); cross-module CI Gate 1 + Governance BreachIncident
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Phase 0 audit found "No SAS token references"; CI scan rule absent

### [8]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement` (Governance v3-new) records: (a) **Microsoft / Azure BAA** for the subscription (one BAA covers all Azure services); (b) **per-vendor BAAs** for HIPAA-impacting external services (Vyne, Stedi, payment processor, geocoding vendor, etc.); (c) **per-DSO BAA** if DSOs are themselves business associates. BAA records SHALL include execution date, signatory, expiration, scope, and signed-document handle to M8 Documents. Tenant `onboarding → active` transition (EXT-002) SHALL be gated by BAA presence.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist ("★ Azure BAA signed for the subscription"); HIPAA 45 CFR 164.504(e); cross-module M8 + Governance domain
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: BAA tracking system absent; existing BAAs in Document repos but not modeled

### [9]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL configure **data residency** per tenant — by default, all PHI-containing storage (Azure SQL, Redis, WORM Storage, Service Bus) SHALL reside in US Azure regions only. DSO/practice tenants SHALL declare `dataResidencyRegion` (`us_east` / `us_west` / `us_central`); cross-region replication SHALL be disabled for PHI domains. Out-of-region data movement SHALL trigger a `DPIM_BreachIncident`.
source: HTML m2 §2.11 ("data residency"); HIPAA Verification Checklist; cross-module IF-2 + IF-6 + Governance BreachIncident
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Region pinning at infrastructure layer; per-tenant declaration absent

### [10]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL create `DPIM_BreachIncident` (Governance v3-new) records for any of: (a) cross-tenant access without WORM audit (EXT-005); (b) PHI in Redis or logs (EXT-007); (c) SAS token detection (EXT-008); (d) cross-region PHI movement (EXT-010); (e) credential leak detection; (f) RLS bypass detection. Incidents SHALL be reported per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.400 Breach Notification within 60 days when threshold is met (>500 affected individuals = HHS + media notification).
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.400 Subpart D; v3-LOCKED §7.2 (`POST .../governance/breach_incidents/{id}/report`); cross-module Governance domain
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Detection signals absent across cited triggers

### [11]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL fulfill `DPIM_DataExportRequest` (Governance v3-new) per 21st Century Cures Act §4002 and HIPAA 45 CFR 164.524 right-of-access — patient-initiated requests SHALL be fulfilled within state-required timeline (default 30 days; HIPAA federal floor is 30 days extendable by 30) in machine-readable format (FHIR R4 bundle preferred per Cures Act). Tenant offboarding (EXT-003) SHALL invoke this flow for the entire tenant patient population.
source: 21st Century Cures Act §4002; HIPAA 45 CFR 164.524; v3-LOCKED §7.2 (`POST .../governance/data_export_requests/{id}/fulfill`); cross-module M1 + M4 + M8 + Governance
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DataExportRequest fulfillment infrastructure absent

### [12]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-013
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **JIT PIM (Just-In-Time Privileged Identity Management)** + **break-glass workflow** for super-admin elevation per HIPAA Verification Checklist Phase 7. Super-admin role SHALL NOT be standing — elevation SHALL require: (a) approver (different operator), (b) time-bounded grant (default 4 hours), (c) justification text, (d) full WORM audit. Break-glass SHALL be a separate "emergency access" path with mandatory post-hoc review within 24 hours.
source: HIPAA Verification Checklist Phase 7 ("JIT PIM, break-glass"); cross-module Microsoft Entra ID + PS-7 WORM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: JIT PIM configuration depends on Entra ID setup; break-glass workflow undefined

### [13]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-014
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_TenantBilling` (Governance v3-new) per-tenant usage and billing accrual: governed_write count, ctx.read count, storage consumed (Azure SQL + Blob WORM + Redis), AI token consumption (`DPIM_TokenConsumption` related), per-channel comm volume (SMS/voice/email). Billing SHALL be paused during tenant `suspended` state (EXT-004) and finalized at offboarding (EXT-003).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (TenantBilling v3-new); cross-module IF-1 (Container Apps usage) + IF-2 (storage) + IF-7 (AI tokens) + M7
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: TenantBilling class v3-new; per-tenant usage metering absent

### [14]
reqId: M2-TEN-EXT-015
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit tenant lifecycle events: `dpim.governance.tenant.created` / `.onboarding_started` / `.onboarded` / `.suspended` / `.offboarding_started` / `.offboarded` / `.terminated`; `dpim.governance.business_associate_agreement.created` / `.updated` / `.expired`; `dpim.governance.breach_incident.flagged` / `.reported` / `.resolved`; `dpim.governance.data_export_request.created` / `.fulfilled`; `dpim.governance.cross_tenant_access.invoked`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — tenant-lifecycle events carry no PHI; data-export events carry tokenized patient identifiers only (full PHI export is the **fulfilled** action's response, not the event payload). All Tenancy events SHALL be `full-delta` audited via PS-7 WORM.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 (`dpim.governance.tenant.onboarded` exemplar); DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3); tenant events not yet defined in mapping

## 2.12
id: 2.12
name: Cross-cutting · DPIM Property Propagation
purpose: Requirements that span M2's relationship to the DPIM v3 LOCKED model — DPIM Gate (CI Gate 1) compliance for M2-owned manifest changes, property metadata propagation across the 16 Org-domain classes, ODIN category alignment, event envelope compliance, and the meta-requirement codifying the v3-new property-doc gap (Finding #4 trigger). These reqs apply uniformly across all 11 sub-modules.
dpimClasses: `DPIM_Organization` (abstract), `DPIM_DSO`, `DPIM_PracticeValuation`, `DPIM_Practice`, `DPIM_PracticeLocation`, `DPIM_Operatory`, `DPIM_PracticeSettings`, `DPIM_BankAccount` (v3-new), `DPIM_MerchantConfig` (v3-new), `DPIM_TaxJurisdiction` (v3-new), `DPIM_InsuranceCompany`, `DPIM_InsurancePlan`, `DPIM_PayerContract` (v3-new), `DPIM_DentalLab`, `DPIM_Supplier`, `DPIM_ReferralPartner`, `DPIM_HospitalAffiliation` (v3-new — out of scope for M2; M10 owns), `DPIM_Pharmacy` (v3-new — M4 prescription cross-module), `DPIM_Clearinghouse`.
externalIntegrations: None directly. Internal: DPIM Validator tool (`02 - Architecture & Design/DPIM Model/tools/dpim-validator/`), DPIM Gate CI workflow (`manifest-validate.yml`).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-DPIM-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR M2 manifests SHALL pass the **DPIM Gate (CI Gate 1)** on every PR per DPIM v3 §12. The gate SHALL validate that every M2-owned class declared in `manifest.yaml` reads/writes/emits is present in `dpim-v3-registry.json`; references to undeclared classes SHALL fail the gate. The 11/11 DPIM Validator test suite SHALL remain green for any M2 manifest mutation.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §12 (DPIM Gate); CI Gate 1 (`manifest-validate.yml`); DPIM Validator (`02 - Architecture & Design/DPIM Model/tools/dpim-validator/`)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Validator passes on existing manifests; no M2-owned manifests yet exist on main

### [1]
reqId: M2-DPIM-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply DPIM property metadata uniformly across all 16 Org-domain classes per DPIM v3 §2.1 schema: every property SHALL carry `mutability`, `sensitivity`, `agenticAccess`, `eventTrigger`, `terminology` (where applicable), `searchable`, `mvlField`, `auditLevel`, `odinCategory`, `description`, plus the v3-new optional `ciKind`. Properties without complete metadata SHALL fail DPIM Gate validation.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §2.1 Property Metadata Schema; DPIM v3 §2.2 Class Metadata; cross-module DPIM Validator
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 D2 properties complete; v3-new classes (BankAccount, MerchantConfig, etc.) lack property metadata

### [2]
reqId: M2-DPIM-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL align M2 Org-domain classes to ODIN data category per DPIM v3 §3 — primary `business_ops` for entity-management classes (Practice, Location, Operatory, Lab, Supplier, Referral, Tenancy) and `payment` for financial classes (InsuranceCompany, InsurancePlan, PayerContract, BankAccount, MerchantConfig, Clearinghouse). Multi-category classes (e.g., `DPIM_PracticeValuation` is `business_ops + payment`) SHALL carry both. ODIN category drives event envelope `metadata.odinCategory`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §3 ODIN Data Category Alignment; ADA Standard No. 1111 (ODIN); DPIM v3 §6.1 event envelope
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: ODIN categorization in property docs; envelope propagation absent

### [3]
reqId: M2-DPIM-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit M2 events on the standard envelope per DPIM v3 §6.1 with all required fields: `eventId` (UUID v7), `eventType` (`dpim.org.{class_snake_case}.{action}`), `timestamp` (ISO-8601), `tenantId`, `actorId`, `personaId`, `correlationId`, `payload.resourceType`, `payload.resourceId`, `payload.action`, `payload.changedProperties`, `payload.delta`, `payload.governanceVerdict`, `metadata.odinCategory`, `metadata.sensitivity`, `metadata.domain`, `metadata.ciKind`. Events with missing required fields SHALL be rejected at the SDK validation gate.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §6.1 Event Envelope; cross-module Layer 3 SDK
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Envelope schema documented; SDK enforcement partial

### [4]
reqId: M2-DPIM-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL ensure that every M2 entity carrying any `internal` or `financial` sensitivity property (e.g., `DPIM_Practice.taxId` financial; `DPIM_PracticeValuation.totalActivePatients` internal) **NEVER emits the property value verbatim across the SDK boundary** — events reaching subscribers outside the originating tenant SHALL carry tokenized identifiers or aggregate-only fields. PHI is not present on M2 entities, but `internal`/`financial` sensitivity SHALL be respected with the same boundary discipline as PHI per the Three Lanes / Three Channel Rule.
source: DPIM v3 §2.4 Sensitivity; SDK validate_no_phi extended to internal/financial sensitivity; cross-module Layer 3 + PS-9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: SDK validation today rejects PHI; internal/financial sensitivity rejection absent

### [5]
reqId: M2-DPIM-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL register **all 11 Org-domain v3-new classes lacking property docs** as DPIM Gate `partial-coverage` exceptions until D2 v3 update lands. Specifically: `DPIM_BankAccount`, `DPIM_MerchantConfig`, `DPIM_TaxJurisdiction`, `DPIM_PayerContract`, `DPIM_HospitalAffiliation`, `DPIM_Pharmacy`, `DPIM_Tenant`, `DPIM_TenantOnboarding`, `DPIM_TenantOffboarding`, `DPIM_TenantBilling`, `DPIM_NPI` (and the related Governance classes consumed by M2: `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement`, `DPIM_BreachIncident`, `DPIM_DataExportRequest`, `DPIM_RateLimitPolicy`, `DPIM_Integration`, `DPIM_IntegrationCredential`, `DPIM_MalpracticeInsurance`). DPIM Gate SHALL emit `dpim_gate_partial_coverage_warning` (non-blocking) until property docs land. **This requirement is the codification of Finding #4.**
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §12 DPIM Gate; D2 v2.0.0 vs v3 LOCKED gap (Finding #4); v3 §13.6 Property-level work post-lock
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM Gate exception/partial-coverage path absent

## 2.13
id: 2.13
name: Cross-cutting · External Integrations (INTG)
purpose: Cross-sub-module external-integration requirements — NPI Registry validation (consumed by M2.2 + M2.9), clearinghouse routing infrastructure (M2.6 + M2.10), supplier punch-out catalogs (M2.8), lab platforms (M2.7), geocoding (M2.3 + M2.9), payment processors (M2.5), and the DPIM_Integration governance lifecycle that wraps all of them.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Integration` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) — registry of all external integrations - `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) — Key Vault-backed credential references
externalIntegrations: NPI Registry / CMS NPPES (Type 1 + Type 2 validation); Vyne Dental (primary clearinghouse — X12 270/271, 277CA, 837D, 835, NEA attachments); Stedi (failover clearinghouse); supplier punch-out catalogs (Henry Schein One · Patterson · Benco — cXML 1.2 / OCI 4.0; Net32 REST); dental lab platforms (DDX, LabStar, optional Magic Touch / Evident); Azure Maps (HIPAA-BAA geocoding); Azure Key Vault (credential custody for all integrations).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M2-INTG-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain a `DPIM_Integration` registry (Governance v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) listing every external dependency: clearinghouses (Vyne, Stedi), supplier catalogs (Henry Schein, Patterson, Benco, Net32), lab platforms (DDX, LabStar), NPI Registry (NPPES), payment processors (TBD), geocoding (Azure Maps recommended), and any future vendor. Each integration SHALL have: vendor name, integration type, protocol, BAA status, `isActive`, last-health-check timestamp. **DPIM_Integration replaces ad-hoc integration tracking** and is the single source of truth for "what external systems does OSCAR depend on?"
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Integration v3-new); v3 §8 Standards Compliance Matrix; cross-module Governance domain
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Class is v3-new property-doc-pending; registry table absent

### [1]
reqId: M2-INTG-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL validate **NPI** (National Provider Identifier) against CMS NPPES at multiple points: (a) Type 2 organizational NPI on `DPIM_Practice.npiOrg` (M2-PRAC-EXT-005); (b) Type 2 NPI on `DPIM_PracticeLocation` for location-specific NPI (M2-LOC-EXT-003); (c) Type 1 individual NPI on `DPIM_ReferralPartner` (M2-REF-EXT-004); (d) Type 1 NPI on `DPIM_Practitioner` (M10 cross-module). Validation SHALL be: format (10-digit Luhn checksum) at write time; OPTIONAL live registry validation when `referralNpiLiveValidation` feature flag enabled (rate-limited per NPPES quotas).
source: CMS NPPES; X12 837D loop 2010AA; cross-module M2.2 + M2.3 + M2.9 + M10
phase: P1

Health-check SHALL run every 15 minutes; degraded health SHALL trigger Stedi failover per M2-CLR-EXT-007.

Digital workflow SHALL be preferred when both lab and patient case support digital impressions (cross-module M5 IntraOralScan + M9 LabCase).

M2.10 clearinghouse failover (M2-CLR-EXT-007) and M2.11 breach detection (M2-TEN-EXT-011) consume these events for automated response.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy (`dpim.governance.integration.*` extended); cross-module M2.10 + M2.11 + Governance

## m5
moduleId: m5

## subModules

## 5.1
id: 5.1
name: Sensor / Capture Bridge
purpose: Direct integration layer between operatory-mounted intraoral sensors and OSCAR's DICOM hierarchy. Owns vendor-bridge software, capture-protocol negotiation (TWAIN, DICOM-native, vendor SDK), multi-sensor-per-operatory routing, and the immediate `DPIM_ImagingSession` lifecycle from sensor activation to DICOM instance write. Per HTML m5 §5.1, OSCAR supports 8 named sensor vendors — the integration layer SHALL be vendor-pluggable, not hard-coded per vendor.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_SensorBridge` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_ImagingSession` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — capture output writes directly to DICOM hierarchy - Cross-module: `DPIM_Operatory` (M2.4) for sensor-to-operatory binding; `DPIM_Device` (M9) for sensor-as-equipment registration
externalIntegrations: - **TWAIN** — legacy sensor protocol (Dexis older lines, Schick CDR, Carestream legacy) - **DICOM-native** — modern sensors emitting DICOM SR / Modality Performed Procedure Step - **Vendor SDKs:** Dexis · Carestream · Schick · MyRay · Owandy · Dürr · Vatech · Planmeca - **DICOM Modality Worklist (DMWL)** — pre-capture worklist push from M3 scheduling

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_SensorBridge` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per sensor — UUID v7 record id, vendor (`dexis` / `carestream` / `schick` / `myray` / `owandy` / `dürr` / `vatech` / `planmeca` / `other`), `model`, `serialNumber`, `protocol` (`twain` / `dicom_native` / `vendor_sdk`), parent `operatory` (Reference to `DPIM_Operatory`, M2.4), `isActive`, last-calibration-date, last-software-version. Sensor SHALL NOT be writable to clinical workflows when `isActive=false`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (SensorBridge v3-new); HTML m5 §5.1; Module Catalog §6.2.1; cross-module M2-OP-EXT-003
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending; no `imaging` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement a **vendor-pluggable bridge architecture** — a single `SensorBridge` interface SHALL accept capture commands from M5 sub-modules (5.2 PA, 5.3 BWX, 5.4 Pano, 5.6 CBCT, 5.7 Photo) and dispatch to the configured vendor SDK / TWAIN bridge / DICOM-native receiver. New vendor support SHALL be addable without M5 sub-module changes. Vendor capability matrix SHALL be a BackboneElement: `{vendor, supportsDicomNative, supportsTwain, supportsDmwl, supportsRealTimeStreaming, captureProtocol}`.
source: HTML m5 §5.1 (8-vendor matrix); v3 LOCKED §1 architectural principle (vendor-agnostic)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Bridge architecture not yet designed

### [2]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **TWAIN protocol** for legacy sensors (per HTML m5 §5.1). TWAIN bridge SHALL run as a per-workstation Windows service (TWAIN is workstation-bound by design); captured images SHALL be uploaded to OSCAR's DICOM tier within 30 seconds of capture. TWAIN-captured images SHALL be DICOM-wrapped on upload (synthetic DICOM Study/Series IDs generated server-side).
source: HTML m5 §5.1; TWAIN Working Group standards; DICOM PS3.10 file format
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: TWAIN bridge service absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **DICOM-native sensors** with DICOM Storage SCP receivers in OSCAR's DICOM tier. Sensors SHALL push DICOM instances to OSCAR via configured AE Title (Application Entity Title); OSCAR SHALL accept DICOM C-STORE requests and write to `DPIM_DICOMInstance`. AE Title configuration per `DPIM_SensorBridge` SHALL be `agentic-access: blocked` (infrastructure config).
source: DICOM PS3.4 (Service Classes); DICOM PS3.7 (Message Exchange); HTML m5 §5.1
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM C-STORE receiver absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **DICOM Modality Worklist (DMWL)** push from M3 scheduling — when a procedure with `requiresImaging=true` (cross-module M3.1) is checked-in (M3.15), OSCAR SHALL push a DMWL entry to all sensors bound to the operatory. DMWL entry SHALL contain: patient identifier (tokenized per PHI boundary), accession number, scheduled procedure code, requesting physician, modality. **DMWL push SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient name SHALL appear as masked initials only.
source: DICOM PS3.4 (Modality Worklist Service Class); HTML m5 §5.1 + §5.8; cross-module M3.1 + M3.15
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: DMWL absent; PHI-tokenization for DICOM tags requires additional spec

### [5]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **multi-sensor environments per operatory** — a single `DPIM_Operatory` (M2.4) MAY have multiple `DPIM_SensorBridge` instances (e.g., a PA sensor + a BWX sensor + a pano hookup). Capture commands from sub-modules SHALL select the appropriate sensor by capture-type → sensor-capability match. Operatories with no matching sensor for a requested capture-type SHALL surface a "no compatible sensor" error rather than silently falling back.
source: HTML m5 §5.1 ("Multi-sensor environments per operatory"); cross-module M2.4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-sensor routing logic absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ImagingSession` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records as the per-encounter capture-session anchor — UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `operatory` Reference (M2.4), `practitioner` Reference (M10), `encounter` Reference (M4 when extracted), `startedAt` / `completedAt` timestamps, `status` (`in_progress` / `completed` / `aborted`), and `dicomStudy` Reference (DICOM Study UID — composed on session completion). All radiograph captures within a session SHALL share the session's `dicomStudy`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ImagingSession v3-new); HTML m5 §5.1 + §5.8; DICOM PS3.3 Information Object Definitions (Study)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [7]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track **sensor calibration** per `DPIM_SensorBridge.lastCalibrationDate` with cadence enforcement per state radiation-safety regulations (typically annual; per CA Title 17, NY 16 NYCRR Part 89, TX 25 TAC §289). Overdue calibration SHALL flag the sensor `isActive=false` and prevent capture. Calibration events SHALL emit `dpim.imaging.sensor_bridge.calibrated` and SHALL be tracked via cross-module M9 Equipment maintenance workflow (`DPIM_CalibrationRecord` v3-new).
source: State radiation safety regulations (CA / NY / TX representative); NCRP Report 145 (dental radiation safety); cross-module M9 + M2.4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Calibration tracking absent; M9 not yet extracted

### [8]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit sensor-capture lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.imaging_session.started` / `.completed` / `.aborted`; `dpim.imaging.dicom_instance.received` (per individual capture); `dpim.imaging.sensor_bridge.connected` / `.disconnected` / `.calibrated`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifiers are tokenized in event payload; full PHI access requires `ctx.read` against tokenized references.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy (`dpim.imaging.dicom_study.received` exemplar); SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

### [9]
reqId: M5-SENS-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL restrict `DPIM_SensorBridge` mutations to `office_manager` and `owner` roles per OPA — clinical staff SHALL NOT modify sensor configuration. Sensor calibration **records** (M5-SENS-EXT-008) MAY be created by `dental_assistant` or `hygienist` roles per DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix. Sensor `serialNumber` mutation SHALL be `full-delta` audit (regulatory traceability).
source: DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; HIPAA Verification Checklist (RBAC); state radiation safety record-keeping
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Sensor RBAC matrix not seeded

id: OQ-5.1.1
text: TWAIN bridge architecture (per-workstation Windows service vs cloud-side TWAIN-proxy gateway). Recommend hybrid: per-workstation for capture; cloud-routed for upload + audit. Defer to Phase 1 implementation.

## 5.2
id: 5.2
name: Periapical Radiograph (PA)
purpose: Per-tooth or per-region X-ray capture, the most common dental radiograph type. Each PA captures a single tooth or small region (2–3 adjacent teeth) at full resolution. Per HTML m5 §5.2, PA captures support annotation overlay and AI caries / pathology detection (Pearl AI integration via M5.9). PAs MAY be captured individually or as part of a `DPIM_PASeries` (multiple PAs in a session — e.g., a 4-PA periapical survey).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — was Clinical v2, now under Imaging top-level domain in v3, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_PASeries` (v3-new abstract subclass of `DPIM_RadiographSeries`, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (v3-new) — underlying DICOM storage - `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new, AI provenance first-class) — produced by Pearl AI overlay - Cross-module: `DPIM_DentalFinding` (M4 Clinical) — AI-derived findings link back to PAs
externalIntegrations: - **Pearl AI** — caries detection, periapical pathology detection (Phase 3) - **DICOM standards** — DICOM Image SOP Class (Digital Intra-Oral X-Ray Image Storage, UID `1.2.840.10008.5.1.4.1.1.1.3`)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per PA capture — UUID v7, `imagingSession` Reference (M5.1), `dicomInstance` Reference, `anatomicReference` BackboneElement (`{toothNumber: int, region: code}` per Universal Numbering System or FDI ISO 3950), `captureDateTime`, `kVp` (radiation parameter), `mA`, `exposureTimeMs`, `practitioner` Reference (M10), and `imageView` (`paralleling_technique` / `bisecting_angle` / `interproximal_periapical`).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (PeriapicalRadiograph); DICOM Digital Intra-Oral X-Ray Image Storage SOP Class; HTML m5 §5.2
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [1]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support per-tooth (single tooth) and per-region (2–3 adjacent teeth) PA capture modes per HTML m5 §5.2. Capture mode SHALL drive `anatomicReference` slicing — single-tooth PAs declare one tooth; per-region PAs declare a tooth-range. The clinical workflow (M4) SHALL link findings to specific tooth references within the captured image.
source: HTML m5 §5.2 ("Per-tooth or per-region capture"); cross-module M4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Workflow absent

### [2]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support `DPIM_PASeries` grouping for multi-PA captures within a single session — when a periapical survey (e.g., 4-PA endo workup) is taken, OSCAR SHALL group the captures under a single `PASeries` with consistent `seriesDate`, `seriesDescription`, and ordered position metadata. PASeries SHALL inherit `DPIM_RadiographSeries` abstract per v3 §4.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (PASeries v3-new); DICOM PS3.3 Series IOD; clinical workflow conventions
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new; not modeled

### [3]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **Pearl AI caries detection** on every PA capture (Phase 3) when `DPIM_PracticeSettings.aiFeatureToggles.clinicalDecisionSupport=true` (M2-SET-EXT-011). Pearl AI SHALL receive the DICOM image + tokenized patient identifier; response SHALL produce a `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` with detected `DPIM_DentalFinding` candidates (M4) that the clinician confirms or rejects per M5.9 acceptance loop.
source: HTML m5 §5.2 ("AI caries / pathology detection"); HTML m5 §5.9 (Pearl AI integration); cross-module M2-SET-EXT-011 + M4 + M5.9
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Pearl AI integration absent; M4 Findings cross-module gap

### [4]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **Pearl AI periapical pathology detection** on every PA capture (Phase 3) — distinct from caries detection. Pathology findings (periapical lesion, root resorption, cysts, granulomas) SHALL be linked to the relevant tooth reference and surfaced as M4 `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` candidates pending clinician acceptance.
source: HTML m5 §5.2 + §5.9 (Pearl AI pathology detection); cross-module M5.9 + M4
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Pearl AI pathology endpoint absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **annotation overlay** on PA captures via M5.10 (Image Annotation & Markup) — clinicians MAY add measurement, markup, and freehand annotations stored as `DPIM_ImageAnnotation` (v3-new) referenced by the PA. Annotations SHALL be versioned (clinician edit history) and SHALL be exportable with the underlying image for referral or claim attachment.
source: HTML m5 §5.2 ("annotation overlay"); cross-module M5.10
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M5.10 not yet specified

### [6]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL log radiation-dose-per-PA capture in `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — per-capture mGy / mSv exposure derived from `kVp`/`mA`/`exposureTimeMs` per NCRP Report 145 dental radiation safety guidance. Cumulative patient dose SHALL be queryable for radiation-safety reporting (cross-module M5.11 Compliance).
source: NCRP Report 145; state radiation safety boards; DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (RadiationDoseLog v3-new); cross-module M5.11
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: RadiationDoseLog property doc pending

### [7]
reqId: M5-PA-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit PA-capture lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.periapical_radiograph.captured` / `.annotated` / `.shared`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — DICOM image references are tokenized; AI inference findings are tokenized at the M5.9 boundary. Subscribers (M4 Clinical findings, M5.9 AI pipeline, M5.11 compliance dose tracker) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## openQuestions

### [0]
id: OQ-5.2.1
text: `DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph` was a Clinical v2 class; per v3 §4 it now lives under Imaging top-level domain. Recommend PS-11 Migration Framework specify v2-to-v3 PA class re-mapping for existing v2 PA records during PMS migration.

### [1]
id: OQ-5.2.2
text: `anatomicReference` tooth-numbering — Universal Numbering (1–32 US standard) vs FDI ISO 3950 (international standard). Recommend dual-encoding with practice-locale default.

### [2]
id: OQ-5.2.3
text: **RESOLVED in Step 4 / Batch 6 — see M5-AI-EXT-010.** Pearl AI invocation cadence: AI inference SHALL be invoked **asynchronously** — clinical capture SHALL NOT block on AI response. Results delivered via WebSocket push (PS-5 Delivery) after AI processing completes.

## 5.3
id: 5.3
name: Bitewing Radiograph (BWX)
purpose: Posterior bitewing X-ray capture for interproximal caries detection and existing-restoration verification. Distinct from PAs (M5.2) in capture technique and clinical purpose — bitewings show the crowns of upper and lower posterior teeth in a single image. Per HTML m5 §5.3, OSCAR supports `2 / 4 / 7` BWX series variants in `vertical / horizontal` orientations, with cadence reminders and Pearl AI interproximal caries detection. The 4-BWX adult series is the most common dental radiograph configuration.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_BitewingRadiograph` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — was Clinical v2, now Imaging top-level v3, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_BWXSeries` (v3-new abstract subclass of `DPIM_RadiographSeries`, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (v3-new) — underlying DICOM storage - `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new, AI provenance) - Cross-module: `DPIM_DentalCondition` (M4 Clinical) — interproximal caries findings; `DPIM_RecallSchedule` (M3) — BWX cadence drives recall
externalIntegrations: - **Pearl AI** — interproximal caries detection (Phase 3) - **DICOM standards** — DICOM Digital Intra-Oral X-Ray Image Storage SOP Class

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_BitewingRadiograph` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per BWX capture — UUID v7, `imagingSession` Reference (M5.1), `dicomInstance` Reference, `anatomicReference` BackboneElement (`{quadrant: code, toothRange: int[]}` for the visible posterior teeth), `orientation` (`horizontal` / `vertical`), `kVp`, `mA`, `exposureTimeMs`, `practitioner` Reference (M10), and `captureDateTime`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (BitewingRadiograph); DICOM Digital Intra-Oral X-Ray Image Storage SOP Class; HTML m5 §5.3
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [1]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **BWX series variants**: `2_bwx` (pediatric, posterior only) / `4_bwx` (adult standard) / `7_bwx` (vertical perio survey, 7 vertically-oriented films covering full posterior dentition). Series type SHALL be selectable at capture initiation; `DPIM_BWXSeries.seriesType` SHALL constrain expected film count per series. Mid-series interruption SHALL surface a "series incomplete" alert.
source: HTML m5 §5.3 ("2 / 4 / 7 BWX series"); ADA practice guidelines; clinical workflow conventions
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Series-variant logic absent

### [2]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **horizontal vs vertical orientation** per BWX capture (HTML m5 §5.3). Vertical orientation SHALL be required for `7_bwx` perio series; horizontal SHALL be default for `2_bwx` / `4_bwx`. Orientation SHALL be captured in `DPIM_BitewingRadiograph.orientation` and SHALL drive AI-overlay rendering (interproximal regions differ by orientation).
source: HTML m5 §5.3; clinical workflow conventions
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Orientation handling absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_BWXSeries` (v3-new) records grouping multi-BWX captures within a single session — `seriesType` (`2_bwx` / `4_bwx` / `7_bwx`), ordered position metadata (e.g., right-posterior / left-posterior), shared `seriesDescription`, and series-level `DICOM Series UID`. All BWXs in a series SHALL share the same `seriesDate` and `dicomStudy`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (BWXSeries v3-new); DICOM PS3.3 Series IOD
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; not modeled

### [4]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **Pearl AI interproximal caries detection** on every BWX capture (Phase 3) when `aiFeatureToggles.clinicalDecisionSupport=true` (M2-SET-EXT-011). Pearl AI SHALL return per-tooth interproximal-surface caries detections with confidence scores; OSCAR SHALL render the AI overlay on the BWX viewer per M5.9 and create `DPIM_DentalCondition` candidates (M4 cross-module) pending clinician acceptance. **Interproximal caries detection is BWX's primary AI value** (vs PA pathology focus) per HTML m5 §5.3.
source: HTML m5 §5.3 ("AI interproximal caries detection"); cross-module M5.9 + M4 + M2-SET-EXT-011
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Pearl AI integration absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **BWX cadence reminders** per patient caries risk (cross-module M1.5 Patient Risk Profile): low-risk adults → 24–36 month BWX interval; moderate-risk → 12–24 month; high-risk → 6–12 month. Overdue BWX SHALL surface in M11 `DPIM_ActionCard` ("Mr. Smith due for BWX series"). Cadence rules SHALL be practice-configurable via `DPIM_PracticeSettings.imagingCadenceRules` (proposed D2 v3 addition).
source: HTML m5 §5.3 ("cadence reminders"); ADA Selection Criteria for Dental Radiographs (2012); cross-module M1.5 + M11
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Cadence rule engine absent; `imagingCadenceRules` property absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL log radiation dose per BWX capture in `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` (v3-new) per NCRP Report 145. Cumulative patient dose SHALL include all imaging modalities (PA + BWX + Pano + CBCT + IntraOral) for radiation-safety reporting. Multi-image BWX series (7-BWX) SHALL roll up to a single cumulative-dose entry per session.
source: NCRP Report 145; state radiation safety boards; DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (RadiationDoseLog v3-new); cross-module M5.11
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: RadiationDoseLog property doc pending

### [7]
reqId: M5-BWX-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit BWX lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.bitewing_radiograph.captured` / `.annotated`; `dpim.imaging.bwx_series.completed`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — DICOM image references are tokenized; AI inference findings are tokenized at the M5.9 boundary. Subscribers (M4 Conditions per HTML m5 §5.3 integ note, M5.9 AI, M5.11 cadence tracker, M11 ActionCard) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 5.4
id: 5.4
name: Panoramic Radiograph
purpose: Single-image, full-mouth panoramic X-ray showing all teeth, jawbones, TMJ, maxillary sinuses, and surrounding anatomy in one view. Lower resolution than PA but covers far more anatomy. Per HTML m5 §5.4, OSCAR supports the base pano capture plus TMJ views, maxillary sinus views, and cephalometric companion captures (for orthodontic workflows). Pearl AI detection focuses on third-molar pathology (impactions, cysts, follicular abnormalities) and broader maxillofacial pathology. Distinct from CBCT (M5.6) which produces 3D volumes; pano is 2D.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_PanoramicRadiograph` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — was Clinical v2, now Imaging top-level v3, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_CephalometricRadiograph` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — companion lateral cephalometric capture for orthodontic workflows - `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (v3-new) - `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new) — produced by Pearl AI third-molar / pathology detection - Cross-module: `DPIM_OrthoCase` (M4 Clinical, v3-new), `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` (M4 v3-new), `DPIM_CephTracing` (M4 v3-new — ortho measurement)
externalIntegrations: - **Pearl AI** — third-molar / pathology detection (Phase 3) - **DICOM standards** — DICOM Panoramic X-Ray Image Storage SOP Class (UID `1.2.840.10008.5.1.4.1.1.1.3.1`)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PanoramicRadiograph` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per panoramic capture — UUID v7, `imagingSession` Reference (M5.1), `dicomInstance` Reference, `panoView` (`standard_full_arch` / `tmj_open_closed` / `maxillary_sinus`), `kVp`, `mA`, `exposureTimeMs`, `practitioner` Reference (M10), `captureDateTime`, and `panoramicLayer` (`standard` / `extended_focal_trough`).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (PanoramicRadiograph); DICOM Panoramic X-Ray Image Storage SOP Class; HTML m5 §5.4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [1]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **TMJ open/closed view** capture as a `panoView=tmj_open_closed` variant (HTML m5 §5.4). TMJ views SHALL capture two exposures (patient mouth open + closed) for TMD assessment workflows. Cross-module with `DPIM_TMDAssessment` (M4 v3-new Clinical) — TMJ pano captures SHALL link to the assessment record.
source: HTML m5 §5.4 ("TMJ … views"); cross-module M4 DPIM_TMDAssessment
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 not yet extracted

### [2]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **maxillary sinus view** as a `panoView=maxillary_sinus` variant. Sinus views SHALL be captured per oral-surgery workflow when implant placement is being planned in posterior maxilla (HTML m5 §5.4 sinus reference). Cross-module with `DPIM_ImplantPlan` (M4 v3-new).
source: HTML m5 §5.4 ("sinus views"); cross-module M4 ImplantPlan
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 not yet extracted

### [3]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **cephalometric companion capture** as `DPIM_CephalometricRadiograph` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — lateral cephalometric or PA cephalometric views captured alongside the panoramic in orthodontic workflows. Cephalometric captures SHALL link to `DPIM_OrthoCase` (M4 v3-new) and SHALL feed `DPIM_CephTracing` (M4 v3-new) for orthodontic measurement. Cephalometric capture parameters differ from standard pano (higher kVp typical, lateral view).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (CephalometricRadiograph v3-new); HTML m5 §5.4 ("cephalometric companion (ortho)"); cross-module M4 OrthoCase + CephTracing
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new classes; ortho workflow not extracted

### [4]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **Pearl AI third-molar pathology detection** on every panoramic capture (Phase 3) when `aiFeatureToggles.clinicalDecisionSupport=true`. Pearl AI SHALL detect: impacted third molars (angulation classification — mesial/distal/vertical/horizontal), associated cysts (follicular, dentigerous), root resorption of adjacent teeth, and pathology candidates. Findings SHALL produce `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` with linked `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` (M4) candidates pending clinician acceptance.
source: HTML m5 §5.4 ("AI third-molar / pathology detection"); cross-module M5.9 + M4 RadiologicFinding
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Pearl AI third-molar endpoint absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **broader Pearl AI pathology detection** on every panoramic capture (Phase 3) — bone-level assessment, mandibular nerve canal visualization (for implant planning), bone-density variations indicating osteoporosis / paget disease / malignancy. Findings SHALL be captured as `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` (M4) with severity scoring.
source: HTML m5 §5.4 + §5.9 Pearl AI broader pathology; cross-module M5.9 + M4
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Broader pathology AI absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL log radiation dose per panoramic capture — pano effective dose is higher than PA/BWX (typically 9–24 μSv vs 1–8 μSv for PA per NCRP Report 145). TMJ open/closed counts as two captures; cephalometric companion adds an additional exposure. Cumulative dose SHALL aggregate into `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` per M5-BWX-EXT-007 pattern.
source: NCRP Report 145; ICRP 103; cross-module M5.11 RadiationDoseLog
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Dose tracking absent

### [7]
reqId: M5-PAN-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit panoramic lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.panoramic_radiograph.captured` / `.annotated`; `dpim.imaging.cephalometric_radiograph.captured`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — DICOM image references are tokenized. Subscribers (M4 ortho per HTML m5 §5.4 integ, M5.9 Pearl AI, M5.11 dose tracker, M5.12 sharing) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 5.5
id: 5.5
name: Full Mouth Series (FMX)
purpose: The full-mouth radiographic survey — a templated combination of PAs (M5.2) and BWXs (M5.3) covering all dentition, captured as a single clinical event. Per HTML m5 §5.5, OSCAR supports `18 / 21` PA+BWX templates with mounted FMX layout (positional mapping). FMX cadence is **mandatory per state and per payer** — many state Medicaid programs require FMX every 3–5 years; commercial payers vary. Phase 3 introduces the dedicated FMX Compliance Tracker (separate spec: `OSCAR_Phase3_Clinical_Compliance_FMX_v3.html`) which cross-references with M5.11.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_FMXSeries` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new abstract subclass of `DPIM_RadiographSeries`, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — FMX cadence compliance tracking - Composes `DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph` (M5.2) and `DPIM_BitewingRadiograph` (M5.3) under a single series anchor - Cross-module: `DPIM_RecallSchedule` (M3 v3 SchedulingTemplate); `DPIM_PayerContract` (M2.6) — per-payer cadence rules
externalIntegrations: - State Medicaid programs (cadence requirements vary per state) - Commercial dental payers (per-plan cadence in `DPIM_InsurancePlan` — M2.6) - ADA Selection Criteria for Dental Radiographs (2012 guidance)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_FMXSeries` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per FMX capture — UUID v7, `imagingSession` Reference (M5.1), `template` (`fmx_18` / `fmx_21` / `custom`), composed `periapicalRadiographs[]` (Reference list to M5.2 PAs), composed `bitewingRadiographs[]` (Reference list to M5.3 BWXs), `mountingLayout` JSON (positional template per ADA mounting standards), `seriesDate`, `practitioner` Reference (M10), and series-level `dicomStudy` UID.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (FMXSeries v3-new); HTML m5 §5.5 ("18 / 21 PA + BWX template"); Module Catalog §6.2.5
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [1]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **18-image FMX template** (`fmx_18`) — typically 14 PAs + 4 BWXs covering full dentition (clinical-standard adult FMX). Template SHALL drive capture-workflow prompts: "next image position: right posterior periapical maxillary" etc., guiding the capture sequence in the prescribed order. Mid-series interruption SHALL preserve partial completion with explicit `series_status=incomplete`.
source: HTML m5 §5.5 ("18 … PA + BWX template"); ADA mounting standards; clinical workflow conventions
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Capture-workflow template engine absent

### [2]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **21-image FMX template** (`fmx_21`) — typically 14 PAs + 7 BWXs (vertical perio variant) for periodontal practices. Template selection SHALL respect `DPIM_Practice.practiceType` (M2-PRAC-EXT-004) — periodontic practices default to `fmx_21`; general practices default to `fmx_18`. Custom templates SHALL be configurable per-tenant.
source: HTML m5 §5.5 ("21 PA + BWX template"); cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-004; clinical workflow conventions
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Template variants not modeled

### [3]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL render a **mounted FMX layout** — a 2D grid display of all PAs and BWXs in their anatomic positions (maxillary on top, mandibular on bottom; right side of patient on left of view per dental convention). Mounted layout SHALL be the default FMX viewer presentation; individual-radiograph drill-down SHALL preserve mounting context. Layout JSON SHALL be exportable for referral packets (M5.12).
source: HTML m5 §5.5 ("mounted FMX layout"); ADA mounting standards; cross-module M5.12
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Mounting viewer absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **state-specific FMX cadence rules** via `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord` (v3-new). State Medicaid programs SHALL be the authoritative source for state-level cadence (e.g., California Medi-Cal — 5-year FMX; Texas Medicaid — 3-year FMX). Practice's state SHALL be derived from `DPIM_Practice.address.state` (M2.2). Overdue FMX SHALL surface M11 `DPIM_ActionCard` with state-rule citation.
source: State Medicaid dental policy manuals (per state); cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-001 + M5.11 + M11
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: State-rule library absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **per-payer FMX cadence** via `DPIM_InsurancePlan.frequencyLimitations` (M2-INS-EXT-011) — when CDT code D0210 (FMX) frequency limitation applies to the patient's active coverage, M6 estimator SHALL warn the provider that the FMX would not be reimbursable. Payer-specific rules SHALL override state-Medicaid rules when more restrictive.
source: M2-INS-EXT-011 (frequency limitations); CDT code D0210; cross-module M2.6 + M6 estimator
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Frequency-limitation evaluation absent for FMX

### [6]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface **overdue / due-soon FMX flagging** in M11 ActionCards per Phase 3 FMX Compliance Tracker spec (`OSCAR_Phase3_Clinical_Compliance_FMX_v3.html`). Due-soon threshold SHALL be 60 days before cadence expiration; overdue SHALL be from expiration date. Flagging SHALL respect `aiFeatureToggles.actionCards` master toggle (M2-SET-EXT-011). Per-practice flagging cadence SHALL aggregate to provider-level compliance reports.
source: Phase 3 spec `OSCAR_Phase3_Clinical_Compliance_FMX_v3.html`; HTML m5 §5.11; cross-module M11 + M2.5
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Phase 3 spec exists; cadence engine absent

### [7]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL track **FMX completion gates** — when `DPIM_FMXSeries.status` transitions to `completed`, OSCAR SHALL: (a) emit `dpim.imaging.fmx_series.completed`; (b) create/update `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord` with new `lastFMXDate`; (c) reset cadence countdown per applicable state + payer rules; (d) cumulate radiation dose across all FMX captures into `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog`. Aborted FMXs SHALL NOT reset compliance counters.
source: HTML m5 §5.5 + §5.11; cross-module M5.11 RadiationDoseLog + ImagingComplianceRecord
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Completion-event wiring absent

### [8]
reqId: M5-FMX-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit FMX lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.fmx_series.started` / `.image_captured` / `.completed` / `.aborted`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifiers tokenized; DICOM references tokenized. Subscribers (M5.11 Compliance, M11 ActionCard, M3 RecallSchedule, M6 RCM claim attachment auto-link) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)
