## 5.6
id: 5.6
name: Cone Beam CT (CBCT)
purpose: 3D volumetric imaging — Cone Beam Computed Tomography produces a DICOM volume (hundreds of cross-sectional slices) of the dentomaxillofacial region. Used for implant planning, endodontic case analysis, third-molar surgical planning, airway analysis (sleep apnea), and complex orthodontic workflows. Per HTML m5 §5.6, OSCAR provides DICOM volume storage, slice viewer integration, 3D rendering, implant planning bridges (coDiagnostix and Blue Sky Plan), airway analysis, and AI segmentation. CBCT effective dose is **significantly higher than 2D imaging** (50–500 μSv depending on field-of-view) — radiation safety reporting (M5.11) is critical.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ConeBeamCT` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — was Clinical v2, now Imaging top-level v3, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_DICOMStudy` (v3-new) + `DPIM_DICOMSeries` (v3-new) + `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (v3-new) — CBCT produces hundreds of instances per study - `DPIM_3DDentalModel` (v3-new) — 3D rendering output - `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new) — AI segmentation provenance - Cross-module: `DPIM_ImplantPlan` (M4 v3-new), `DPIM_SurgicalGuide` (M4 v3-new), `DPIM_HSAT` + `DPIM_OralAppliance` (M4 SleepDentistry v3-new)
externalIntegrations: - **coDiagnostix** — implant planning software (DICOM volume import → surgical guide design) - **Blue Sky Plan** — alternate implant planning software - **DICOM CT Image Storage SOP Class** (UID `1.2.840.10008.5.1.4.1.1.2`) - **DICOMDIR** — CD/USB volume export

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ConeBeamCT` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per CBCT volume capture — UUID v7, `imagingSession` Reference (M5.1), `dicomStudy` Reference (linked to hundreds of `DPIM_DICOMInstance` slice records), `fieldOfView` (`small_focal` / `medium_arch` / `large_maxillofacial`), `voxelSize` (decimal, mm), `kVp`, `mA`, `exposureTimeMs`, `acquisitionParameters` (BackboneElement), `practitioner` Reference (M10), `captureDateTime`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ConeBeamCT); DICOM CT Image Storage SOP Class; HTML m5 §5.6; Module Catalog §6.2.6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [1]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **DICOM volume storage** — CBCT studies typically contain 200–600 axial slice instances; OSCAR SHALL store them under a single `DPIM_DICOMStudy` with appropriate `DPIM_DICOMSeries` grouping per orientation (axial / sagittal / coronal reconstructions where applicable). Volume integrity SHALL be validated (slice count + spacing consistency) at storage time. Mid-upload failures SHALL NOT leave partial studies in queryable state.
source: DICOM PS3.3 IOD (CT Image); DICOM PS3.4 (Storage Service Class); HTML m5 §5.6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM volume reception path absent

### [2]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate a **slice viewer** supporting axial / sagittal / coronal multi-planar reconstruction (MPR) per HTML m5 §5.6. Viewer SHALL: (a) load volume from `DPIM_DICOMStudy` reference; (b) render slice-by-slice with windowing controls (window/level for bone vs soft tissue); (c) support synchronized cross-hair across orientations; (d) annotate slices via M5.10. Viewer SHALL run client-side (browser-based) with volume streaming from server.
source: HTML m5 §5.6 ("slice viewer integration"); DICOM MPR conventions
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Slice viewer absent; vendor evaluation deferred

### [3]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL produce **3D volume renderings** of CBCT studies as `DPIM_3DDentalModel` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records — volume-rendering output (surface mesh + opacity transfer function) for visualization in 3D viewer. Renderings SHALL be cached server-side and SHALL be refreshable on rendering-parameter change. Per HTML m5 §5.6 cross-reference, 3D models SHALL be exportable as STL for downstream CAD/CAM (M5 cross-cuts with `DPIM_STLModel` v3-new).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (3DDentalModel + STLModel v3-new); HTML m5 §5.6 ("3D rendering"); cross-module CAD/CAM
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new classes; rendering pipeline absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate with **coDiagnostix** implant planning per HTML m5 §5.6 — CBCT volume SHALL be exportable in DICOM format compatible with coDiagnostix import (DICOMDIR or direct DICOM C-STORE forward). Returned surgical guide design SHALL be linked back to `DPIM_ImplantPlan` (M4 v3-new) via `DPIM_SurgicalGuide` (M4 v3-new). Vendor credentials SHALL be Azure Key Vault-backed per IF-4.
source: HTML m5 §5.6 ("coDiagnostix"); cross-module M4 ImplantPlan + SurgicalGuide + IF-4
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: coDiagnostix integration absent; M4 implant workflow not extracted

### [5]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate with **Blue Sky Plan** implant planning as alternate vendor to coDiagnostix per HTML m5 §5.6. Practice's preferred vendor SHALL be configurable via `DPIM_PracticeSettings.preferredImplantPlanningVendor` (proposed D2 v3 addition). When practice has selected Blue Sky Plan, M5.6 exports SHALL target Blue Sky Plan; coDiagnostix path SHALL be inactive.
source: HTML m5 §5.6 ("Blue Sky Plan"); cross-module M2.5 + IF-4
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Blue Sky Plan integration absent; vendor-preference property absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **airway analysis** on CBCT volumes for sleep-apnea workflow per HTML m5 §5.6. AI segmentation SHALL identify the upper airway (oropharyngeal volume, narrowest cross-sectional area, length). Findings SHALL link to `DPIM_HSAT` and `DPIM_OralAppliance` (M4 SleepDentistry v3-new) workflow records and SHALL contribute to `DPIM_SleepApneaScreening` (M4 v3-new).
source: HTML m5 §5.6 ("airway analysis"); cross-module M4 SleepDentistry v3-new
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Airway-AI absent; M4 SleepDentistry not extracted

### [7]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **AI anatomic segmentation** on CBCT volumes per HTML m5 §5.6 — segmentation SHALL identify: maxillary sinuses, mandibular nerve canal (critical for implant planning), tooth roots, alveolar bone, airway. Segmentation results SHALL be captured as `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` with linked segmentation output (mesh references or DICOM SEG objects). Pearl AI is the primary vendor; alternates evaluated case-by-case.
source: HTML m5 §5.6 ("AI segmentation"); cross-module M5.9 + DICOM SEG IOD
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: AI segmentation absent

### [8]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL log **CBCT-specific radiation dose** — significantly higher than 2D modalities (typically 50–500 μSv depending on FOV size per NCRP Report 145). `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` SHALL capture: effective dose (μSv), CTDIvol where reported by scanner, DLP (dose-length product), and FOV used. Cumulative patient dose history SHALL highlight CBCT exposures distinctly given the higher per-capture risk. **Pediatric CBCT requires explicit clinical justification** per ALARA principle — workflow SHALL require justification text before capture for patients under 18.
source: NCRP Report 145; ICRP 103; ALARA principle (as low as reasonably achievable); cross-module M5.11 + M1 (patient age)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: CBCT dose tracking absent; pediatric-justification workflow absent

### [9]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **field-of-view management** — CBCT scanners support multiple FOV sizes: `small_focal` (5×5 cm, single tooth/region), `medium_arch` (8×8 cm, single arch), `large_maxillofacial` (16×13 cm or larger, full skull). FOV selection SHALL be driven by clinical indication per ALARA — smallest FOV producing diagnostic-quality image SHOULD be selected. OSCAR workflow SHALL warn when selected FOV exceeds clinical indication (e.g., large FOV for single-tooth indication).
source: NCRP Report 145; ALARA principle; AAOMR (American Academy of Oral and Maxillofacial Radiology) position statements; HTML m5 §5.6
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: FOV-vs-indication mapping absent

### [10]
reqId: M5-CBCT-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit CBCT lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.cone_beam_ct.captured` / `.volume_uploaded` / `.segmented` / `.exported_to_planning`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifiers tokenized; DICOM Study UID is tokenized as well (DICOM UIDs MAY be considered PHI-adjacent under HIPAA Safe Harbor). Subscribers (M4 ImplantPlan + SleepDentistry, M5.9 AI, M5.11 dose tracker, M5.12 sharing) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; HIPAA Safe Harbor (DICOM UID consideration); SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 5.7
id: 5.7
name: Intraoral Photography
purpose: Clinical photographic capture — extraoral, intraoral, retracted, occlusal views — distinct from radiographs (5.2–5.6) which use ionizing radiation. Per HTML m5 §5.7, OSCAR supports standardization templates (positional protocols), before/after comparison, and explicit marketing-use consent gating. Photos are particularly relevant to cosmetic dentistry workflows (M4 v3 `DPIM_AestheticTreatmentPlan` / `DPIM_BleachingPlan`), pediatric growth tracking (`DPIM_PediatricGrowthRecord` v3-new), and orthodontic case documentation. Patient photos are explicitly PHI (HIPAA Safe Harbor identifies facial photos as identifiable).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_PhotoSeries` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new under **Clinical domain**, **property-doc-pending**) - `DPIM_IntraOralPhoto` (per v3 §4 "kept here; clinical photo" — lives in Clinical domain, not Imaging top-level; **cross-domain consumption pattern** paralleling M2.11 Governance consumption) - `DPIM_PatientPhoto` (M1 — distinct: M1.1 patient demographic photo for operatory recognition + kiosk check-in) - `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new) — for AI photo analysis (cosmetic shade matching, smile-line analysis) - Cross-module: `DPIM_AestheticTreatmentPlan` (M4 v3-new), `DPIM_BleachingPlan` (M4 v3-new), `DPIM_PhotoSeries` consent linkage (M8 NO-DATA)
externalIntegrations: - **No primary external integration** — photo capture uses webcam / DSLR / vendor-bridged intraoral cameras (via M5.1 SensorBridge) - HIPAA Safe Harbor §164.514(b)(2)(i)(R) — full facial photos are identifying

M5.7 owns the capture-workflow and series-grouping logic; Clinical domain owns the underlying photo CI. **Pattern parallels M2.11 Governance-domain consumption** — M5 reqs cite `DPIM_IntraOralPhoto` with "consumed-from-Clinical-domain" annotation.

### [4]
reqId: M5-PHOTO-EXT-005

Without consent, M11 marketing workflows (Reputation Engine, before/after social proof) SHALL NOT include the photo. Consent SHALL be patient-revocable; revocation SHALL trigger photo-removal from all marketing surfaces within the practice-configured retraction window (default 30 days).

## 5.8
id: 5.8
name: DICOM Storage & PACS
purpose: The DICOM-conformant storage tier and Picture Archiving and Communication System backbone for OSCAR. All radiograph captures (M5.2 PA, M5.3 BWX, M5.4 Pano, M5.6 CBCT) and DICOM-wrapped photos (M5.7) flow into this storage layer. Per HTML m5 §5.8, OSCAR provides DICOM-conformant local store with cloud archive, state-specific 7–10 year retention, DICOM tag standardization, and DMWL worklist integration for modality-driven capture. PACS is the **most architecturally complex M5 sub-module** — DICOM interoperability, retention policy enforcement, WORM audit, hot/cold storage tiering, and DICOM Query/Retrieve all live here.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_DICOMStudy` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — top of DICOM hierarchy - `DPIM_DICOMSeries` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — series within study - `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — individual image / slice - Cross-module: `DPIM_RetentionPolicy` (M8 v3-new), `DPIM_LitigationHold` (M8 v3-new) — retention rules from M8 Documents; `DPIM_AuditEntry` (Governance) — WORM audit on DICOM operations
externalIntegrations: - **DICOM standards** — PS3.3 IODs, PS3.4 Service Classes, PS3.7 Message Exchange, PS3.10 Media Storage - **DICOM Modality Worklist (DMWL)** — pre-capture worklist push (M5.1 cross-ref) - **DICOM Query/Retrieve (C-FIND, C-MOVE, C-GET)** — outbound retrieval for external systems - **DICOMDIR** — CD/USB export format (M5.12 cross-ref) - **Azure Blob Storage WORM tier** (`stoscarwormdev`) — long-term retention storage - **Azure SQL** — DICOM metadata index (per IF-2)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain the **DICOM hierarchy** (`DPIM_DICOMStudy` → `DPIM_DICOMSeries` → `DPIM_DICOMInstance`) as the canonical storage anchor for all imaging captures. DICOM Study UID, Series UID, and SOP Instance UID SHALL be globally unique per DICOM PS3.5; UIDs SHALL be either preserved from sensor (DICOM-native captures) or generated server-side (TWAIN-wrapped captures).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (DICOM hierarchy v3-new); DICOM PS3.3 IOD; DICOM PS3.5 UID rules; HTML m5 §5.8
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new classes; property docs pending; DICOM tier absent

### [1]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL be **DICOM-conformant** — full DICOM Conformance Statement SHALL be published declaring supported SOP Classes (per modality), supported transfer syntaxes, supported Service Classes (Storage SCP, Query/Retrieve SCP, MWL SCP), supported character sets, and DICOM extensions. Conformance Statement SHALL be versioned and SHALL be available at a stable URL for vendor-side interoperability testing.
source: DICOM PS3.2 Conformance Statement; HTML m5 §5.8 ("DICOM-conformant"); industry interop testing requirements
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Conformance Statement not authored

### [2]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **DICOM Storage SCP** (Service Class Provider) — accept inbound DICOM C-STORE requests from sensors (M5.1) and external systems. Storage SCP SHALL: (a) validate DICOM IOD conformance; (b) extract metadata into `DPIM_DICOMStudy/Series/Instance`; (c) persist DICOM file to Azure Blob; (d) emit `dpim.imaging.dicom_instance.received` event. Rejected C-STORE requests SHALL log reason and source AE Title.
source: DICOM PS3.4 Storage Service Class; HTML m5 §5.8; cross-module M5.1 SensorBridge
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM C-STORE receiver absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **DICOM Query/Retrieve SCP** — accept inbound C-FIND, C-MOVE, and C-GET requests for OSCAR-stored studies. Query SHALL respect `practice_id` RLS (M2-PRAC-EXT-002) — external querying systems authenticate to a specific tenant and see only that tenant's studies. Cross-tenant query attempts SHALL be `DPIM_BreachIncident` per M2-TEN-EXT-011.
source: DICOM PS3.4 Query/Retrieve Service Class; cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-002 + M2-TEN-EXT-011
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Query/Retrieve SCP absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **DICOM Modality Worklist (DMWL) SCP** — answer C-FIND-Worklist queries from sensors with upcoming scheduled procedures requiring imaging (cross-module M3.15 check-in events). DMWL responses SHALL contain DICOM tags (0040,0002) Scheduled Procedure Step Sequence with tokenized patient identifier per M5-SENS-EXT-005.
source: DICOM PS3.4 Modality Worklist Service Class; cross-module M3.15 + M5.1
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: DMWL SCP absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **state-specific dental-record retention** per HTML m5 §5.8 — typically 7–10 years post-last-encounter for adults, age-of-majority + 7–10 years for minors (state-specific). Retention rules SHALL be sourced from M8 `DPIM_RetentionPolicy` (NO-DATA per handoff §4.4; M5 reqs depend on M8 extraction). DICOM Studies SHALL not be deletable while retention period is active. After retention expiration, automated destruction SHALL produce `DPIM_DocumentDestructionRecord` (M8 v3-new) per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.530(c).
source: HTML m5 §5.8 ("state-specific 7–10 year retention"); HIPAA 45 CFR 164.530(c); state dental board record-retention rules; cross-module M8 NO-DATA
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Retention policy engine absent; M8 source NO-DATA

### [6]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **litigation hold** override on retention — when `DPIM_LitigationHold` (M8 v3-new) is active against a patient, all imaging records (regardless of retention expiration) SHALL be preserved indefinitely until hold release. Hold release SHALL trigger re-evaluation of retention status. Litigation hold creation/release SHALL be `full-delta` audited per M2-TEN-EXT-005 cross-tenant pattern.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.530(j) (data preservation); state e-discovery rules; cross-module M8 LitigationHold + PS-7 WORM
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: LitigationHold class v3-new; M8 NO-DATA

### [7]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply **DICOM tag standardization** at storage time per HTML m5 §5.8 — DICOM tags SHALL be normalized: (0010,0010) Patient Name as `Last^First^Middle` per DICOM convention; (0010,0020) Patient ID as OSCAR's UUID v7; (0008,0050) Accession Number tied to `DPIM_ImagingSession.id`; (0008,0090) Referring Physician's Name from M10 `DPIM_Practitioner`; (0008,0080) Institution Name from `DPIM_Practice.name`. Non-conformant tags SHALL be corrected on inbound C-STORE.
source: DICOM PS3.3 Patient Module + General Series Module + General Equipment Module; HTML m5 §5.8 ("DICOM tag standardization")
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Tag normalization logic absent

### [8]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **hot/cold storage tiering** — recent studies (within practice-configured window, default 90 days) SHALL be in hot Azure Blob tier (immediate retrieval); older studies SHALL be moved to cool / archive tiers (1–15 hour retrieval latency). Tiering SHALL respect `DPIM_PracticeSettings.imagingHotTierDays` (proposed D2 v3 addition). Retrieval from cold tier SHALL emit `dpim.imaging.dicom_study.cold_retrieval_requested` for cost-tracking.
source: Azure Blob Storage tier semantics; cross-module M2.5
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Storage tiering policy absent; `imagingHotTierDays` property pending

### [9]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL **WORM-audit every DICOM operation** per HTML m5 §5.8 integ ("WORM Audit") — store, retrieve, query, hold, delete (retention expiration) SHALL each emit `DPIM_AuditEntry` (Governance) with cryptographic hash chain per RAIVS patent (DPIM v3 §3.12). Audit entries SHALL include operator identity, patient identifier (tokenized), DICOM Study UID, operation type, timestamp, and result.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b) (audit controls); RAIVS patent (DPIM v3 §3.12); cross-module PS-7 WORM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM-operation audit not wired to PS-7

### [10]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **DICOM tag de-identification** for cases where studies are shared outside the originating practice (research, teaching, vendor support). De-identification SHALL follow DICOM PS3.15 Attribute Confidentiality Profiles (Basic Profile + Clean Pixel Data Option + Retain Patient Characteristics Option as applicable). De-identified studies SHALL retain referential integrity to original via OSCAR-internal cross-reference table (not exported).
source: DICOM PS3.15 Confidentiality Profiles; HIPAA Safe Harbor §164.514(b); cross-module M5.12 sharing
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: De-identification pipeline absent

### [11]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **DICOMDIR export** for CD/USB media — when patient or referral packet requires physical media export, OSCAR SHALL generate DICOMDIR-conformant directory structure with: DICOMDIR index file + per-study folder hierarchy + IHE-PDI (Portable Data for Imaging) viewer (optional). DICOMDIR exports SHALL contain only PHI-authorized studies per patient consent.
source: DICOM PS3.10 Media Storage Profile; IHE PDI Profile; HTML m5 §5.12 (referral context); cross-module M5.12
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOMDIR export absent

### [12]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-013
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain **DICOM Study index** in Azure SQL (IF-2) for fast metadata-only queries — Patient ID, Study UID, Study Date, Modality, Description, Series Count, Instance Count, Accession Number. Index SHALL support sub-100ms query response for "show me all studies for patient X" or "show me all CBCT studies in date range". RLS SHALL apply on `practice_id` per M2-PRAC-EXT-002.
source: DICOM PS3.4 Query/Retrieve (informational); cross-module IF-2 Azure SQL + M2-PRAC-EXT-002
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM metadata index absent

### [13]
reqId: M5-PACS-EXT-014
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit DICOM-tier lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.dicom_study.created` / `.updated` / `.tier_transitioned` / `.deleted_per_retention` / `.litigation_held` / `.cold_retrieval_requested`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — Study UID and Patient ID are tokenized; full DICOM metadata access requires `ctx.read` against authenticated session. RBAC for DICOM operations — `clinical` roles SHALL retrieve/view; `office_manager` + `owner` SHALL configure tier policies; deletion SHALL require time-bounded super-admin elevation (M2-TEN-EXT-013 JIT PIM).
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-013 JIT PIM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3); JIT-PIM-gated deletion absent

## phaseSummary

P3: 1

## 5.9
id: 5.9
name: AI Diagnostic Engine
purpose: The AI orchestration layer for radiographic and photographic diagnostic inference. Per HTML m5 §5.9, Pearl AI integration (Phase 3) covers caries detection, periapical lesion detection, bone level (perio), calculus detection, and existing-restoration verification. Confidence scoring drives clinician-facing presentation; clinician acceptance/rejection feeds back into model evaluation for continuous improvement. Per DPIM v3 §1.7 design principle ("AI provenance is first-class"), every AI-derived finding SHALL reference a `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` capturing model version, inputs, outputs, and clinician disposition.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — AI provenance entity per v3 §1.7 - Cross-module: `DPIM_DentalCondition` (M4 — caries), `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` (M4 v3-new — radiographic pathology), `DPIM_DentalFinding` (M4 — restoration verification), `DPIM_PeriodontalFinding` (M4 — bone-level) - `DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph` / `DPIM_BitewingRadiograph` / `DPIM_PanoramicRadiograph` / `DPIM_ConeBeamCT` (M5.2–5.6) — input images - `DPIM_Integration` + `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` (Governance v3-new) — Pearl AI vendor registration
externalIntegrations: - **Pearl AI** — primary AI vendor (Phase 3) — caries, periapical lesion, bone level, calculus, existing-restoration verification, third-molar pathology - **PS-2 Agent Orchestrator** — invocation gateway with PHI boundary enforcement - **Vendor BAA** — Pearl AI BAA required (HIPAA Business Associate)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) as the **first-class AI provenance entity** per DPIM v3 §1.7 design principle. Each inference run SHALL have UUID v7, `vendor` (`pearl_ai` / `other`), `model` (model identifier + version), `inputs[]` (Reference list to source DICOM instances / `DPIM_IntraOralPhoto`), `outputs[]` (BackboneElement[] with detected findings + confidence scores), `invokedAt`, `completedAt`, `latencyMs`, `practitioner` Reference (clinician who invoked or session-owner), `acceptance` (`pending` / `accepted` / `rejected` / `modified`), `acceptanceTimestamp`, and `acceptanceText` (clinician justification).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §1.7 ("AI provenance is first-class"); DPIM v3 §4 (AIInferenceRun v3-new); HTML m5 §5.9; Module Catalog §6.2.9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending; AI provenance infrastructure absent

### [1]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate with **Pearl AI** as the primary AI vendor per HTML m5 §5.9 + §5.2 + §5.3 + §5.4. Integration SHALL: (a) register Pearl AI as `DPIM_Integration` (Governance v3-new); (b) store API credentials in Azure Key Vault (IF-4) referenced by `DPIM_IntegrationCredential` (v3-new); (c) submit images via HIPAA-BAA-protected API; (d) receive per-image findings with confidence scores. Pearl AI BAA SHALL be tracked via `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement` (M2-TEN-EXT-009).
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("Pearl AI integration"); HIPAA 45 CFR 164.504(e) BAA requirement; cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-009 + IF-4
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Pearl AI vendor not yet contracted; BAA pending

### [2]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **caries detection** on PA captures (M5.2) and BWX captures (M5.3) when `DPIM_PracticeSettings.aiFeatureToggles.clinicalDecisionSupport=true` (M2-SET-EXT-011). Detected caries findings SHALL produce `DPIM_DentalCondition` candidates (M4) referencing the `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` and the source image. Per-tooth, per-surface caries findings SHALL include severity scoring (incipient / moderate / severe).
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("caries"); HTML m5 §5.2 + §5.3; cross-module M2-SET-EXT-011 + M4 DentalCondition
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 not extracted; caries inference path absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **periapical lesion detection** on PA captures (M5.2) when AI toggle enabled. Detected lesions SHALL produce `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` candidates (M4 v3-new) with lesion classification (granuloma / cyst / abscess candidate). Findings SHALL link to the affected tooth via `anatomicReference` from the source PA (M5-PA-EXT-001).
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("periapical lesion"); HTML m5 §5.2; cross-module M4 RadiologicFinding v3-new
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 RadiologicFinding v3-new property-doc-pending

### [4]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **bone-level detection (periodontal)** on BWX (M5.3) and PA (M5.2) captures. Bone-level findings SHALL produce `DPIM_PeriodontalFinding` candidates (M4) with crestal-bone-loss measurement per tooth-surface. Bone-level findings SHALL feed into M4 `DPIM_PerioRiskAssessment` and SHALL drive recall cadence in M3.
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("bone level (perio)"); cross-module M4 PeriodontalFinding + PerioRiskAssessment
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 perio workflow not extracted

### [5]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **calculus detection** on PA (M5.2) and BWX (M5.3) captures. Calculus findings SHALL produce `DPIM_DentalFinding` candidates (M4) for hygienist reference at next visit. Calculus distribution patterns SHALL inform M3 recall cadence recommendations (heavier calculus → shorter recall interval per M2-SET-EXT-003 default override).
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("calculus"); cross-module M4 DentalFinding + M3 RecallSchedule
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M4 DentalFinding cross-module gap

### [6]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke **existing-restoration verification** on PA (M5.2) and BWX (M5.3) captures — detect existing fillings, crowns, bridges, implants visible radiographically. Detected restorations SHALL be cross-validated against M4 chart restorations; **mismatches SHALL surface to M11 IntegrityAlerts** (image shows restoration not in chart → possible phantom procedure / missed documentation; chart shows restoration not in image → possible coding fraud per M12.3 Phantom Visit Detection).
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("existing-restoration verification"); cross-module M4 chart + M11 (surfacing) + M12.3 (investigation)
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Restoration cross-validation absent; M12 NO-DATA

### [7]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **per-finding confidence scoring** — every AI-derived finding SHALL carry a confidence value (0.0–1.0). Practice-configurable thresholds SHALL gate UI presentation: above `displayThreshold` (default 0.50) finding is shown to clinician; above `autoAcceptThreshold` (default 0.85) finding pre-fills as draft `DPIM_DentalCondition`; clinician confirmation still required per `aiFeatureToggles.clinicalDecisionSupport` gating. Confidence thresholds SHALL be configurable via `DPIM_PracticeSettings.aiConfidenceThresholds` (proposed D2 v3 addition).
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("Confidence scoring"); cross-module M2.5 D2 v3 update
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Threshold-management UI absent; D2 v3 property pending

### [8]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement the **clinician acceptance feedback loop** per HTML m5 §5.9 — for every AI-derived finding the clinician's disposition (`accepted` / `rejected` / `modified`) SHALL be captured on `DPIM_AIInferenceRun.acceptance` along with optional `acceptanceText` justification. Acceptance signal SHALL feed back to Pearl AI for model improvement (per BAA-compliant feedback channel — practice MAY opt-in or opt-out of feedback-data sharing). Per v3-LOCKED §7.2 domain actions, `accept` and `reject` POSTs SHALL be supported.
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("clinician acceptance feedback loop"); DPIM v3 §7.2 (`POST .../imaging/ai_inference_runs/{id}/accept` and `/reject`); cross-module Pearl AI feedback API
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Feedback loop architecture absent

### [9]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke AI inference **asynchronously** — clinical capture (M5.2 / M5.3 / M5.4) SHALL NOT block on AI response (per OQ-5.2.3 recommendation). Inference results SHALL be delivered to the clinical workflow via WebSocket push (PS-5 Delivery) after AI processing completes. Stale inference results (results arriving after the clinical session has ended) SHALL still be persisted to `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` and surfaced as M11 ActionCard for clinician review at next session.
source: OQ-5.2.3 resolution; cross-module PS-5 Delivery + M11 ActionCard
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Async-inference dispatching absent

### [10]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL invoke AI inference through **PS-2 Agent Orchestrator** per HTML m5 §5.9 integ ("PS-2 Orchestrator"). PS-2 SHALL: (a) enforce PHI boundary (`services/agent-orchestrator/app/phi_boundary.py`); (b) tokenize patient identifiers in the outbound payload to Pearl AI; (c) attribute the inference invocation to a `DPIM_AgentSession` (Platform v3-new); (d) record `DPIM_ToolCall` (Platform v3-new) per inference invocation for replay/audit; (e) enforce per-tenant rate limiting per M2-TEN-EXT-006.
source: HTML m5 §5.9 ("PS-2 Orchestrator"); cross-module PS-2 + Platform domain v3-new + M2.11
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: PS-2 not yet extracted; Platform v3-new property-doc-pending

### [11]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL handle **AI-vendor PHI boundary** — DICOM images submitted to Pearl AI carry tokenized DICOM tags per M5-PACS-EXT-008 standardization. Patient Name in DICOM tag (0010,0010) SHALL be replaced with OSCAR's internal token before submission; Pearl AI SHALL receive image data + tokenized identifier only. **Pearl AI BAA covers PHI handling on their side**; OSCAR SHALL NOT rely on de-identification alone — vendor is treated as Business Associate.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.504(e) Business Associate provisions; cross-module M5-PACS-EXT-008 + M2-TEN-EXT-009 BAA + Agent Orchestrator PHI boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM tag tokenization for vendor calls absent

### [12]
reqId: M5-AI-EXT-013
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit AI-inference lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.ai_inference_run.invoked` / `.completed` / `.accepted` / `.rejected` / `.modified` (per v3 §6.2 event taxonomy). **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — inference references are tokenized; raw AI outputs (findings + confidence scores) carry tokenized image references only. Subscribers (M4 finding-candidate inbox, M11 ActionCard, M5.11 cumulative-AI-usage analytics, M12 phantom-visit cross-validator) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy (`dpim.imaging.ai_inference_run.completed` exemplar); SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 5.10
id: 5.10
name: Image Annotation & Markup
purpose: The clinical annotation layer overlaying captured images — drawing tools (arrow, circle, freehand), measurement tools (linear, angular), and structured findings markup. Per HTML m5 §5.10, annotations SHALL be versioned (clinician edit history preserved) and exportable with the underlying image for referral and claim-attachment purposes. Annotations link source images to M4 clinical findings, providing the visual evidence trail that supports treatment planning and insurance documentation.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ImageAnnotation` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) - Cross-module: `DPIM_DentalFinding` / `DPIM_RadiologicFinding` / `DPIM_PeriodontalFinding` (M4) — annotations bind to findings; `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (M5.9) — AI-generated annotations distinguished from human
externalIntegrations: None directly. Internal-only annotation pipeline.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ImageAnnotation` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records per annotation — UUID v7, `image` Reference (to `DPIM_DICOMInstance` / `DPIM_IntraOralPhoto`), `annotationType` (`arrow` / `circle` / `freehand` / `linear_measurement` / `angular_measurement` / `text_label` / `region_of_interest`), `geometry` JSON (coordinates, dimensions, scale), `color`, `text` (optional label), `provenance` (`human` / `ai_inference` — distinguishes clinician-drawn vs AI-overlay), `createdBy` Reference (M10 Practitioner or `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` reference), `createdAt`, `version`, and optional `findingReference` (Reference to M4 finding entity).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ImageAnnotation v3-new); HTML m5 §5.10 ("Drawing tools, measurement tools, versioned"); Module Catalog §6.2.10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending

### [1]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **drawing tools** per HTML m5 §5.10 — arrow (pointed line), circle (closed ellipse), freehand (polyline). Drawing tools SHALL operate in DICOM patient-coordinate space (millimeters via DICOM Pixel Spacing tag 0028,0030) when available; otherwise pixel-coordinate space. Tool selection SHALL be a single-click UI control with keyboard shortcut bindings.
source: HTML m5 §5.10 ("Drawing tools (arrow, circle, freehand)"); DICOM PS3.3 Pixel Spacing
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Drawing UI absent

### [2]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **measurement tools** per HTML m5 §5.10 — linear (distance between two points, mm if Pixel Spacing available, px otherwise) and angular (angle between three points or two line segments). Measurements SHALL be persisted in `geometry.measurement` with unit. Per-image calibration (when Pixel Spacing absent) SHALL allow operator-entered scale reference (e.g., 6mm reference object).
source: HTML m5 §5.10 ("measurement tools (linear, angular)"); DICOM Pixel Spacing; CBCT slice-thickness for 3D measurement
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Measurement tools absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain **versioned annotation history** per HTML m5 §5.10 — every annotation mutation SHALL produce a new version row; prior versions SHALL be retained. `DPIM_ImageAnnotation.version` SHALL be monotonic integer; viewers SHALL default to current version with operator-toggleable historical replay. Annotation deletion SHALL be soft-delete (`status=deleted`) preserving audit trail per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b).
source: HTML m5 §5.10 ("versioned annotated overlays"); HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b) audit controls; cross-module PS-7 WORM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Versioning + soft-delete absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **annotation export with image** per HTML m5 §5.10 — when an image is exported (referral packet M5.12, claim attachment M5.12 / M6, patient portal), annotations SHALL be embedded as either: (a) burned-in pixel overlay (default for non-DICOM JPEG export); (b) DICOM Presentation State Storage SOP Class (UID `1.2.840.10008.5.1.4.1.1.11.1`) for DICOM-conformant export. Burned-in option SHALL be irreversible; Presentation State preserves original image + separable overlay.
source: HTML m5 §5.10 ("annotation export with image"); DICOM PS3.3 Presentation State IOD; cross-module M5.12 + M6 attachments
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Export pipeline absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL distinguish **AI-generated annotations from human annotations** via `provenance` field (EXT-001). AI-generated annotations SHALL render in a distinct visual style (default: dashed-line + AI-confidence-color-coded); human annotations render in solid line + clinician-color. Mixed-provenance images (AI overlay + clinician markup) SHALL preserve both layers. Annotation export SHALL include provenance metadata in DICOM Presentation State or burned-in legend.
source: DPIM v3 §1.7 (AI provenance first-class); HTML m5 §5.10; cross-module M5.9 AI overlays
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Provenance-styled rendering absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL link annotations to **M4 clinical findings** via `findingReference` (EXT-001) — when a clinician annotates a region and confirms an associated finding, the annotation SHALL bind to the M4 finding entity (`DPIM_DentalFinding`, `DPIM_RadiologicFinding`, `DPIM_PeriodontalFinding`). Finding-bound annotations SHALL be queryable both directions: "show me all annotations on image X" and "show me the visual evidence for finding Y".
source: HTML m5 §5.10 integ ("m4 Findings"); cross-module M4 Findings
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Finding-binding workflow absent; M4 not extracted

### [7]
reqId: M5-ANN-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit annotation lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.image_annotation.created` / `.updated` / `.deleted` / `.bound_to_finding`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — annotation geometry contains no PHI directly, but image-reference UIDs are tokenized per M5-PACS-EXT-014. Subscribers (M4 finding inbox, M5.12 referral packets, M5.11 compliance audit) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 5.11
id: 5.11
name: FMX / Imaging Compliance
purpose: Per-payer and per-state imaging cadence enforcement, cumulative radiation-dose tracking, and provider-level compliance reporting. Per HTML m5 §5.11, OSCAR surfaces overdue / due-soon flagging plus compliance reporting per provider. Sentinel PS-4 consumes cumulative-dose and compliance signals for the PRS surface; WORM Audit (PS-7) captures every compliance event. This sub-module is the structured-rules engine that operationalizes the cadence requirements introduced in M5.3 (BWX), M5.4 (Pano), M5.5 (FMX), and M5.6 (CBCT).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — per-patient, per-modality compliance state - `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — cumulative dose-per-patient - Cross-module: `DPIM_PracticeRiskScore` (Platform v3-new — PS-4 Sentinel composite score); `DPIM_IntegrityAlert` (M11 v2); `DPIM_ActionCard` (M11); `DPIM_InsurancePlan.frequencyLimitations` (M2-INS-EXT-011); state Medicaid cadence rules (M5-FMX-EXT-005)
externalIntegrations: - State Medicaid programs (cadence rules) - Commercial dental payers (per-plan cadence in `DPIM_InsurancePlan`) - NCRP Report 145 (cumulative-dose guidance) - State radiation safety boards (per-state record-keeping requirements)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records — one per patient per modality (BWX / FMX / Pano / CBCT / Photo). Each record SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `modality` (enum), `lastCaptureDate`, `nextDueDate`, `cadenceRuleApplied` (citation to state-rule or payer-rule that drove the next-due calculation), `riskTier` (`low` / `moderate` / `high` — drives cadence interval), and `complianceStatus` (`current` / `due_soon` / `overdue`).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ImagingComplianceRecord v3-new); HTML m5 §5.11; Module Catalog §6.2.11
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending; engine absent

### [1]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL evaluate **per-payer cadence rules** per `DPIM_InsurancePlan.frequencyLimitations` (M2-INS-EXT-011) — when a patient has active coverage with frequency limitations on imaging CDT codes (e.g., D0210 FMX, D0274 BWX, D0330 Pano, D0367 CBCT), OSCAR SHALL evaluate the limitation against the patient's `lastCaptureDate` and compute `nextDueDate` accordingly. Payer-rule citation SHALL be retained on `cadenceRuleApplied` for audit traceability.
source: M2-INS-EXT-011 (frequencyLimitations); cross-module M2.6 + M6 estimator
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Frequency-rule evaluation not yet wired to compliance engine

### [2]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL evaluate **per-state cadence rules** per state Medicaid + state dental board record-retention rules (cross-module M5-FMX-EXT-005). Practice's state (derived from `DPIM_Practice.address.state`, M2.2) SHALL drive state-rule selection. State rules SHALL override less-restrictive payer rules when applicable; more-restrictive payer rules SHALL override state rules per OQ-5.5.4 resolution (more-restrictive wins).
source: M5-FMX-EXT-005; state Medicaid policy manuals; OQ-5.5.4 resolution; cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-001
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: State-rule library absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface **due-soon flagging** when `nextDueDate` is within `dueSoonWindowDays` (default 60) of current date. Due-soon SHALL produce a non-blocking M11 `DPIM_ActionCard` ("Mrs. Smith due for BWX in 45 days") routed to the front-office persona. ActionCard generation SHALL respect `aiFeatureToggles.actionCards` master toggle (M2-SET-EXT-011). `dueSoonWindowDays` SHALL be configurable via `DPIM_PracticeSettings.imagingDueSoonWindowDays` (proposed D2 v3 addition).
source: HTML m5 §5.11 ("due-soon flagging"); Phase 3 spec `OSCAR_Phase3_Clinical_Compliance_FMX_v3.html`; cross-module M11 + M2.5
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Due-soon engine absent; D2 v3 property pending

### [4]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface **overdue flagging** when current date exceeds `nextDueDate` AND patient remains active. Overdue SHALL produce a higher-priority M11 ActionCard with escalating cadence — initial flag at overdue+0; reminder at overdue+30; provider-visible flag at overdue+60. **Overdue compliance SHALL feed Sentinel PS-4 PRS dimension** (Articulation dimension, per Sentinel spec) when systemic across practice patient panel.
source: HTML m5 §5.11 ("overdue … flagging"); Sentinel PS-4 PRS Articulation dimension; cross-module M11 + PS-4
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Overdue engine + PS-4 wiring absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL produce **compliance reporting per provider** per HTML m5 §5.11 — for each provider in M10, OSCAR SHALL compute compliance rate (% of attributed patients with current imaging compliance, by modality). Reports SHALL be queryable in M11 Practice Pulse dashboards and SHALL drive provider-comparison views for DSO benchmarking (cross-module M2.1 DSO rollups per M2-DSO-EXT-003).
source: HTML m5 §5.11 ("compliance reporting per provider"); cross-module M10 + M11 + M2.1
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Per-provider compliance computation absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) cumulative-per-patient records — each capture event (M5.2 / M5.3 / M5.4 / M5.6) adds a dose-log entry; OSCAR SHALL maintain a rolling 12-month cumulative-dose total per patient. Per-modality dose values SHALL align with NCRP Report 145 reference values when scanner-reported dose is unavailable. **Per-capture scanner-reported dose** SHALL be preferred when available (modern DICOM-conformant sensors emit DICOM tag (0040,0316) CTDIvol or equivalent).
source: NCRP Report 145; ICRP 103; DICOM PS3.3 Radiation Dose tags; HTML m5 §5.11; cross-module M5.2 / M5.3 / M5.4 / M5.6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: RadiationDoseLog property doc pending; cumulative tracking absent

### [7]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface **cumulative-dose alerts** when patient's rolling 12-month cumulative dose exceeds practice-configured threshold (default 1500 μSv = ICRP public dose-limit baseline, configurable per `DPIM_PracticeSettings.cumulativeDoseAlertThreshold` proposed D2 v3 addition). Threshold breach SHALL produce M11 ActionCard alerting the provider before any new imaging request. **Pediatric patients (age < 18) SHALL have a more restrictive default threshold (750 μSv)** per ALARA principle and AAOMR pediatric position statements.
source: NCRP Report 145; ICRP 103 public dose limit; AAOMR pediatric position statements; cross-module M2.5 D2 v3 update
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Dose-threshold engine absent; D2 v3 property pending

### [8]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL feed compliance signals to **Sentinel PS-4 PRS Articulation dimension** — practice-wide imaging-compliance rate, overdue-count, and cumulative-dose-breach-count SHALL be computed nightly and fed to PS-4 per the PRS pattern. Imaging-compliance contribution to PRS SHALL be configurable per-tenant (some practices weigh compliance heavily, others lightly).
source: Sentinel PS-4 PRS spec (`09 - Sentinel AI/Sentinel_AI_Canopy_OSCAR_v1.1.docx`); HTML m5 §5.11 integ ("PS-4 Sentinel"); cross-module PS-4
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: PS-4 not yet extracted; current emits constant `15` per Phase 0 §5.2

### [9]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL **WORM-audit every compliance event** per HTML m5 §5.11 integ ("WORM Audit") — `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord` creation/update, dose-log entries, threshold breaches, overdue-flag-state changes, and ActionCard emissions SHALL each produce `DPIM_AuditEntry` (Governance) with cryptographic hash chain per RAIVS patent (DPIM v3 §3.12). Audit entries SHALL support post-hoc regulatory inspection (state dental board audit, OIG audit).
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b); RAIVS patent (DPIM v3 §3.12); state dental board audit requirements; cross-module PS-7 WORM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Compliance-event audit not wired to PS-7

### [10]
reqId: M5-COMP-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit compliance lifecycle events: `dpim.imaging.imaging_compliance_record.created` / `.due_soon` / `.overdue` / `.brought_current`; `dpim.imaging.radiation_dose_log.entry_added` / `.threshold_breached`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient references are tokenized; cumulative dose values are non-PHI numeric aggregates but patient-linkage SHALL stay tokenized at the SDK boundary. Subscribers (M11 ActionCard, PS-4 Sentinel, M3 RecallSchedule, M7 patient communication for overdue reminders) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary

## 5.12
id: 5.12
name: Image Sharing & Referral
purpose: The image-outbound layer — secure specialist sharing, claim-attachment routing via NEA / Vyne / Tesia attachment networks, HIPAA-compliant patient portal access, and DICOM CD/USB physical export. Per HTML m5 §5.12, this is M5's interface to the outside-of-practice ecosystem. Distinct from M5.8 PACS (storage / internal retrieval) — M5.12 is outbound-only.
dpimClasses: - Cross-module: `DPIM_ClaimAttachment` (M6 Financial) — claim attachments produced from M5 images - `DPIM_OutboundReferral` (M7 Communication v2) — specialist referrals composing images - `DPIM_PatientPortalAccount` (M1 Identity v2) — portal-visible image catalog - `DPIM_AuditEntry` (Governance) — every share audited
externalIntegrations: - **NEA (National Electronic Attachments)** — HIPAA-conformant claim attachment service - **Vyne** — claim attachment via primary clearinghouse (M2.10 / M2-INTG-EXT-003) - **Tesia** — alternate attachment network - **HIPAA Patient Portal** — patient-self-service image access - **DICOMDIR + IHE PDI** — physical media export (CD/USB) - **Secure link generation** — short-lived signed URLs for specialist sharing (vendor-pluggable: Azure Blob Shared Access Signature with strict TTL — but per M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS-tokens policy this requires special handling)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-SHARE-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **secure-link sharing to specialists** per HTML m5 §5.12 — generate a time-bounded, recipient-bound, single-use link granting access to a specific `DPIM_DICOMStudy` (or set of studies). Links SHALL: (a) be cryptographically signed (HMAC-SHA-256 over recipient_email + study_uid + expiration); (b) expire within practice-configured TTL (default 7 days); (c) require recipient email verification at click time; (d) revocable by originating practice. **Per M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS-tokens policy**, links SHALL NOT use Azure Blob SAS — link must route through OSCAR's authenticated API surface with link-token validation.
source: HTML m5 §5.12 ("Send to specialist via secure link"); HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312 access controls; M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS policy
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Secure-link gateway absent; SAS-token policy creates API-routed-link architecture requirement

### [1]
reqId: M5-SHARE-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL route **claim attachments via NEA** for payers with `supportsAttachments=true` AND M2.10 clearinghouse routing-target supporting NEA. M6 RCM SHALL invoke M5.12 to attach the specified DICOM Study (or annotated export of it per M5-ANN-EXT-005) to a pending 837D submission. Attachment-routing failures SHALL block claim submission until resolved.
source: HTML m5 §5.12 ("NEA"); cross-module M2-INS-EXT-004 supportsAttachments + M2.10 + M6 RCM claim submission

### [4]
reqId: M5-SHARE-EXT-005

Portal access SHALL: (a) authenticate the patient per M1 portal-auth; (b) tokenize image URLs (no direct blob URLs); (c) audit every image view via PS-7 WORM; (d) honor M5-PHOTO-EXT-005 marketing-use consent (separate from clinical-image-viewing access). Patient-portal image access SHALL satisfy Cures Act §4002 patient-right-of-access requirement when combined with M2-TEN-EXT-012 DataExportRequest fulfillment.
source: HTML m5 §5.12 ("HIPAA-compliant patient portal access"); 21st Century Cures Act §4002; HIPAA 45 CFR 164.524; cross-module M1 PatientPortalAccount + M2-TEN-EXT-012 + PS-7

Export packages SHALL include: (a) DICOMDIR index; (b) per-study folder hierarchy; (c) IHE PDI viewer (optional, configurable per export); (d) annotation overlays (burned-in or Presentation State per M5-ANN-EXT-005); (e) export-manifest text file listing study metadata for paper records. Export operations SHALL be `full-delta` WORM-audited (operator, destination, study list, justification).

## 5.13
id: 5.13
name: Imaging Analytics
purpose: The analytics surface for M5 — diagnostic yield per image type, AI agreement / override rates, re-take rate (radiation safety), modality utilization. Per HTML m5 §5.13, M5.13 feeds M11 Practice Operations & Analytics dashboards (Practice Pulse, Cost Control, Strategy Zone) with imaging-specific KPIs. Distinct from M5.11 Compliance (rule enforcement) — M5.13 is descriptive/analytical, not prescriptive. Cross-module with PS-3 Thread Intelligence for snapshot-driven analytics rollups.
dpimClasses: - Consumed from Analytics domain: `DPIM_PracticeKPI` (v2), `DPIM_ProcedureProfitability` (v2 — for AI ROI computation), `DPIM_BenchmarkMetric` (v3-new — for imaging benchmarks), `DPIM_BusinessReport` (v3-new), `DPIM_ScheduledReportRun` (v3-new) - Cross-module: `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (M5.9) — agreement/override rate computation source; `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog` (M5.11) — retake-rate source; `DPIM_SensorBridge` (M5.1) — modality utilization source
externalIntegrations: None directly. Internal-only analytics rollup.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **diagnostic-yield-per-image-type** per HTML m5 §5.13 — for each modality (PA / BWX / Pano / FMX / CBCT / Photo) and image-type combination, OSCAR SHALL track: total captures (per period), AI-detected-findings count, clinician-confirmed-findings count, billable-procedures-produced count, yield ratio (`confirmedFindings / totalCaptures`). Metrics SHALL be computed nightly and surfaced via M11 Practice Pulse.
source: HTML m5 §5.13 ("Diagnostic yield per image type"); cross-module M11 + M5.9 + M5.2–5.6
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics pipeline absent; M11 not extracted

### [1]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **AI agreement / override rates** — per `DPIM_AIInferenceRun.acceptance` (M5.9), the rate of `accepted` vs `rejected` vs `modified` outcomes per finding-type per AI vendor. Override-rate drift over time SHALL surface as M11 IntegrityAlert when material change is detected (per-finding-type override-rate increases > 20% over 30-day window — suggests model drift or workflow change).
source: HTML m5 §5.13 ("AI agreement / override rates"); cross-module M5.9 + M11 IntegrityAlert
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M5.9 acceptance tracking absent

### [2]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **re-take rate** per HTML m5 §5.13 — flag captures of the same modality + same anatomic region within short timeframes (default 15 minutes for clinical-retake, 24 hours for QA-retake) as potential re-takes. Per-provider, per-sensor retake-rate SHALL surface in M11 as a radiation-safety KPI. **Re-take rate above practice-configured threshold (default 0.05 = 5% retake rate) SHALL alert per ALARA principle** — retakes contribute to cumulative patient dose.
source: HTML m5 §5.13 ("re-take rate (radiation safety)"); ALARA principle; cross-module M11 + M10 (per-provider attribution)
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Re-take detection absent

### [3]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **modality utilization** per HTML m5 §5.13 — per `DPIM_SensorBridge`, captures-per-day / captures-per-week / captures-per-month rates. Underutilized sensors SHALL surface in M11 Cost Control (potential capital-equipment redirection); over-utilized sensors SHALL flag potential maintenance / calibration cadence issues (cross-module M9 Equipment maintenance).
source: HTML m5 §5.13 ("modality utilization"); cross-module M11 + M9 + M5.1
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Sensor-utilization analytics absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **per-provider imaging-volume analytics** — captures-per-provider per modality per period, with peer-comparison rollup at DSO level (per M2-DSO-EXT-003 rollup pattern). Outliers (provider captures 2σ above peers for same patient demographic) SHALL surface to M11 IntegrityAlert as **potential phantom-procedure signal feeding M12.3 Phantom Visit Detection** per HTML m5 deps.downstream M12 routing.
source: HTML m5 §5.13; cross-module M10 + M11 (surfacing) + M12.3 (investigation) + M2.1 DSO rollup
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Per-provider rollup absent; M12 NO-DATA

### [5]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **patient cumulative-dose cohort analytics** — distribution of cumulative-12-month-dose across the active patient panel. Practice-level percentile distribution (P50 / P75 / P95 / P99 dose patients) SHALL surface in M11 for radiation-safety leadership review. **Cohort identifying patients in P95+ dose tier with active treatment plans SHALL trigger clinical review workflow** before next imaging request.
source: NCRP Report 145; cross-module M5.11 + M11 + M4 treatment plan
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Cohort analytics absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL feed M11 **Strategy Zone Portfolio Uplift** with imaging-domain metrics — practice-wide imaging-compliance rate (M5.11), AI-acceptance rate (M5.9), retake rate (M5.13), CBCT-utilization rate (high-margin modality indicator). These metrics SHALL contribute to `DPIM_PracticeValuation.valuationNarrative` per M2-DSO-EXT-008 (resolved OQ-2.1.3 — narrative generated by M11 Strategy Zone Portfolio Uplift agent).
source: OQ-2.1.3 resolution (M11 Portfolio Uplift narrative agent); cross-module M11 + M2.1
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: M11 Portfolio Uplift agent not yet specified

### [7]
reqId: M5-ANLY-EXT-008

Subscribers (M11 dashboards, M12.3 phantom-procedure surveillance, PS-4 Sentinel Articulation dimension) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 5.14
id: 5.14
name: Cross-cutting · DPIM Property Propagation
purpose: Requirements spanning M5's relationship to the DPIM v3 LOCKED model — DPIM Gate (CI Gate 1) compliance for M5 manifests, AI Provenance first-class pattern (v3 §1.7 design principle), DICOM hierarchy semantics, Imaging-domain property propagation, ODIN clinical category alignment, FHIR R4 ImagingStudy mapping discipline, and the codification of the v3-new Imaging-domain property-doc gap (Finding #5).
dpimClasses: `DPIM_ImagingSession`, `DPIM_SensorBridge`, `DPIM_DICOMStudy`, `DPIM_DICOMSeries`, `DPIM_DICOMInstance`, `DPIM_Radiograph` (abstract), `DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph`, `DPIM_BitewingRadiograph`, `DPIM_PanoramicRadiograph`, `DPIM_ConeBeamCT`, `DPIM_CephalometricRadiograph`, `DPIM_RadiographSeries` (abstract), `DPIM_FMXSeries`, `DPIM_BWXSeries`, `DPIM_PASeries`, `DPIM_IntraOralScan`, `DPIM_STLModel`, `DPIM_3DDentalModel`, `DPIM_CADCAMDesign`, `DPIM_MillingJob`, `DPIM_ImageAnnotation`, `DPIM_ImagingComplianceRecord`, `DPIM_RadiationDoseLog`, `DPIM_AIInferenceRun`. Plus cross-domain `DPIM_IntraOralPhoto` (Clinical), `DPIM_PhotoSeries` (Clinical), `DPIM_PatientPhoto` (Person v3-new).
externalIntegrations: 

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR M5 manifests SHALL pass the **DPIM Gate (CI Gate 1)** on every PR per DPIM v3 §12. The gate SHALL validate that every M5-owned class declared in `manifest.yaml` reads/writes/emits is present in `dpim-v3-registry.json`; references to undeclared classes SHALL fail the gate. Cross-domain consumption (`DPIM_IntraOralPhoto` from Clinical, `DPIM_PatientPhoto` from Person, Governance-domain classes from M5.8 audit and M5.12 sharing) SHALL be explicitly declared with `consumesFrom` annotation.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §12 (DPIM Gate); CI Gate 1 (`manifest-validate.yml`); DPIM Validator (`02 - Architecture & Design/DPIM Model/tools/dpim-validator/`)
phase: P1
status: Code Present, Untested
evidence: Validator passes on existing manifests; no M5-owned manifests yet exist on main

### [1]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **AI Provenance as a first-class pattern** per DPIM v3 §1.7 ("AI provenance is first-class") — every AI-derived clinical artifact (caries finding, lesion finding, segmentation result, annotation, anatomic measurement) SHALL reference a `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (M5.9) and SHALL NOT carry inline provenance metadata. The `findingReference → DPIM_AIInferenceRun` indirection SHALL be the canonical pattern across all 13 M5 sub-modules.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §1.7 + §1.8 design principles; cross-module M5.9 throughout M5
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: First-class AI provenance pattern absent across M4 + M5 (interim AI findings carry metadata)

### [2]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply **DPIM property metadata uniformly across all 18 Imaging-domain v3-new classes** per DPIM v3 §2.1 schema. Per Finding #5, **none of these classes have a property doc** — M5 manifests SHALL register them as DPIM Gate `partial-coverage-warning` per M2-DPIM-EXT-006 pattern. Property docs SHALL be authored as a dedicated D-Imaging property doc before Phase 3 M5 implementation begins.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §2.1 Property Metadata Schema; Finding #5 codification; cross-module M2-DPIM-EXT-006 pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: D-Imaging property doc absent (per Finding #1: "Imaging — None")

### [3]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain **DICOM hierarchy semantics** per v3 §4 — `DPIM_DICOMStudy` (per imaging session) → `DPIM_DICOMSeries` (per modality / orientation grouping) → `DPIM_DICOMInstance` (per individual image / slice). Storage-tier classes (Study/Series/Instance) are distinct from clinical-tier classes (`DPIM_PeriapicalRadiograph`, `DPIM_ConeBeamCT`, etc.). Clinical-tier classes SHALL reference one-or-more `DPIM_DICOMInstance` records as the underlying storage. **M5.8 PACS owns the storage-tier; M5.2–5.6 own clinical-tier.**
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 hierarchy; DICOM PS3.3 IOD; cross-module M5.8 + M5.2–5.6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DICOM hierarchy implementation absent

### [4]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL align Imaging-domain classes to **ODIN data category `clinical` (primary)** per DPIM v3 §5 Domain Registry — Imaging classes carry PHI (image-correlated patient identity); ODIN category drives event envelope `metadata.odinCategory=clinical`. Cross-domain consumed `DPIM_IntraOralPhoto` retains its Clinical-domain `odinCategory` even when consumed by M5.7.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §5 (Imaging primary ODIN = clinical); DPIM v3 §6.1 event envelope
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: ODIN category propagation absent

### [5]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit M5 events on the **standard envelope per DPIM v3 §6.1** with all required fields, **including the cross-domain `dpim.clinical.intra_oral_photo.*` events per M5-PHOTO-EXT-009** (mixed-domain prefix). Event envelope `metadata.domain` SHALL reflect the source-class domain (Clinical for IntraOralPhoto; Imaging for everything else), not the consuming module. Subscription filters per PS-9 SHALL honor source-domain routing.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §6.1 Event Envelope; cross-module Layer 3 SDK + PS-9 + OQ-5.7.2 resolution
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Mixed-domain event prefix handling absent

### [6]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain **FHIR R4 mapping discipline** per DPIM v3 §1.8 — every Imaging-domain class SHALL declare its FHIR R4 resource mapping (FHIR `ImagingStudy` / `Media` / `DiagnosticReport` / `DocumentReference` / `Observation`) or declare "OSCAR-profile" if no clean FHIR equivalent exists. Cures Act §4002 patient-right-of-access fulfillment (M2-TEN-EXT-012) depends on FHIR-conformant export.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §1.8 ("FHIR R4 mapping discipline"); 21st Century Cures Act §4002; FHIR R4 ImagingStudy resource; cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-012
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: FHIR mapping incomplete for v3-new Imaging classes

### [7]
reqId: M5-DPIM-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL register **Finding #5 codification** — all 18 Imaging-domain v3-new classes lacking property docs SHALL be registered as DPIM Gate `partial-coverage-warning` exceptions until D-Imaging property doc lands. **This requirement is the M5 analog of M2-DPIM-EXT-006** (which codified Finding #4 for Org-domain). The combined Finding #4 + Finding #5 indicates DPIM Property Doc completion is the canonical blocker for Phase 1+3 module implementation.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §12 DPIM Gate; D2 v2.0.0 + D-Imaging gap (Finding #5); cross-module M2-DPIM-EXT-006 pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: DPIM Gate exception/partial-coverage path absent

## features

### [0]
id: F-DPIM-1
name: DPIM Gate Compliance (M5)
desc: 

## composedOf

Each integration SHALL track BAA status, last-health-check, vendor capability matrix.

## m7
moduleId: m7

## subModules

## 7.1
id: 7.1
name: Communication Log
purpose: The foundational record of every patient interaction across every channel — SMS, email, voice, portal, in-person, fax, paper letter — captured uniformly with direction, channel, initiator, disposition, and WORM audit chain. Per HTML m7 §7.1, M7.1 is the **shared infrastructure** every other M7 sub-module emits to: M7.2 thread messages, M7.3 notifications, M7.6 calls, M7.7 template-driven sends, M7.9 review requests, M7.10 marketing campaigns, M7.11 surveys, M7.12 recalls, M7.13 reactivations, M7.14 family digests, M7.15 complaints all produce `DPIM_CommLog` entries. Per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.528 accounting-of-disclosures requirement, the comm-log is the canonical record for "who communicated with this patient, when, how, and about what."
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_CommLog` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**)
- Inherited abstract: `DPIM_Communication`
- Cross-module: `DPIM_CallRecord` (7.6), `DPIM_ConversationThread` (7.2), `DPIM_Notification` (7.3) all back-reference `DPIM_CommLog` entries; `DPIM_AuditEntry` (Governance) — every comm-log entry produces a PS-7 WORM hash-chain entry
externalIntegrations: None directly. Internal-only logging pipeline; downstream consumers populate this.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_CommLog` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records per patient interaction. Each entry SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `direction` (`inbound` / `outbound`), `channel` (`sms` / `email` / `voice` / `portal` / `in_person` / `fax` / `letter`), `initiator` (`staff` / `patient` / `system` / `ai`), `initiatorRef` (Reference to `DPIM_Practitioner` / `DPIM_Staff` / `DPIM_Patient` / `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` / `system`), `disposition` (`delivered` / `failed` / `read` / `replied` / `bounced` / `opted_out` / `escalated`), `timestamp`, `purposeCategory` (`appointment_reminder` / `recall` / `statement` / `marketing` / `referral` / `survey` / `complaint_response` / `clinical_followup` / `treatment_plan_present`), and `correlationId` to upstream source CI (Notification / CallRecord / etc.).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (CommLog v2); HTML m7 §7.1; Module Catalog §8.2.1; HIPAA 45 CFR 164.528
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 update pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **universal channel logging** — every patient-facing communication on every channel SHALL produce a `DPIM_CommLog` entry per HTML m7 §7.1 integ ("Every patient interaction across all channels"). Channel-bypass (e.g., direct vendor API call without log) SHALL be rejected at the SDK validation gate. The `DPIM_CommLog` entry SHALL be created via `ctx.governed_write` co-transactionally with the underlying send/receive operation per PS-8 Outbox Relay atomic triple-write pattern.
source: HTML m7 §7.1 ("Every patient interaction across all channels"); cross-module Layer 3 SDK + PS-8 atomic triple-write
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Universal logging gate absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL classify the `initiator` field with provenance distinctions per DPIM v3 §1.7 (AI provenance first-class): `staff` initiator SHALL reference `DPIM_Practitioner` or `DPIM_Staff`; `patient` initiator SHALL reference `DPIM_Patient`; `system` initiator SHALL be unattributed (automated cadence-driven sends); `ai` initiator SHALL reference `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (cross-module M5.9 pattern, retrofitted per OQ-DPIM-M5-3). Initiator mutation post-create SHALL be prohibited (immutable provenance).
source: DPIM v3 §1.7 (AI provenance first-class); cross-module M5-AI-EXT-001 + OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: AI provenance retrofit pending

### [3]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL **WORM-audit every `DPIM_CommLog` entry** via PS-7 per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.528 accounting-of-disclosures requirement. Audit entries SHALL form RAIVS-pattern hash chain per DPIM v3 §3.12. Comm-log audit trail SHALL be the canonical source for patient-facing "accounting of disclosures" reports per HIPAA right-of-access (cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-012 Cures Act data export).
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.528; DPIM v3 §3.12 RAIVS; HTML m7 §7.1 integ ("WORM Audit · DPIM CommLog"); cross-module PS-7 + M2-TEN-EXT-012
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: PS-7 wired for other modules; CommLog-specific path absent

### [4]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `correlationId` linkage from `DPIM_CommLog` to upstream source CI — e.g., comm-log entry for outbound appointment reminder references the source `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3); comm-log entry for inbound call references the source `DPIM_CallRecord` (M7.6); comm-log entry for portal message references `DPIM_ConversationThread` (M7.2). Bi-directional queryability SHALL be supported: "show all comm-logs for thread X" and "show source CI for comm-log Y."
source: HTML m7 §7.1 (channel-aggregator role); cross-module M7.2 + M7.3 + M7.6 + M7.7 + M7.9 + M7.10 + M7.11 + M7.12 + M7.13 + M7.14 + M7.15
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Correlation infrastructure absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL classify `purposeCategory` to drive downstream behavior — TCPA frequency caps (M1-CONT-EXT-007) operate **across-category** per FCC TCPA Order, but per-category aggregation drives marketing-attribution (M7.10), recall conversion analytics (M7.12), and reactivation tracking (M7.13). `purposeCategory` mutation post-create SHALL be prohibited (immutable per send).
source: M1-CONT-EXT-007 (TCPA frequency caps); FCC TCPA Order (per-recipient limits); cross-module M7.10 + M7.12 + M7.13
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Category taxonomy not yet seeded

### [6]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL classify `DPIM_CommLog.disposition` lifecycle states with channel-specific terminal states: SMS supports `delivered` / `failed` / `replied` / `opted_out` (via STOP keyword); email supports `delivered` / `failed` / `bounced` / `read` (via open-pixel) / `replied` / `opted_out` (unsubscribe link); voice supports `delivered` / `failed` / `voicemail_left` / `replied` (callback); portal supports `read` / `replied`. Channel-specific terminal-state transitions SHALL emit `dpim.comm.comm_log.disposition_updated`.
source: HTML m7 §7.1; CAN-SPAM 15 USC 7701 (unsubscribe + bounce handling); FCC TCPA Order (STOP keyword honor); cross-module M7.3 + M7.10 + M7.11
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Disposition tracking absent across channels

### [7]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL treat `DPIM_CommLog` content as **PHI** (per HIPAA Safe Harbor — message content can correlate to patient identity + treatment). Storage SHALL be in tenant-scoped RLS-protected schema; message-body retention SHALL respect M8 `DPIM_RetentionPolicy` (NO-DATA — typically 7–10 years per state dental record retention). Message body SHALL NOT enter LLM context without explicit clinician-initiated tool call per PS-2 Agent Orchestrator PHI boundary.
source: HIPAA Safe Harbor §164.514; cross-module M8 RetentionPolicy (NO-DATA) + PS-2 PHI boundary + IF-2 RLS
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: PHI boundary on message body not specifically wired

### [8]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `DPIM_CommLog` lifecycle events: `dpim.comm.comm_log.created` / `.disposition_updated` / `.reply_received`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifier tokenized; message body summary (purpose + channel + disposition + timestamp) only in event payload; full content requires `ctx.read` against authenticated session. Subscribers (M7.2 ConversationThread back-population, M11 engagement analytics, M2-TEN-EXT-012 Cures Act access-log compositor, M12.8 Insurance Fraud cross-validator on suspicious comm patterns) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary; cross-module M11 + M2.11 + M12
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

### [9]
reqId: M7-LOG-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL restrict `DPIM_CommLog` reads to: (a) the recipient patient (via portal Cures Act access-log per M2-TEN-EXT-012); (b) staff assigned to the patient (clinical / front-office / billing per role); (c) `super_admin` cross-tenant access (WORM-audited per M2-TEN-EXT-005). RBAC SHALL be enforced at OPA verdict layer (PS-6) with per-purpose-category visibility — e.g., billing staff SHALL see statement comm-logs but NOT clinical follow-up comm-logs unless cross-trained.
source: DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; HIPAA Minimum Necessary doctrine (45 CFR 164.502(b)); cross-module PS-6 + M2-TEN-EXT-005
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Per-purpose-category RBAC absent
