## 7.2
id: 7.2
name: Conversation Threads
purpose: Two-way conversational threading across SMS, email, and portal — every patient ↔ practice interaction within a continuous thread context. Per HTML m7 §7.2, M7.2 owns: two-way SMS threading per patient, email threading (Message-ID/References header chain), portal messaging, cross-channel unified inbox, thread assignment to staff, and AI-suggested replies via PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM verdict pattern. AI-drafted replies extend the DPIM v3 §1.7 AI provenance first-class pattern (M5-AI-EXT-001 / OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope) — every AI-suggested reply references `DPIM_AIInferenceRun`.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ConversationThread` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, D8 v3 update pending)
- `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new — for AI-suggested replies)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_CommLog` (7.1 — each thread message produces a CommLog entry); `DPIM_AgentSession` + `DPIM_ToolCall` (Platform v3-new — for PS-2 invocation provenance); `DPIM_Practitioner` / `DPIM_Staff` (M10 — thread assignment); `DPIM_Patient` (M1 — thread participant)
externalIntegrations: - **Twilio SMS** — inbound webhook → thread match
- **SendGrid email** — inbound parse webhook → thread match (Message-ID / References / In-Reply-To header chain)
- **i4 Patient Portal** — portal messaging surface

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ConversationThread` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records as per-patient per-channel-or-cross-channel thread anchors. Each thread SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `channelScope` (`sms_only` / `email_only` / `portal_only` / `cross_channel`), `state` (`open` / `in_progress` / `resolved` / `closed` / `escalated`), `assignedStaff` Reference (optional, M10), `subject` (optional, primarily email-driven), `lastActivityAt`, `messageCount`, and `correlationIdentifiers[]` (BackboneElement[] — phone numbers, email addresses, portal-message-IDs that match this thread).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ConversationThread v2); HTML m7 §7.2; Module Catalog §8.2.2
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **two-way SMS threading** per HTML m7 §7.2 — inbound SMS messages received via Twilio webhook SHALL be matched to existing patient via `DPIM_Patient.phoneNumber` lookup (M1.2 Patient Contact) and appended to the open `DPIM_ConversationThread` with `channelScope=sms_only` or `cross_channel`. Match-failure (unknown phone number) SHALL create a `DPIM_Lead` (Person v3-new) entity + new thread with `channelScope=sms_only`, routed to front-office persona for triage.
source: HTML m7 §7.2 ("Two-way SMS threading per patient"); cross-module M1.2 patient contact + M1 Lead v3-new + Twilio inbound webhook
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Twilio webhook receiver absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **email threading** via standard email-header convention — Message-ID, References, In-Reply-To headers SHALL be tracked per inbound/outbound email. Thread continuity SHALL be preserved across email-client variations (Gmail/Outlook quoting differences). Inbound email match SHALL prefer References-chain match → In-Reply-To match → subject-string match → patient-email-address match in that priority order. Match-failure SHALL create a new thread or route to a lead-triage workflow per M7-THR-EXT-002 pattern.
source: RFC 5322 §3.6.4 (Identification Fields); IETF email-threading conventions; cross-module M1.2 patient contact (email)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Email parse webhook + threading engine absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **portal messaging threads** with messaging-surface authenticated via `DPIM_PatientPortalAccount` (M1 Identity v2). Portal messages SHALL be tagged `channelScope=portal_only`; cross-channel escalation (e.g., portal-to-SMS) SHALL produce a new thread with `channelScope=cross_channel` and inbound-message back-reference. Portal threads SHALL respect family-account routing per M7.14.
source: HTML m7 §7.2 ("portal messaging"); cross-module M1 PatientPortalAccount + i4 Portal + M7.14
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Portal messaging surface absent

### [4]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **cross-channel unified inbox** per HTML m7 §7.2 — staff workstation SHALL display SMS / email / portal messages in a single chronological inbox per assigned-staff filter. Cross-channel threading SHALL preserve channel-of-origin metadata on each message and SHALL allow staff to reply on a different channel than the inbound origin (e.g., reply via SMS to a patient who emailed in). Channel-switch reply SHALL emit `dpim.comm.conversation_thread.channel_switched` for analytics.
source: HTML m7 §7.2 ("cross-channel unified inbox"); cross-module Workstation Web i2
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Unified inbox absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **thread assignment workflow** — front-office staff MAY claim, reassign, or unassign threads via i2 Workstation Web. Assignment state SHALL be persisted on `DPIM_ConversationThread.assignedStaff` and SHALL drive inbox filtering. Unassigned threads SHALL surface to the practice-wide unassigned-inbox view. Assignment changes SHALL emit `dpim.comm.conversation_thread.assigned` / `.reassigned` / `.unassigned` events.
source: HTML m7 §7.2 ("thread assignment"); cross-module M10 Practitioner/Staff + i2
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Assignment workflow absent

### [6]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **AI-suggested replies** per HTML m7 §7.2 — the integrated AI assistant SHALL generate reply drafts via PS-2 Agent Orchestrator with `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` verdict per DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix and OSCAR Three Lanes / Three Channel Rule (Lane 3 Agentic). Each AI-drafted reply SHALL reference a `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` per DPIM v3 §1.7 (M5-AI-EXT-001 pattern, retrofitted per OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope). Staff SHALL accept, edit, or reject the draft before send; accepted-and-sent replies SHALL be initiator-classified as `ai` per M7-LOG-EXT-003.
source: HTML m7 §7.2 ("AI-suggested replies (PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM)"); DPIM v3 §1.7 + §10 verdict matrix; cross-module PS-2 + M5-AI-EXT-001 pattern + Layer 3 SDK
phase: P3

### [7]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL pass AI-drafted reply text through the **PS-2 Agent Orchestrator PHI boundary** — patient identifiers in the prompt context SHALL be tokenized before the LLM call (services/agent-orchestrator/app/phi_boundary.py); the LLM SHALL receive tokenized context only. Generated draft SHALL be de-tokenized only at the staff-facing display layer. AI-drafted replies SHALL NOT be auto-sent — `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` verdict requires staff confirmation per M7-THR-EXT-007.
source: OSCAR PHI boundary spec; cross-module PS-2 phi_boundary.py + M5-AI-EXT-012 pattern
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: PHI boundary on outbound LLM call partial

### [8]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **TCPA + CAN-SPAM compliance** on every outbound thread message per M7.3 NOTIF gates (M1-CONT-EXT-006 quiet hours, M1-CONT-EXT-007 frequency caps). Thread-reply sends SHALL evaluate `DPIM_TCPAConsent.{phone_number, channel, purpose}` triple before transmission; failure = verdict `BLOCK` with staff-facing reason. STOP keyword honor on inbound SMS SHALL immediately update `DPIM_OptOut` and block subsequent outbound on that channel for the recipient.
source: M1-CONT-EXT-006 (TCPA quiet hours); M1-CONT-EXT-007 (frequency caps); FCC TCPA Order (STOP keyword honor); DPIM D8 P.8.5 TCPAConsent; cross-module M1 + M7.3 + Layer 3 SDK
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: TCPA enforcement gate absent

### [9]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **thread state lifecycle**: `open` → `in_progress` (when staff replies) → `resolved` (staff marks resolved) → `closed` (auto-close after 30 days no-activity, configurable per practice). Re-engagement on a closed thread SHALL create a new thread or re-open the closed thread based on practice-configurable policy. `escalated` is a parallel state (escalation to office manager + WORM-audited per HIPAA minimum-necessary).
source: HTML m7 §7.2; cross-module M2.5 PracticeSettings
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: State machine absent

### [10]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `DPIM_ConversationThread` lifecycle events: `dpim.comm.conversation_thread.created` / `.message_appended` / `.assigned` / `.reassigned` / `.state_transitioned` / `.escalated` / `.channel_switched`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifier tokenized; message-body NEVER in event payload (back-reference to `DPIM_CommLog` UUID only). Subscribers (M7.1 CommLog co-emission, M11 thread-throughput analytics, M12.8 Insurance Fraud cross-validator on suspicious comm patterns) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

### [11]
reqId: M7-THR-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL restrict `DPIM_ConversationThread` participation: thread reads SHALL be allowed for assigned staff + practice clinical/front-office roles per HIPAA minimum-necessary; thread writes SHALL be allowed only for assigned staff (until reassigned) + `office_manager` + `owner`; thread-escalation SHALL require minimum `office_manager` role per OPA verdict matrix. `super_admin` cross-tenant access SHALL be WORM-audited per M2-TEN-EXT-005.
source: HIPAA Minimum Necessary doctrine (45 CFR 164.502(b)); DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; cross-module PS-6 + M2-TEN-EXT-005
phase: P1
status: Not Started

## phaseSummary

P3: 2

## 7.3
id: 7.3
name: Notifications (Outbound)
purpose: The outbound-send infrastructure that operationalizes every patient-facing communication initiated by OSCAR or staff. Per HTML m7 §7.3, M7.3 owns: multi-channel send (SMS / email / voice / push), templated content (via M7.7), cadence and retry, channel fall-through on no-response, send-time optimization, TCPA / CAN-SPAM enforcement. This is the **enforcement gate** for the TCPA chain (M1 stores consent → M7.3 evaluates → PS-5 Delivery executes) per Step 2 Decision #4. Largest non-Reputation sub-module by enforcement complexity.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Notification` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**)
- `DPIM_TCPAConsent` (D8 P.8.5 v3-aligned — per-phone-number-per-channel consent)
- `DPIM_OptOut` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**)
- `DPIM_SuppressionList` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_MessageTemplate` (M7.7); `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (M1 v3-new, D8 v3-new pending — patient-stated channel preferences); `DPIM_CommLog` (M7.1 — every send emits)
externalIntegrations: - **Twilio SMS** — primary SMS vendor (A2P 10DLC registration)
- **Twilio Voice** — primary voice vendor (text-to-speech outbound + voicemail drop)
- **SendGrid** — primary email vendor (CAN-SPAM compliance + bounce handling + open-pixel)
- **Azure Web PubSub** (via PS-5 Delivery) — push notifications
- **A2P 10DLC** — US carrier SMS marketing registration
- **FCC TCPA Order** — per-recipient frequency limits

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Notification` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records per outbound send. Each record SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `channel` (`sms` / `email` / `voice` / `push`), `purposeCategory` (per M7-LOG-EXT-006 enum), `template` Reference (M7.7), `renderedContent` (post-token-substitution body), `scheduledAt`, `sentAt`, `deliveryStatus` (`pending` / `enqueued` / `sent` / `delivered` / `failed` / `bounced` / `opted_out`), `retryCount`, `vendorMessageId` (Twilio MSID / SendGrid message ID / etc.), and `correlationId` to source CI (recall workflow / campaign / thread / etc.).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Notification v2); HTML m7 §7.3; Module Catalog §8.2.3
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **multi-channel send architecture** per HTML m7 §7.3 — SMS via Twilio (A2P 10DLC-registered for marketing), email via SendGrid (CAN-SPAM-conformant), voice via Twilio (text-to-speech + voicemail-drop), push via Azure Web PubSub (PS-5 Delivery integration). Channel selection at send time SHALL respect `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (M1 v3-new) channel-preference declarations subject to TCPA/CAN-SPAM gates.
source: HTML m7 §7.3; cross-module IF-4 (vendor credentials in Key Vault) + PS-5 Delivery + Twilio + SendGrid; cross-module M1 CommunicationPreference v3-new
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Vendor integrations absent; PS-5 Delivery wiring partial

### [2]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **templated content rendering** — every outbound `DPIM_Notification` SHALL reference a `DPIM_MessageTemplate` (M7.7) and SHALL substitute personalization tokens (patient first name, appointment time in practice timezone per M2-PRAC-EXT-007, provider name, balance amount in cents per OSCAR Three Absolute Rules, etc.) at send time. Token substitution SHALL be deterministic and auditable — rendered content SHALL be persisted on `DPIM_Notification.renderedContent` for later audit. Token-substitution failures SHALL fail-closed (notification queued for staff review, never sent with `{{unfilled_token}}` literal).
source: HTML m7 §7.3 ("templated content"); cross-module M7.7 + M2-PRAC-EXT-007 (timezone) + OSCAR money-as-cents rule
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Template-rendering engine absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **cadence and retry policy** per notification — initial send → retry on failure (default 3 retries with exponential backoff: 15min / 1hr / 4hr) → final-fail disposition. Cadence rules SHALL be per-`purposeCategory` configurable via `DPIM_PracticeSettings` (e.g., appointment confirmations retry aggressively; marketing campaigns retry minimally). Bounce / hard-fail dispositions SHALL NOT retry (e.g., SMS to invalid number, email bounced with hard-fail code) — final-fail SHALL emit `dpim.comm.notification.failed` for staff review.
source: HTML m7 §7.3 ("cadence and retry"); cross-module M2.5 PracticeSettings; vendor-API best practices
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Retry-policy engine absent

### [4]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **channel fall-through on no-response** per HTML m7 §7.3 — when patient does not respond to an SMS appointment reminder within the configured window, OSCAR SHALL fall through to email; if email no-response, fall through to voice (only during TCPA-permitted hours). Fall-through order SHALL respect `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (M1 v3-new) patient-stated channel ranking. Each fall-through send SHALL be a separate `DPIM_Notification` record correlated to the original via `correlationId`.
source: HTML m7 §7.3 ("channel fall-through on no-response"); cross-module M1 CommunicationPreference + M2.5 PracticeSettings
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Fall-through orchestration absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **send-time optimization** — per-patient learned best send time (e.g., patient typically reads SMS in evenings; email in mornings). Optimization SHALL use historical `DPIM_CommLog.disposition` data per patient + channel + time-of-day. Optimization SHALL respect TCPA quiet-hours (M1-CONT-EXT-006) as a hard constraint — never override quiet hours regardless of learned preference. Optimization SHALL be opt-in via `DPIM_PracticeSettings.aiFeatureToggles.sendTimeOptimization` (proposed D2 v3 addition); off by default for compliance-conservative practices.
source: HTML m7 §7.3 ("send-time optimization"); cross-module M1-CONT-EXT-006 (TCPA quiet hours) + M2.5 aiFeatureToggles
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Send-time-optimization ML model absent; toggle property absent in D2 v2.0.0

### [6]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **TCPA quiet-hours gate** at send time per M1-CONT-EXT-006 — default 8am–9pm patient-local time per TCPA + state expansions (FL/OK 8am–8pm stricter). Per-tenant override SHALL be supported per M1-CONT-EXT-006. Quiet-hours gate SHALL evaluate against the **location's** timezone (M2-LOC-EXT-005) for multi-location DSOs, NOT the practice's timezone. Quiet-hours violation SHALL fail-closed with `BLOCK` verdict + deferred-send to next allowed window.
source: TCPA 47 USC 227(b)(1)(B); FCC TCPA Order; M1-CONT-EXT-006; cross-module M2-LOC-EXT-005 per-location timezone; Layer 3 SDK verdict
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: TCPA quiet-hours enforcement gate absent

### [7]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **TCPA frequency caps** at send time per M1-CONT-EXT-007 — default 3 SMS/patient/day, 7 voice-calls/patient/week, configurable per-tenant. Caps SHALL apply **across `purposeCategory` boundaries** per FCC TCPA Order (marketing SMS + appointment reminder count toward same daily cap). Cap-breach SHALL fail-closed with `BLOCK` verdict + deferred-send to next allowed window OR staff-review-flag depending on practice policy.
source: M1-CONT-EXT-007 (TCPA frequency caps); FCC TCPA Order; cross-module Layer 3 SDK
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Frequency-cap counter absent

### [8]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL evaluate the `DPIM_TCPAConsent.{phone_number, channel, purpose}` triple (D8 P.8.5) at every outbound SMS/voice send — consent SHALL be present, non-expired, and matching the send's channel + purpose. Missing/expired/non-matching consent SHALL fail-closed with `BLOCK` verdict. Per-channel-per-purpose granularity SHALL be enforced — appointment-reminder consent SHALL NOT authorize marketing SMS. Consent capture surface lives in M1 (M1-CONT-EXT-* family); enforcement lives in M7.3.
source: DPIM D8 P.8.5 TCPAConsent; TCPA 47 USC 227; FCC TCPA Order; cross-module M1 + Layer 3 SDK
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: TCPAConsent evaluation gate absent

### [9]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL honor **STOP keyword opt-out** on inbound SMS per FCC TCPA Order — when patient SMS contains "STOP" / "UNSUBSCRIBE" / "CANCEL" / "OPT OUT" / "QUIT" / "END" (FCC-recognized opt-out keywords), OSCAR SHALL immediately: (a) create `DPIM_OptOut` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) entry for the (phone_number, channel) tuple; (b) block all subsequent outbound SMS to that phone-number-channel; (c) add to `DPIM_SuppressionList` (D8 v3-new) for marketing-channel suppression; (d) send single confirmation SMS per FCC requirement ("You have been unsubscribed. Reply HELP for help."). Subsequent override SHALL require explicit patient re-consent capture.
source: FCC TCPA Order (STOP keyword honor); CTIA Short Code Guidelines; cross-module M1 + Twilio inbound webhook + D8 OptOut + SuppressionList v3-new
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: STOP keyword detection absent

### [10]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **CAN-SPAM compliance** on every outbound marketing email per 15 USC 7701 — every email SHALL include: (a) accurate `From` header (practice business name); (b) accurate non-deceptive subject line; (c) physical postal address in footer; (d) one-click unsubscribe link; (e) honor unsubscribe within 10 business days per §7704(a)(4). Email content rendering SHALL inject these elements automatically from `DPIM_Practice.address` (M2-PRAC-EXT-001) + per-practice `DPIM_SuppressionList`. Unsubscribe-link click SHALL emit `dpim.comm.opt_out.created` and update `DPIM_OptOut` + `DPIM_SuppressionList`.
source: CAN-SPAM Act 15 USC 7701-7713; FTC CAN-SPAM Rule 16 CFR 316; cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-001 + D8 OptOut + SuppressionList
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Email-footer injection + unsubscribe-handler absent

### [11]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL register the practice's SMS-sender presence under **A2P 10DLC** before any marketing SMS send to US carriers (per CTIA + carrier-mandated US registration since 2023). Registration SHALL be performed via Twilio Trust Hub or equivalent; the practice's registered Brand + Campaign SHALL be tracked in `DPIM_Integration` (Governance v3-new) per M2-INTG-EXT-001 pattern. Unregistered marketing SMS sends SHALL fail-closed with `BLOCK` verdict; transactional SMS (appointment reminders, OTPs) SHALL fall under Twilio's transactional-exemption flow but SHALL still respect TCPA gates.
source: A2P 10DLC (CTIA registration); Twilio Trust Hub; FCC TCPA Order; cross-module M2-INTG-EXT-001 + IF-4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: A2P 10DLC registration not yet performed

### [12]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-013
requirement: OSCAR SHALL hand off the `delivery-execute` step to **PS-5 Delivery** (Azure Web PubSub-backed orchestration layer) for actual vendor-API invocation. M7.3 SHALL: (a) construct the send request with rendered content + recipient + vendor metadata; (b) evaluate all enforcement gates (TCPA quiet-hours, frequency caps, consent, opt-out, A2P 10DLC registration); (c) pass `BLOCK`-verdict notifications to deferred-send queue; (d) pass `AUTO_EXECUTE`-verdict notifications to PS-5 for vendor-API dispatch. PS-5 SHALL handle vendor-API retries + circuit breakers + per-vendor rate limits.
source: HTML m7 §7.3 integ ("PS-5 Delivery"); cross-module PS-5 Delivery + Layer 3 SDK verdict
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: PS-5 Delivery not yet extracted; M7.3-to-PS-5 contract absent

### [13]
reqId: M7-NOTIF-EXT-014
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `DPIM_Notification` lifecycle events: `dpim.comm.notification.scheduled` / `.enqueued` / `.sent` / `.delivered` / `.failed` / `.bounced` / `.opted_out_blocked` / `.tcpa_blocked` / `.frequency_capped` / `.deferred`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifier tokenized; rendered content body NEVER in event payload (back-reference to `DPIM_Notification.id` only). Subscribers (M7.1 CommLog co-emission, M11 deliverability analytics, M12.8 Insurance Fraud cross-validator on suspicious-volume marketing patterns, PS-4 Sentinel Behavioral dimension on opt-out spike detection) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary; cross-module M11 + M12 + PS-4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

## 7.4
id: 7.4
name: Inbound Referrals
purpose: The inbound referral intake layer — when an external provider refers a patient to the practice, M7.4 owns: source identification (matching to `DPIM_ReferralPartner` from M2.9 or creating a new `DPIM_LeadSource`), packet ingestion (records / imaging / narrative via FHIR Dental DEX, Direct Messaging, or fax with OCR), acknowledgement workflow back to referring provider, fast-track new-patient onboarding, and ROI tracking per referral source. Per HTML m7 §7.4, this surface feeds M2.9 Referral Partner stats (M2-REF-EXT-005 `referralsInbound` counter) and M11 Analytics referral-attribution dashboards.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_InboundReferral` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**)
- `DPIM_LeadSource` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_ReferralPartner` (M2.9); `DPIM_Patient` + `DPIM_Lead` (M1); `DPIM_DICOMStudy` (M5.8 — for imaging packet ingestion); `DPIM_ScannedDocument` (M8 — for fax-OCR-ingested narrative)
externalIntegrations: - **FHIR Dental Data Exchange Implementation Guide** — primary inbound referral protocol (`Referral` / `ConsultNote` / `ServiceRequest` resources)
- **Direct Messaging Protocol** — HIPAA-secure inter-provider email-equivalent (per ONC Direct standards)
- **Fax with OCR** — legacy fallback for non-FHIR-capable referring providers
- **CMS NPPES** — referring-provider NPI Type 1 validation (cross-module M2-INTG-EXT-002)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_InboundReferral` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records per inbound referral event. Each record SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1 — created if not exists), `referralPartner` Reference (M2.9 — created if not exists), `leadSource` Reference (D8 v3-new — captures non-`ReferralPartner` source attribution like Google Ads, patient-as-source, walk-in, etc.), `inboundChannel` (`fhir_dde` / `direct_messaging` / `fax` / `email` / `phone` / `portal_form`), `referralReason` (free text + optional ICD-10-CM), `urgency` (`emergency` / `urgent` / `routine`), `receivedAt`, `acknowledgedAt`, `firstAppointmentAt`, `state` (`received` / `acknowledged` / `scheduled` / `completed` / `lost`).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (InboundReferral v2); HTML m7 §7.4; Module Catalog §8.2.4
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **source identification** per HTML m7 §7.4 — inbound referrals SHALL match against the M2.9 `DPIM_ReferralPartner` registry first (provider-as-source); when no match, source SHALL be classified to `DPIM_LeadSource` (D8 v3-new) — taxonomy: `google_ads` / `google_organic` / `social_media` / `patient_referral` / `community_event` / `insurance_directory` / `walk_in` / `other`. Match-failure (unknown referring provider with no LeadSource correlation) SHALL surface to front-office for manual source-tagging.
source: HTML m7 §7.4 ("Source identification"); cross-module M2-REF-EXT-001 + D8 LeadSource v3-new
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Source-attribution engine absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **FHIR Dental DEX inbound** per HTML m7 §7.5 integ ("FHIR DDE") and HTML m7 §7.4 packet-ingestion implication. Inbound FHIR Bundles SHALL be received via authenticated endpoint (mTLS, per M2-REF-EXT-007 `acceptsOSCARReferrals` flag on referring `DPIM_ReferralPartner`). Bundle SHALL contain: (a) `Patient` resource (parsed to create/match M1 `DPIM_Patient`); (b) `ServiceRequest` (referral indication); (c) optional `DiagnosticReport` (clinical context); (d) optional `ImagingStudy` (cross-module M5.8 DICOM Study ingestion); (e) `Composition` (clinical-note bundle).
source: HTML m7 §7.5 integ (FHIR DDE inbound applies to 7.4 too); FHIR R4 + FHIR Dental Data Exchange IG; cross-module M2-REF-EXT-007 + M1 + M5.8
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: FHIR DDE inbound endpoint absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **Direct Messaging Protocol inbound** per ONC Direct standards — secure inter-provider email-equivalent with XDM (Cross-Enterprise Document Media Interchange) attachment bundling. Direct messages SHALL be received via OSCAR's HISP (Health Information Service Provider — vendor TBD with HIPAA BAA); attached documents SHALL be classified (PDF clinical note, DICOM Study, FHIR Bundle attachment, etc.) and ingested per content type.
source: ONC Direct Project standards; ASTM Standard ED-04 (XDM); cross-module M2.11 BAA + IF-4 HISP credentials
phase: P3

### [4]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **fax-with-OCR fallback** for non-FHIR/non-Direct-capable referring providers — inbound faxes SHALL be received via cloud-fax provider (Phaxio / SRFax / vendor TBD with BAA); fax PDF SHALL be OCR'd to extract patient demographics + referring provider + reason; OCR-extracted text SHALL surface to front-office for manual review before `DPIM_InboundReferral` creation. Fax retention SHALL respect M8 `DPIM_RetentionPolicy` (NO-DATA) per state record-retention requirements. Cross-module with M8 `DPIM_ScannedDocument`.
source: HTML m7 §7.4 packet-ingestion implication; cross-module M8 ScannedDocument (NO-DATA) + cloud-fax BAA vendor

### [5]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-006

Acknowledgement SHALL include: confirmed receipt, assigned-staff name (M10 referral-coordinator role), and provisional first-appointment date (if scheduled). Acknowledgement event SHALL emit `dpim.comm.inbound_referral.acknowledged`.
source: HTML m7 §7.4 ("acknowledgement workflow"); FHIR DDE acknowledgement pattern; cross-module M10 referral-coordinator role

### [6]

Duplicate-patient candidate detection SHALL run per M1 `DPIM_DuplicatePatientCandidate` (v3-new) before patient creation.

### [7]
reqId: M7-REFIN-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **ROI tracking per referral source** per HTML m7 §7.4 — each `DPIM_InboundReferral` SHALL track downstream outcomes: did the patient schedule? (state transition `received` → `scheduled`); did the patient complete first appointment? (state → `completed`); what is the lifetime-revenue contribution from the patient? (cross-module M6 + M11 Analytics rollup). ROI metrics SHALL roll up to `DPIM_ReferralPartner.conversionRate` (M2-REF-EXT-005) at the source level. Lost referrals (state → `lost`) SHALL capture loss-reason (no-response / scheduled-but-no-show / scheduled-but-cancelled / etc.) for source-quality analytics.
source: HTML m7 §7.4 ("ROI tracking per referral source"); cross-module M2-REF-EXT-005 conversionRate + M6 lifetime-revenue + M11 Analytics
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: ROI rollup pipeline absent

## 7.5
id: 7.5
name: Outbound Referrals
purpose: The outbound referral generation layer — when the practice refers a patient to an external specialist or provider. Per HTML m7 §7.5, M7.5 owns: specialist selection (in-network status, geography, prior-outcomes), packet generation (clinical notes from M4, imaging from M5, attachments from M8), secure transmission via FHIR Dental Data Exchange or Direct Messaging, outcome tracking, and loop closure (did the patient see the specialist? what was the outcome? did they return?). Cross-module with M2.9 Referral Partners (specialist registry + anti-kickback compliance), M4 Clinical (referral note generation), and M5.12 Image Sharing (DICOM packet bundling).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_OutboundReferral` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_ReferralPartner` (M2.9 — specialist registry); `DPIM_ReferralNote` (M4 v2 — clinical note composition); `DPIM_DICOMStudy` (M5.8 — imaging packet); `DPIM_Attachment` (M8 v2 — additional attachments); `DPIM_OutboundReferral` ← `DPIM_InboundReferral` reciprocity (when specialist refers back, M7.4 creates the inbound side)
externalIntegrations: - **FHIR Dental Data Exchange Implementation Guide** — primary outbound referral protocol (`Patient` + `ServiceRequest` + `DiagnosticReport` + `ImagingStudy` + `Composition` Bundle per M5-SHARE-EXT-007 pattern)
- **Direct Messaging Protocol** — secure inter-provider email-equivalent fallback
- **Fax / print / email** — non-Direct-capable specialist fallback (Phase 2)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_OutboundReferral` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records per outbound referral event. Each record SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `referralPartner` Reference (M2.9 — selected specialist), `referringPractitioner` Reference (M10), `clinicalIndication` (free text + optional ICD-10-CM diagnosis code), `urgency` (`emergency` / `urgent` / `routine`), `outboundChannel` (`fhir_dde` / `direct_messaging` / `fax` / `email` / `print`), `bundleContents[]` (BackboneElement[] — referenced clinical note + imaging studies + attachments), `sentAt`, `acknowledgedAt`, `outcomeReceivedAt`, `state` (`drafted` / `sent` / `acknowledged` / `outcome_received` / `closed` / `lost_to_followup`).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (OutboundReferral v2); HTML m7 §7.5; Module Catalog §8.2.5
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **specialist selection** per HTML m7 §7.5 — the referral workflow SHALL present a ranked list of `DPIM_ReferralPartner` (M2.9) candidates filtered by: (a) `partnerType=specialist` and matching `specialty` (NUCC taxonomy per M2-REF-EXT-003); (b) `inNetworkStatus` for the patient's `DPIM_Coverage` (M6); (c) `geoCoordinates` proximity to patient address (M2-REF-EXT-008 composite ranking with `conversionRate` and `acceptsOSCARReferrals` capability); (d) prior outcomes (`DPIM_ReferralPartner.conversionRate` + practice-curated quality notes). Ranking SHALL be deterministic + auditable.
source: HTML m7 §7.5 ("Specialist selection (in-network, geography, prior outcomes)"); cross-module M2-REF-EXT-003 + M2-REF-EXT-008 + M6 Coverage
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Selection-ranking engine absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **packet generation** per HTML m7 §7.5 — referral packet SHALL compose: (a) clinical referral note from M4 (`DPIM_ReferralNote` v2 — generated from `DPIM_Encounter` + `DPIM_TreatmentPlan` context); (b) relevant imaging via M5.12 (`DPIM_DICOMStudy` references with annotations + DICOM Presentation State per M5-SHARE-EXT-005); (c) supporting attachments (lab results / prior referrals / consents) from M8 (`DPIM_Attachment`); (d) patient summary (demographics, allergies, medications). Packet SHALL be reviewed by referring practitioner before send via `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` verdict gate.
source: HTML m7 §7.5 ("packet generation (notes, imaging, attachments)"); cross-module M4 ReferralNote + M5.12 + M8 + M1
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Packet-composition workflow absent; M4 + M8 cross-module gaps

### [3]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **FHIR Dental DEX outbound** as the primary transmission channel — when target `DPIM_ReferralPartner.acceptsOSCARReferrals=true` AND `fhirEndpoint` is set (M2-REF-EXT-007), OSCAR SHALL generate a FHIR R4 Bundle per FHIR Dental Data Exchange Implementation Guide and POST to the partner's endpoint with mTLS authentication. Bundle SHALL conform to M5-SHARE-EXT-007 structure: `Patient` + `ServiceRequest` (referral indication) + `DiagnosticReport` (clinical context) + `ImagingStudy[]` (M5.12 references — reference-only per OQ-5.12.3, not embedded binary) + `Composition` (clinical-note bundle).
source: HTML m7 §7.5 integ ("FHIR DDE"); FHIR R4 + FHIR Dental Data Exchange IG; cross-module M2-REF-EXT-007 + M5-SHARE-EXT-007 + Layer 3 SDK
phase: P3

reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-005

source: ONC Direct Project standards; ASTM ED-04 (XDM); cross-module M2.11 BAA + IF-4 HISP credentials + M5-PACS-EXT-012 + M7-REFIN-EXT-004 vendor selection

### [5]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **fax / email / print fallback** for non-FHIR + non-Direct-capable specialists per HTML m7 §7.5 — when `acceptsOSCARReferrals=false`, M7.5 SHALL: (a) generate printable referral packet (PDF clinical note + DICOM CD/USB export per M5-SHARE-EXT-006 + attachment bundle); (b) deliver via fax (cloud-fax vendor per M7-REFIN-EXT-005 selection) when specialist has fax-on-file; (c) deliver via secure-link email per M5-SHARE-EXT-001 pattern when specialist accepts email; (d) print + mail when neither channel available.
source: HTML m7 §7.5 packet-generation implication; cross-module M5-SHARE-EXT-001 + M5-SHARE-EXT-006 + M7-REFIN-EXT-005 vendor + M2-REF-EXT-007 capability

### [6]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **outcome tracking** per HTML m7 §7.5 — `DPIM_OutboundReferral.state` SHALL transition through `drafted` → `sent` → `acknowledged` (specialist confirms receipt) → `outcome_received` (specialist reports back with consult note / treatment plan / follow-up recommendation) → `closed` (patient outcome documented). Outcome reception MAY arrive via: (a) FHIR DDE inbound (cross-module M7.4 creates a reciprocal `DPIM_InboundReferral` linked to the original outbound); (b) Direct Messaging inbound; (c) fax inbound (OCR per M7-REFIN-EXT-005); (d) manual staff entry. Outcomes SHALL be linked to `DPIM_OutboundReferral.outcomeReceivedAt` and the linked `DPIM_InboundReferral.id`.
source: HTML m7 §7.5 ("outcome tracking, loop closure"); cross-module M7.4 reciprocal flow
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Loop-closure pipeline absent

### [7]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL update cross-module `DPIM_ReferralPartner.referralsOutbound` counter (M2-REF-EXT-005) on every `DPIM_OutboundReferral.sent` event. Successful outcome (state → `closed` with positive outcome) SHALL contribute to `DPIM_ReferralPartner.outcomeQualityScore` (proposed D2 v3 addition — track for Finding #5). Lost-to-followup (state → `lost_to_followup`) SHALL flag the specialist for practice-policy review (over-threshold loss-rate may indicate poor specialist-quality match).
source: M2-REF-EXT-005; cross-module M2.9 ReferralPartner; D2 v3 update needed for `outcomeQualityScore`
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Counter-update + outcome-quality tracking absent

### [8]
reqId: M7-REFOUT-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface **anti-kickback compliance** at outbound-referral time per Stark Law (42 USC 1395nn) and Anti-Kickback Statute (42 CFR 411 Subpart J) — when target `DPIM_ReferralPartner.compensationRelationship` ≠ `none` (M2-REF-EXT-009), the referral generation workflow SHALL surface a compliance warning to the referring practitioner BEFORE sending: "This specialist has a declared compensation relationship of type {type}. Verify clinical justification per anti-kickback safe harbors before proceeding." Acknowledgement SHALL be `full-delta` audited via PS-7. Cross-module with **M11 IntegrityAlerts surfacing → M12.9 Referral & Kickback Compliance investigation** per M2-REF-EXT-009 routing.
source: Stark Law 42 USC 1395nn; Anti-Kickback Statute 42 CFR 411 Subpart J; M2-REF-EXT-009; cross-module M11 (surface) + M12.9 (investigate) + PS-7 WORM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `compensationRelationship` flag-driven workflow absent; M12 NO-DATA

## 7.6
id: 7.6
name: Call Records (Telephony)
purpose: The voice-channel record-keeping layer — inbound and outbound calls captured with caller-ID match-to-patient, optional consented recording, AI transcription, disposition tagging, voicemail-to-text-to-ActionCard, and call analytics. Per HTML m7 §7.6, M7.6 owns: inbound caller-ID popup with patient match, new-caller lead capture, consented call recording, AI transcription, disposition tagging, voicemail-to-text → ActionCard, call analytics. PHI handling per Step 2 Decision #8 (M5.7 photo pattern — PS-2 Agent Orchestrator boundary; no LLM context without explicit clinician-initiated tool call). State recording-consent varies (one-party vs all-party) — verbal/recorded notification at call start required in all-party-consent states.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_CallRecord` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**)
- `DPIM_CallTranscription` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — AI-transcribed call text
- `DPIM_CallDisposition` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — call outcome tagging
- `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new) — AI transcription provenance per OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope
- Cross-module: `DPIM_Lead` (M1 v3-new — for unknown-caller capture); `DPIM_Patient` (M1); `DPIM_CommLog` (M7.1 — every call emits)
externalIntegrations: - **VoIP 4-vendor matrix** (single integration req with capability matrix per M5 8-vendor sensor pattern):
  - **Mango Voice** (dental-industry-specific VoIP)
  - **Weave** (dental-industry-specific VoIP + patient comms platform — also competes with M7.3)
  - **RingCentral** (general-business VoIP)
  - **NexHealth** (PMS-integrated VoIP — overlaps with M3 scheduler integration)
- **AI Transcription** — Azure OpenAI Whisper recommended per OQ-7.6.3 (Architectural-decision item #4) — BAA required
- **State recording-consent laws** — federal one-party consent (default) + all-party-consent states (CA, FL, IL, MD, MA, MT, NV, NH, PA, WA + DC at minimum — verify against current state-law matrix during implementation; states change)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_CallRecord` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records per call event. Each record SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**, optional — null for unmatched new-callers), `lead` Reference (M1 `DPIM_Lead` v3-new — created for unmatched callers), `direction` (`inbound` / `outbound`), `vendor` (`mango_voice` / `weave` / `ringcentral` / `nexhealth` / `other`), `vendorCallSid`, `callerPhoneNumber`, `practitionerOrStaff` Reference (M10 — who answered/initiated), `startedAt`, `endedAt`, `durationSeconds`, `disposition` Reference (`DPIM_CallDisposition` v3-new), `recordingAvailable` boolean, `recordingUri` (Key Vault-referenced URI to encrypted audio blob if recorded), `transcription` Reference (`DPIM_CallTranscription` v3-new if AI-transcribed).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (CallRecord v2); HTML m7 §7.6; Module Catalog §8.2.6
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate with **VoIP 4-vendor matrix** per HTML m7 §7.6 — Mango Voice · Weave · RingCentral · NexHealth — via vendor-pluggable bridge architecture (per M5 sensor SDK matrix pattern, M5-SENS-EXT-002). Single `DPIM_Integration` (Governance v3-new) entry per vendor with capability-matrix BackboneElement: `{vendor, supportsInboundCallEvents, supportsOutboundCallInitiation, supportsCallRecording, supportsRealTimeTranscription, dialerProtocol, webhookProtocol, baaStatus}`. Practice's selected VoIP vendor SHALL be configurable per `DPIM_PracticeSettings.voipVendor` (proposed D2 v3 addition). Per OQ-7.6.2 / Architectural-decision item #3: Weave + NexHealth default-supported (clinical context-aware); RingCentral fallback (general-business); Mango Voice optional.
source: HTML m7 §7.6 ("VoIP (Mango Voice · Weave · RingCentral · NexHealth)"); cross-module M2-INTG-EXT-001 + M5-SENS-EXT-002 pattern + IF-4 vendor credentials
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: VoIP integrations absent; vendor selection per practice deferred

### [2]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **inbound caller-ID popup with patient match** per HTML m7 §7.6 — on inbound call, VoIP vendor webhook SHALL fire to OSCAR with caller phone number; OSCAR SHALL: (a) look up `DPIM_Patient` by phone number (M1.2 patient contact); (b) on match, push WebSocket event via PS-5 Delivery to operatory + front-office workstations with patient context (chart open intent, last visit, balance, upcoming appointment); (c) on no-match, surface new-caller capture form to front-office for `DPIM_Lead` (M1 v3-new) creation. Patient-match popup latency SHALL be < 3 seconds from VoIP webhook to workstation display.
source: HTML m7 §7.6 ("Inbound caller-ID popup with patient match, new-caller lead capture"); cross-module M1.2 + M1 Lead v3-new + PS-5 Delivery
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Webhook receiver + caller-ID popup workflow absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **consented call recording** per HTML m7 §7.6 — when `DPIM_PracticeSettings.callRecordingEnabled=true` (proposed D2 v3 addition) AND state-law permits, recording SHALL be initiated automatically per call. **State recording-consent law SHALL be honored** — federal one-party consent (default) + all-party-consent states (CA, FL, IL, MD, MA, MT, NV, NH, PA, WA + DC at minimum, **state law matrix subject to change — verify current state-by-state at implementation time and store in `DPIM_StateRecordingConsentRule` (proposed Governance v3-new per Architectural-decision item #2 — joins `DPIM_StateDentalRule` from M5-FMX-EXT-005 in state-regulatory-rule cluster)**). In all-party-consent states, OSCAR SHALL play an automated verbal/recorded notification at call start: "This call may be recorded for quality assurance purposes." Recording without notification in all-party-consent states SHALL be a legal violation — fail-closed `BLOCK` if state matrix lookup fails.
source: State recording-consent laws (varies by state — Federal Wiretap Act 18 USC §2511 + state extensions); HTML m7 §7.6 ("consented call recording"); cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-001 (state from practice address) + D2 v3 + new Governance class
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: State-law matrix not modeled; recording-consent gate absent

### [4]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL store call recordings in **WORM Azure Blob** with same retention discipline as imaging (M5.8 PACS pattern) — state dental record retention rules (7–10 years post-last-encounter) apply per M5-PACS-EXT-006. Recording URIs SHALL be referenced by Key Vault handle (`agentic-access: blocked` per `DPIM_Clearinghouse.loginId` pattern at M2-CLR-EXT-003); direct blob URLs SHALL NOT be exposed. Recording playback SHALL be via OSCAR-authenticated API surface only (no Azure Blob SAS per M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS policy).
source: M5-PACS-EXT-006 retention pattern; M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS policy; HIPAA Safe Harbor; cross-module IF-2 WORM Blob + IF-4 KV
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Recording storage tier absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **AI transcription** per HTML m7 §7.6 — recorded calls SHALL be transcribed asynchronously via Azure OpenAI Whisper (recommended per OQ-7.6.3 / Architectural-decision item #4 — ecosystem coherence with IF-7) to produce `DPIM_CallTranscription` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records. Per Step 2 Decision #8 (Flag #8 ack) — transcriptions are **PHI**, treated identically to M5.7 IntraOralPhoto pattern. Each transcription SHALL reference a `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` per DPIM v3 §1.7 (M5-AI-EXT-001 pattern, retrofitted per OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope per M7-DPIM-EXT-003). Transcription invocation SHALL route through PS-2 Agent Orchestrator with PHI boundary enforcement.
source: HTML m7 §7.6 ("AI transcription"); DPIM v3 §1.7 AI provenance; Step 2 Decision #8; cross-module PS-2 + M5-AI-EXT-001 + Azure OpenAI Whisper BAA
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Transcription pipeline absent; AI provenance retrofit pending

### [6]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **call recording + transcription PHI boundary** per Step 2 Decision #8 — recordings (audio) and transcriptions (text) SHALL: (a) be tenant-scoped under RLS-equivalent access control; (b) NOT enter LLM context window without explicit clinician-initiated tool call (PS-2 Agent Orchestrator boundary per `services/agent-orchestrator/app/phi_boundary.py`); (c) be tokenized at SDK validation gate when event-emitted (back-reference UID only — never raw transcription text in event payload); (d) be subject to M5.7 photo-data PHI rules. Patient or representative MAY request transcription export per Cures Act §4002 + HIPAA 45 CFR 164.524 (cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-012 fulfillment).
source: HIPAA Safe Harbor §164.514; Cures Act §4002; HIPAA 45 CFR 164.524; OSCAR PHI boundary spec; M5-PHOTO-EXT-006 pattern; cross-module PS-2 + M2-TEN-EXT-012
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: PHI boundary on audio + transcription not specifically wired

### [7]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **disposition tagging** per HTML m7 §7.6 via `DPIM_CallDisposition` (D8 v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records — every call SHALL have a closing disposition: `appointment_scheduled` / `appointment_rescheduled` / `appointment_cancelled` / `clinical_question_answered` / `billing_question_resolved` / `complaint_received` (→ M7.15) / `referral_received` (→ M7.4) / `voicemail_left` / `transferred` / `no_answer` / `wrong_number` / `marketing_solicitation` / `other`. Disposition selection SHALL be required before call-record finalization; AI-suggested disposition (from transcription analysis) SHALL be available per `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` verdict (cross-module M5.9 AI pattern).
source: HTML m7 §7.6 ("disposition tagging"); DPIM D8 v3-new CallDisposition; cross-module M5-AI-EXT-001 pattern
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Disposition taxonomy + workflow absent

### [8]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **voicemail-to-text → ActionCard** per HTML m7 §7.6 — when inbound call results in voicemail (disposition `voicemail_left`), OSCAR SHALL: (a) transcribe voicemail audio via the same AI transcription pipeline (M7-CALL-EXT-006); (b) generate M11 `DPIM_ActionCard` ("Voicemail from {patient_name | unknown caller}: {transcription_summary}") routed to front-office persona; (c) include transcription preview + audio-playback link in ActionCard; (d) trigger callback-followup cadence if `DPIM_PatientCommunicationPreference.voicemailCallbackPreferred=true`. ActionCard generation SHALL respect `aiFeatureToggles.actionCards` master toggle (M2-SET-EXT-011).
source: HTML m7 §7.6 ("voicemail-to-text → action card"); cross-module M11 ActionCard + M2-SET-EXT-011 + AI transcription pipeline
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Voicemail-to-ActionCard pipeline absent

### [9]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **call analytics** per HTML m7 §7.6 — call volume per channel (vendor), call duration distribution, disposition mix, inbound-to-outbound ratio, peak-hour analysis, per-staff call-handling metrics (volume / average duration / disposition mix). Analytics SHALL feed M11 Practice Pulse + M11 Staffing Optimization dashboards. Per-staff metrics SHALL be aggregate (anonymized at DSO-rollup level per M2-DSO-EXT-003) to avoid surveillance-style monitoring at individual-staff granularity outside the originating practice. **Surveillance-disclosure considerations carry-forward to M10 per OQ-7.6.6 state-employment-law cluster.**
source: HTML m7 §7.6 ("call analytics"); cross-module M10 (per-staff) + M11 (Practice Pulse) + M2.1 DSO rollup
phase: P2

### [10]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit `DPIM_CallRecord` lifecycle events: `dpim.comm.call_record.initiated` / `.connected` / `.completed` / `.voicemail_left` / `.transcription_completed` / `.disposition_tagged`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — caller phone number tokenized; patient identifier tokenized; transcription text NEVER in event payload (back-reference UID only); recording URI NEVER in event payload. Subscribers (M7.1 CommLog co-emission, M11 call analytics, M12.7 User Behavior Analytics on suspicious call patterns, PS-5 Delivery for caller-ID popup) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; SDK validate_no_phi boundary; cross-module M11 + M12.7
phase: P1

### [11]
reqId: M7-CALL-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL restrict `DPIM_CallRecord` reads + recording-playback to: (a) call participants (the assigned-staff who handled the call + their direct supervisor); (b) `office_manager` and `owner` roles for compliance review; (c) `super_admin` cross-tenant access (WORM-audited per M2-TEN-EXT-005 + JIT PIM gate per M2-TEN-EXT-013 for cross-tenant playback). Recording-playback events SHALL be `full-delta` WORM-audited per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.528 accounting-of-disclosures. Transcription read events SHALL be similarly audited.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.528; DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix; cross-module PS-6 + PS-7 + M2-TEN-EXT-005 + M2-TEN-EXT-013
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: RBAC matrix not seeded; recording-playback audit absent

## 7.7
id: 7.7
name: Message Templates
purpose: The template library and personalization layer driving every M7 outbound communication. Per HTML m7 §7.7, M7.7 owns: library (recall, confirmation, statement, post-op, marketing) with version control, personalization tokens, compliance approval workflow, A/B testing, per-language variants. Integration via PS-1 Gateway (`ctx.admin_write` — Lane 1 administrative configuration per OSCAR Three Lanes). M7.3 Notifications references templates at render time; marketing templates additionally route through compliance-approval gates before activation.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_MessageTemplate` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3 — every send references a template); `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` (M7.10 — campaign-bound template variants); `DPIM_PracticeSettings` (M2.5 — practice-locale + AI feature toggles)
externalIntegrations: None directly. Internal-only template management. Compliance approval workflow may notify external compliance counsel (Phase 3 audit).

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_MessageTemplate` (v2-retained, **D8 property-doc-pending for v3 update**) records as the template library. Each template SHALL have UUID v7, `templateKey` (semantic identifier, e.g., `appointment_confirmation_v2`, `recall_overdue_30d`), `templateType` (`recall` / `appointment_confirmation` / `appointment_reminder` / `statement` / `post_op_instructions` / `marketing` / `survey_invitation` / `review_invitation` / `referral_thank_you` / `birthday` / `reactivation` / `complaint_response` / `clinical_followup` / `custom`), `channel` (`sms` / `email` / `voice` / `push` — channel-bound; a "same content, different channels" set is multiple templates linked by `templateFamily`), `language` (ISO 639-1 code, default `en`), `subject` (email only), `body` (raw content with token placeholders), `version` (semver), `status` (`draft` / `pending_approval` / `approved` / `active` / `deprecated`), and `practice` Reference (M2.2).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (MessageTemplate v2); HTML m7 §7.7; Module Catalog §8.2.7
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v2 class; D8 v3 pending; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **template library taxonomy** per HTML m7 §7.7 — system-seeded templates SHALL ship for each `templateType` × each language (Phase 1: English + Spanish minimum; per-tenant additional languages configurable). Practice-customized variants SHALL inherit from system-seeded baselines with override delta tracking. Default templates SHALL be TCPA/CAN-SPAM-compliant out-of-the-box (e.g., default marketing email template includes physical address + unsubscribe link).
source: HTML m7 §7.7 ("Library (recall, confirmation, statement, post-op, marketing)"); TCPA 47 USC 227; CAN-SPAM 15 USC 7701
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Template seed library not authored

### [2]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **immutable version control** per HTML m7 §7.7 — every template mutation SHALL produce a new version row; prior versions SHALL be retained per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b) audit-controls + state record-retention rules. Sent notifications SHALL freeze the `templateVersion` at send time (per M7-NOTIF-EXT-001 `renderedContent` capture) — historical re-render against the at-send-time version SHALL be supported for compliance review and patient inquiry. Template deletion SHALL be soft-delete (`status=deprecated`) preserving audit trail.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b); HTML m7 §7.7 ("version control"); cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-001 + PS-7 WORM
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Versioning + immutability absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **personalization tokens** per HTML m7 §7.7 — token syntax SHALL be `{{namespace.field}}` (e.g., `{{patient.firstName}}`, `{{appointment.startTime}}` rendered in practice timezone per M2-PRAC-EXT-007, `{{practice.name}}` per M2-PRAC-EXT-001, `{{balance.amount}}` rendered in dollars from cents per OSCAR money-as-cents rule, `{{provider.name}}`, `{{textToPayLink}}` per M7.3 cross-module). Token catalog SHALL be auditable + extensible. Unknown tokens at render time SHALL fail-closed (`BLOCK` verdict + staff-review-flag per M7-NOTIF-EXT-003).
source: HTML m7 §7.7 ("personalization tokens"); cross-module M2-PRAC-EXT-001/007 + M7-NOTIF-EXT-003
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Token rendering engine absent; token catalog not seeded

### [4]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **compliance approval workflow** per HTML m7 §7.7 — marketing-type templates (`templateType` ∈ {`marketing`, `survey_invitation`, `review_invitation`, `reactivation`}) SHALL transition `draft` → `pending_approval` → `approved` → `active`. Approval SHALL require `office_manager` or `owner` role per OPA verdict matrix. Non-marketing templates (transactional: appointment confirmations, statements, post-op instructions) MAY auto-promote `draft` → `active` when CAN-SPAM and TCPA-compliance-checks pass automated validation (presence of opt-out language where required, presence of disclosures, etc.). Approval transitions SHALL be `full-delta` audited via PS-7.
source: HTML m7 §7.7 ("compliance approval workflow"); TCPA + CAN-SPAM + HIPAA 45 CFR 164.508; cross-module PS-6 + PS-7 + DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Approval-workflow state machine absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **A/B testing** per HTML m7 §7.7 — multiple template versions MAY be linked via `templateFamily` + `experimentArm` attribution; M7.3 NOTIF sends SHALL alternate between arms per practice-configured split (e.g., 50/50, 80/20). Conversion metrics (click-through, response rate, appointment-scheduled-from-recall, payment-completed-from-statement) SHALL be tracked per arm via M11 Analytics. Statistically significant winner determination SHALL trigger M11 ActionCard ("Template variant B shows 18% higher recall conversion; promote to default?").
source: HTML m7 §7.7 ("A/B testing"); cross-module M11 + M2-SET-EXT-011 master toggle
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: A/B testing infrastructure absent

### [6]
reqId: M7-TMPL-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **per-language variants** per HTML m7 §7.7 — `DPIM_MessageTemplate.language` (ISO 639-1) SHALL drive variant selection. Language selection at send time SHALL respect `DPIM_Patient.preferredLanguage` (M1.1 demographics per USCDI v3). When no language-variant matches patient preference, OSCAR SHALL fall back to practice-default language (M2.5 default `en`). Variant divergence SHALL be tracked — practice-customized translations SHOULD pass through translation-review workflow before activation.
source: HTML m7 §7.7 ("per-language variants"); USCDI v3 patient demographics (language); cross-module M1.1 + M2.5
phase: P2

Analytics SHALL feed M11 dashboards and SHALL surface poorly-performing templates ("Template X has 32% bounce rate — investigate").

Subscribers (M7.3 NOTIF for cache invalidation on activation, M11 template analytics, PS-3 Practice Snapshot for tenant-config refresh) consume tokenized envelopes.

## 7.8
id: 7.8
name: Patient Portal Messaging
purpose: The patient-facing portal messaging surface — patient self-service interaction with the practice via secure web/mobile portal. Per HTML m7 §7.8, M7.8 owns: secure messaging, document sharing (forms / imaging / treatment plan), bill view + pay, family-account view (children), notification preferences. Distinct from M7.2 ConversationThread (which spans channels including portal); M7.8 is the **portal infrastructure** specifically — authentication, document-sharing surface, payment surface, family-account routing. **M7.8 owns NO primary DPIM class** — first sub-module in the orchestration-sub-module architectural pattern (also M7.12, M7.14) per Step 4 Architectural-decision item #10. Cross-module with i4 Patient Portal (UI), M1 PatientPortalAccount (auth), M8 Documents (NO-DATA — document sharing payload), M6 Bill Pay (payment processing).
dpimClasses: - M7.8 does NOT own a primary class — orchestration-sub-module pattern per Architectural-decision item #10
- Cross-module consumed: `DPIM_PatientPortalAccount` (M1 Identity v2), `DPIM_ConversationThread` (M7.2 — portal-scope threads), `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (M1 v3-new — notification preferences), `DPIM_FamilyLink` (M1.4 — family-account routing)
externalIntegrations: - **i4 Patient Portal** (Layer 5 Interface) — web portal + mobile app surface
- **Cures Act §4002 patient-right-of-access** — portal MUST satisfy patient-record-access requirement when combined with M2-TEN-EXT-012 DataExportRequest fulfillment

Unauthenticated access SHALL be blocked at portal-auth layer.

Failed-auth attempts SHALL be rate-limited per `DPIM_RateLimitPolicy` (M2-TEN-EXT-006); excessive failures SHALL trigger account lockout + email notification to patient. ## 7.9
id: 7.9
name: Reputation Engine
purpose: The **Phase 1 differentiator** per HTML m7 §7.9 — post-visit public-review-invitation workflow + public-review ingestion + sentiment analysis + response management. Operationalizes the FTC-compliant, multi-platform reputation surface (Google Reviews · Facebook · Healthgrades · BirdEye). Per Step 2 Decision #2 sharpening, review-gating (conditioning public-review invitation on internal survey rating) is **prohibited** by FTC 16 CFR 255 (Endorsement Guides 2023 update), FTC §5 (15 USC §45), Google Reviews Posting Guidelines, Yelp Content Guidelines, and Healthgrades Terms of Service. Negative ratings from internal post-visit surveys (M7.11) MAY route to manager for patient-care follow-up but SHALL NOT exclude patient from subsequent uniform public-review invitation. Architecturally, M7.9 invitation-list generator SHALL NOT accept `internal_survey_rating` as a filter predicate.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ReviewRequest` (DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 — v3-new; D8 P.8.1 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_PatientReview` (v3-new; D8 P.8.2 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_ReviewSentiment` (v3-new; D8 P.8.3 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_ReviewResponse` (v3-new; D8 P.8.4 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` (v3-new — for AI-suggested responses + sentiment analysis per OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3 — invitation send); `DPIM_CommLog` (M7.1 — universal logging); `DPIM_SurveyResponse` (M7.11 v3-new — internal surveys NOT linked to invitation filter); `DPIM_LeadSource` (M7.4 — for non-patient-initiated review submission attribution)
externalIntegrations: - **Google Reviews** — Google My Business API (Place ID + Review API; OAuth 2.0)
- **Facebook** — Facebook Graph API Page Reviews + Recommendations
- **Healthgrades** — Healthgrades partner API (provider-claim required)
- **BirdEye** — BirdEye aggregator API (cross-platform reputation management)
- **FTC 16 CFR 255** — Endorsement Guides (2023 update)
- **FTC §5 (15 USC §45)** — deceptive-practices prohibition
- Platform Terms of Service: Google Reviews Posting Guidelines, Yelp Content Guidelines, Healthgrades ToS

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ReviewRequest` (v3-new; D8 P.8.1 v3-aligned) records per public-review invitation. Each record SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**), `targetPlatform` (`google` / `facebook` / `healthgrades` / `birdeye`), `requestedAt`, `deliveredAt`, `deliveryChannel` (`sms` / `email` — per practice + patient channel preference), `practitioner` Reference (M10 — the provider whose care is being rated, optional), `appointmentContext` Reference (M3 — the visit driving the invitation, optional), `state` (`scheduled` / `sent` / `delivered` / `clicked` / `submitted` / `expired` / `opted_out`), and `correlationId` to source `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ReviewRequest v3-new); D8 P.8.1; Reputation Engine v2 §9 (Review Request Record); HTML m7 §7.9; Module Catalog §8.2.9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; D8 P.8.1 documented; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **uniform public-review invitation** per Step 2 Decision #2 — invitation cadence SHALL apply uniformly to all patients matching the practice's invitation criteria (post-visit, completed appointment, opted-in for marketing comms, within practice's defined cadence window). Invitation criteria SHALL be **rating-independent** — no internal-survey-rating filter, no sentiment-classification filter, no satisfaction-score filter. Invitations to negatively-rated patients SHALL be sent on the same cadence as invitations to positively-rated patients.
source: FTC 16 CFR 255 Endorsement Guides (2023 update); FTC §5 (15 USC §45); Google Reviews Posting Guidelines; Yelp Content Guidelines; Healthgrades ToS; Reputation Engine v2 §4 (Workflow: Review Request); Step 2 Decision #2 sharpening
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Invitation-list generator absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **multi-platform invitation routing** per Reputation Engine v2 §4 (Multi-Platform Landing Page) — invitations SHALL link to a per-tenant multi-platform landing page where patient selects review platform (Google / Facebook / Healthgrades) before redirecting to platform-native review surface. Per HTML m7 §7.9 4-platform matrix: Google Reviews (primary), Facebook Page Reviews, Healthgrades, BirdEye (aggregator covering additional platforms). Per-platform tracking SHALL preserve invitation→submission attribution via short-link tokenization (M7-NOTIF cross-ref + click-through tracking).
source: HTML m7 §7.9 (4-platform); Reputation Engine v2 §4 Multi-Platform Landing Page; cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-003 + Google My Business API + Facebook Graph API + Healthgrades + BirdEye
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-platform routing absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-004
requirement: **OSCAR SHALL NOT support review-gating patterns.** The public-review invitation list generator SHALL NOT accept `internal_survey_rating`, `sentiment_classification`, `nps_score`, or any other satisfaction-derived field as a filter predicate. Implementing review-gating violates **FTC 16 CFR 255 (Endorsement Guides, 2023 update)** Section 255.2 (Consumer Endorsements) which prohibits practices that "could distort the overall sentiment of the reviews available to consumers." Implementing review-gating violates **Google Reviews Posting Guidelines** explicitly prohibiting "soliciting reviews from customers in bulk … or selectively soliciting positive reviews." This requirement SHALL be enforced at the SDK validation gate — invitation-list-generator function signatures SHALL reject `rating`/`sentiment`/`nps`-typed filter predicates at compile time where possible, and SHALL fail-closed at runtime with `BLOCK` verdict.
source: **FTC 16 CFR 255 §255.2** (Endorsement Guides 2023 update — Consumer Endorsements); **FTC §5 (15 USC §45)** deceptive-practices prohibition; **Google Reviews Posting Guidelines** (review-gating section); **Yelp Content Guidelines**; **Healthgrades Terms of Service**; Step 2 Decision #2 sharpening; Reputation Engine v2 §4 alignment
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Anti-review-gating enforcement gate absent — **highest-compliance-risk M7 requirement**

### [4]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-017
requirement: OSCAR SHALL produce a **runtime audit trail per invitation-list generation** enumerating the filter predicates used. OPA policy (PS-6) SHALL evaluate the predicate set and `BLOCK` if any rating-conditioned, sentiment-conditioned, NPS-conditioned, or satisfaction-derived field is present. This runtime gate **supplements the compile-time function-signature gate per M7-REP-EXT-004** and provides the **primary compliance-evidence mechanism for FTC, state AG, and platform-compliance regulatory inquiry response** per M7-REP-EXT-016. Audit trail entries SHALL be `full-delta` WORM-audited via PS-7 with cryptographic hash chain per DPIM v3 §3.12 RAIVS. Every invitation-list generation operation SHALL produce exactly one audit entry; missing audit entry SHALL itself be a compliance violation surfaceable to M11 IntegrityAlerts.
source: Promoted from OQ-7.9.9 per Step 4 §4.7 OQ Resolutions; **FTC 16 CFR 255 §255.2** (Endorsement Guides 2023 update); **FTC §5 (15 USC §45)**; Google Reviews + Yelp + Healthgrades ToS; cross-module PS-6 + PS-7 + Layer 3 SDK + M7-REP-EXT-004 + M7-REP-EXT-016
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Runtime predicate-audit gate absent — **strongest compliance-evidence mechanism for regulatory-inquiry response**

### [5]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **practice-configurable invitation cadence and channel preferences** (replaces any rating-conditioned threshold language). Practice-configurable fields SHALL be: (a) post-visit delay before invitation send (default 2 business days, configurable 0–14 days); (b) preferred channel (SMS first / email first / patient-preferred per `DPIM_CommunicationPreference`); (c) one-send vs reminder (configurable 1–2 sends with practice-default cadence); (d) practitioner-attribution (invitation mentions specific provider by default or omits practitioner name). **No rating-conditioned field SHALL be configurable.** Per-practice cadence SHALL be in `DPIM_PracticeSettings.reviewInvitationCadence` (proposed D2 v3 addition).
source: Reputation Engine v2 §4 Request Frequency Limiting + Request Timing Best Practices; Step 2 Decision #2 (replaces "practice-configurable invitation-threshold" language); cross-module M2.5 D2 v3 update
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Cadence-config UI absent; `reviewInvitationCadence` property doc pending

### [6]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply **request-frequency limiting** per Reputation Engine v2 §4 Request Frequency Limiting — same patient SHALL NOT receive more than one review invitation per appointment instance; consecutive-appointment invitations SHALL respect practice-configured minimum gap (default 90 days between invitations for the same patient). TCPA frequency caps (M1-CONT-EXT-007) apply over-the-top per cross-channel reasonableness. Patients who have already submitted a public review for a given practice within configurable period (default 12 months) SHALL NOT receive new invitations to the same platform.
source: Reputation Engine v2 §4 Request Frequency Limiting; M1-CONT-EXT-007 TCPA caps; FCC TCPA Order; cross-module M1 + M7.3 NOTIF gates
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Frequency-counter absent

### [7]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL apply **request timing best practices** per Reputation Engine v2 §4 Request Timing Best Practices — post-visit invitation SHALL be sent during practice-configured optimal-time window (typically same-day evening or next-day morning per industry research on review-completion conversion). Send time SHALL respect TCPA quiet-hours (M7-NOTIF-EXT-007) as hard constraint. Per-patient send-time optimization (M7-NOTIF-EXT-006) MAY apply when enabled.
source: Reputation Engine v2 §4 Request Timing Best Practices; cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-006 + M7-NOTIF-EXT-007 TCPA quiet hours
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Timing-optimization integration absent

### [8]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL ingest **public patient reviews** per Reputation Engine v2 §5 (Workflow: Review Monitoring) — periodic polling of platform APIs (Google My Business, Facebook Graph, Healthgrades, BirdEye) SHALL fetch new reviews + sentiment scores; ingested reviews SHALL create `DPIM_PatientReview` (v3-new; D8 P.8.2 v3-aligned) records with `platform`, `platformReviewId`, `submittedAt`, `starRating` (1–5), `reviewText`, `reviewerDisplayName` (platform-provided, potentially anonymized), `practitionerMentioned` Reference (M10 — extracted via NLU if name appears in text), `relatedAppointment` Reference (M3 — best-effort correlation by date proximity to invitation). Ingestion SHALL emit `dpim.comm.patient_review.received` event.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (PatientReview v3-new); D8 P.8.2; Reputation Engine v2 §5 Workflow: Review Monitoring; HTML m7 §7.9 ingestion implication
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Platform-polling pipelines absent for all 4 platforms

### [9]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL surface **negative-review alerts** per Reputation Engine v2 §5 Alert Configuration — when a `DPIM_PatientReview.starRating` ≤ practice-configured threshold (default ≤ 3 stars), OSCAR SHALL: (a) generate M11 `DPIM_ActionCard` ("Negative {star_count}-star review on {platform} from {reviewer} mentioning {topic}"); (b) route to `office_manager` persona for response prioritization; (c) link to original `DPIM_ReviewRequest` if invitation→review correlation exists; (d) preserve the original public review submission **unmodified** — OSCAR SHALL NOT block, intercept, or attempt to remove negative reviews from public platforms (PROHIBITED pattern per Decision #2). Alert thresholds SHALL be configurable but threshold-based alerting SHALL be clearly distinct from invitation-list filtering (allowed vs prohibited).
source: Reputation Engine v2 §5 Alert Configuration; cross-module M11 ActionCard + M10 office_manager role; Step 2 Decision #2 distinction (alerting permitted; invitation-filtering prohibited)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Alert workflow absent

### [10]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-010
requirement: OSCAR SHALL compute **review sentiment + topic extraction** per Reputation Engine v2 §8 (Sentiment Analysis) — `DPIM_ReviewSentiment` (v3-new; D8 P.8.3 v3-aligned) records SHALL be produced per `DPIM_PatientReview` via AI inference (Azure OpenAI gpt-4o-mini recommended per Architectural-decision item #5). Sentiment classification: `positive` / `neutral` / `negative` / `mixed` with confidence score. Topic extraction: structured topic tags drawn from a dental-domain taxonomy (`wait_time`, `clinical_quality`, `staff_friendliness`, `cost_billing`, `cleanliness`, `pain_management`, `outcome`, `front_desk`, `parking_facilities`, `wait_room`, `communication`, `appointment_scheduling`, `other`). Each sentiment+topic inference SHALL reference a `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` per DPIM v3 §1.7 (M5-AI-EXT-001 pattern, retrofitted per OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ReviewSentiment v3-new); D8 P.8.3; Reputation Engine v2 §8 Sentiment Analysis + Topic Extraction; DPIM v3 §1.7 AI provenance; cross-module PS-2 + IF-7 + OQ-DPIM-M5-3
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: Sentiment NLU pipeline absent; AI provenance retrofit pending

### [11]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-011
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ReviewResponse` (v3-new; D8 P.8.4 v3-aligned) records per practice reply to a public review. Each response SHALL have UUID v7, `review` Reference (`DPIM_PatientReview`), `responseText`, `responseAuthor` Reference (M10), `responseTemplate` Reference (optional, M7.7 if using template per v2 §6.1 Response Template Categories), `submittedToplatformAt`, `platformResponseId`, `provenance` (`human` / `ai_suggested_human_confirmed`), `aiInferenceRun` Reference (when AI-drafted per M7-REP-EXT-012). Responses SHALL be submitted to public platforms via platform-native APIs; OSCAR SHALL track submission outcome (success / platform-rejected / pending).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ReviewResponse v3-new); D8 P.8.4; Reputation Engine v2 §6 Workflow: Response Management; HTML m7 §7.9 ("reply templates")
