### [12]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-012
requirement: OSCAR SHALL support **AI-suggested review responses** per Reputation Engine v2 §6 — for each `DPIM_PatientReview`, OSCAR MAY generate a sentiment-aware response draft via PS-2 Agent Orchestrator (per OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope). AI-drafted responses SHALL: (a) follow `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` verdict pattern (Lane 3 Agentic per OSCAR Three Lanes); (b) require human review + confirmation before public submission; (c) reference `DPIM_AIInferenceRun`; (d) respect M5-AI-EXT-012 PHI-tokenization pattern (review text + practice context tokenized before LLM call). AI-drafted responses SHALL NOT be auto-submitted to public platforms — `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` requires staff confirmation.
source: Reputation Engine v2 §6 Response Management; DPIM v3 §1.7 + §10 verdict matrix; OQ-DPIM-M5-3 expanded scope; cross-module PS-2 + Layer 3 SDK
phase: P3
status: Not Started
evidence: AI-response drafting absent

### [13]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-013
requirement: **OSCAR SHALL NOT intercept, block, modify, suppress, or attempt-to-remove patient-submitted reviews on public platforms** per Step 2 Decision #2 prohibited-patterns and platform Terms of Service. OSCAR SHALL: (a) treat public-platform review submission as patient-direct interaction with the platform (Google/Yelp/Facebook/Healthgrades — OSCAR is NOT a man-in-the-middle); (b) NOT request review-removal from platforms based on review content (only legitimate platform-level violations like spam/fake/defamatory MAY be reported per platform-specific dispute flows, and only with documented practice-counsel authorization); (c) NOT use platform admin-API capabilities (where they exist) to delete or hide individual negative reviews; (d) preserve full review text + history in `DPIM_PatientReview` even if removed from public platform. **This requirement is the PROHIBITED-pattern enforcement complement to M7-REP-EXT-004 review-gating prohibition.**
source: FTC 16 CFR 255 §255.2; FTC §5 (15 USC §45); Google Reviews Posting Guidelines; Yelp Content Guidelines; Healthgrades Terms of Service; Step 2 Decision #2 prohibited patterns
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Anti-suppression discipline absent in spec; needs explicit gate

### [14]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-014
requirement: OSCAR SHALL produce **reputation aggregation analytics** per HTML m7 §7.9 ("sentiment analytics") — per-platform star-rating distributions, per-practitioner rating breakdown, sentiment-trend over time, topic-frequency analysis (which topics drive positive vs negative ratings). Analytics SHALL feed M11 Practice Pulse + M11 Strategy Zone dashboards; per-practitioner ratings SHALL roll up to DSO-level per M2-DSO-EXT-003 (Reputation Engine v2 §10 API Overview supports cross-platform aggregation queries). Aggregate-level practitioner-rating metrics SHALL be DSO-scoped to avoid surveillance-style monitoring (cross-module OQ-7.6.6 M10 carry-forward — surveillance-disclosure laws).
source: Reputation Engine v2 §10 API Overview; HTML m7 §7.9 ("sentiment analytics"); cross-module M11 + M2.1 DSO rollup + M10 OQ-7.6.6 surveillance considerations
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Analytics pipeline absent

### [15]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-015
requirement: OSCAR SHALL emit Reputation Engine lifecycle events with **source-domain routing** per M5-DPIM-EXT-006 mixed-domain pattern (each event prefixed with source class's domain, not consuming module): `dpim.comm.review_request.scheduled` / `.sent` / `.delivered` / `.clicked` / `.submitted` / `.expired`; `dpim.comm.patient_review.received` / `.platform_updated`; `dpim.comm.review_sentiment.computed`; `dpim.comm.review_response.drafted` / `.submitted_to_platform`. **Events SHALL emit with tokenized PHI fields per the SDK validation gate** — patient identifier tokenized; review text never in event payload (back-reference UID only); reviewer display name from platform may be PHI-adjacent (HIPAA Safe Harbor) and tokenized. Subscribers (M7.1 CommLog co-emission, M11 reputation dashboards, M12.8 Insurance Fraud cross-validator on suspicious-review patterns — though M12.8 routing for review fraud is unusual, retained for completeness) consume tokenized envelopes.
source: DPIM v3 §6.2 event taxonomy; M5-DPIM-EXT-006 mixed-domain routing; SDK validate_no_phi boundary
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: `ACTION_TYPE_TO_EVENT` AG map gap (Finding 3)

### [16]
reqId: M7-REP-EXT-016
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **RBAC + compliance audit** on M7.9 operations — invitation cadence configuration SHALL be `office_manager` or `owner` (cross-module M2-SET-EXT-018 PracticeSettings RBAC pattern); response drafting + submission SHALL be `marketing_admin` / `office_manager` / `owner`; AI-response confirmation SHALL require minimum `marketing_admin` role per OPA verdict matrix. **All M7.9 operations SHALL be `full-delta` WORM-audited via PS-7** — invitation list generation, response submission, AI-draft acceptance, threshold-config changes all logged with cryptographic hash chain per DPIM v3 §3.12 RAIVS. Audit SHALL support **regulatory inquiry response** — FTC, state AG, or platform compliance officer requests for evidence that practice does NOT operate review-gating patterns.
source: FTC 16 CFR 255 audit-trail expectations; HIPAA 45 CFR 164.312(b); DPIM v3 §3.12 RAIVS; OQ-7.7.4 marketing_admin role; cross-module PS-6 + PS-7 + DPIM v3 §10 verdict matrix
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Compliance-audit trail absent; regulatory-inquiry response readiness untested

## 7.10
id: 7.10
name: Marketing Campaigns
purpose: The campaign infrastructure layer driving outbound multi-recipient outreach — review-invitations (M7.9), recall outreach (M7.12), reactivation campaigns (M7.13), and standalone marketing series (holiday / birthday / welcome / win-back). Per HTML m7 §7.10, M7.10 owns: campaign builder (segment + channel + cadence + content), patient segmentation (lifecycle / clinical / financial / risk), attribution, holiday / birthday / welcome series, win-back, suppression rules. TCPA + CAN-SPAM gates apply throughout (cross-module M7.3 enforcement chain). The multi-touch attribution architectural-decision item (OQ-7.4.4 / OQ-7.5.2 / Architectural-decision item #1) materializes here as the canonical `DPIM_CampaignAttribution` model.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` (v3-new; D8 P.8.6 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_PatientSegment` (v3-new; D8 P.8.7 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_CampaignAttribution` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**)
- `DPIM_LeadSource` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — for non-patient-source attribution per M7-REFIN-EXT-002
- `DPIM_ReactivationCampaign` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — specialized sub-class managed in M7.13
- Cross-module: `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3 — every campaign-send produces a Notification); `DPIM_MessageTemplate` (M7.7 — campaign-bound template variants); `DPIM_SuppressionList` + `DPIM_OptOut` (D8 v3-new, M7.3 suppression cross-ref)
externalIntegrations: - Twilio + SendGrid (M7.3 vendor matrix)
- A2P 10DLC registration verification (cross-module M2-INTG-EXT-001 + M7-NOTIF-EXT-012)
- M11 Analytics + PS-4 Sentinel (downstream consumers)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` (v3-new; D8 P.8.6 v3-aligned) records per active campaign. Each campaign SHALL have UUID v7, `campaignName`, `campaignType` (`recall_outreach` / `reactivation` / `birthday` / `welcome` / `holiday_seasonal` / `winback` / `review_invitation` / `educational` / `clinical_followup` / `event_announcement` / `custom`), `segment` Reference (`DPIM_PatientSegment`), `channels[]` (`sms` / `email` / `voice` / `push`), `template` Reference (M7.7), `cadence` (one-time / recurring schedule + recurrence rule), `state` (`draft` / `pending_approval` / `scheduled` / `active` / `paused` / `completed` / `cancelled`), `practice` Reference (M2.2).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (MarketingCampaign v3-new); D8 P.8.6 v3-aligned; HTML m7 §7.10; Module Catalog §8.2.10
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; D8 P.8.6 documented; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_PatientSegment` (v3-new; D8 P.8.7 v3-aligned) records as the patient-cohort definition layer. Each segment SHALL have UUID v7, `segmentName`, `segmentType` (`lifecycle` / `clinical` / `financial` / `risk` / `engagement` / `composite`), `filterExpression` (structured rule expression — list of attributes + operators + values), `computedSize` (count of patients matching, refreshed on segment-evaluation cadence), `practice` Reference (M2.2), and `lastEvaluatedAt`. **Per Decision #2 sharpening, `filterExpression` SHALL NOT accept review-related rating predicates (`internal_survey_rating`, `nps_score`, `csat_score`, `sentiment_classification`) when the segment is used by a `campaignType=review_invitation` campaign — same SDK-gate enforcement as M7-REP-EXT-004. Layer 4 of 4 in Decision #2 four-anchor defense-in-depth (compile gate EXT-004 + runtime audit EXT-017 + M7.11 architectural isolation + segment-filter gate here).**
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (PatientSegment v3-new); D8 P.8.7 v3-aligned; Step 2 Decision #2 + M7-REP-EXT-004 cross-ref; HTML m7 §7.10 ("patient segmentation (lifecycle / clinical / financial / risk)")
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; segment-filter engine absent; anti-gating enforcement gap

### [2]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL provide a **campaign builder** per HTML m7 §7.10 — UI workflow stitching segment + channel + cadence + content. Builder SHALL: (a) preview computed segment size before activation; (b) preview rendered template with sample patient data substitution per M7-TMPL-EXT-004; (c) preview estimated send-cost (A2P 10DLC per-message SMS cost + email cost) per OQ-7.3.4 transparent pass-through; (d) require `PROPOSE_AND_CONFIRM` verdict gate before transitioning `draft` → `pending_approval`.
source: HTML m7 §7.10 ("Campaign builder (segment + channel + cadence + content)"); cross-module M7-TMPL-EXT-004 + OQ-7.3.4 + Layer 3 SDK verdict
phase: P1

evidence: Builder UI absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL enforce **TCPA + CAN-SPAM compliance** on every campaign send via M7.3 cross-module gates (M7-NOTIF-EXT-007 quiet hours, M7-NOTIF-EXT-008 frequency caps, M7-NOTIF-EXT-009 TCPAConsent triple, M7-NOTIF-EXT-010 STOP keyword, M7-NOTIF-EXT-011 CAN-SPAM email compliance). Campaigns SHALL NOT bypass any TCPA gate regardless of campaign type. Suppression list (`DPIM_SuppressionList` + `DPIM_OptOut`) SHALL be evaluated against every recipient before send — opted-out recipients SHALL be excluded from final send list (audit-tracked: recipient excluded for reason = opted_out_channel).
source: TCPA 47 USC 227; CAN-SPAM 15 USC 7701; cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-007/008/009/010/011 + D8 SuppressionList + OptOut
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: TCPA+CAN-SPAM gate integration absent at campaign-send-orchestration layer

### [4]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-005

Transactional non-marketing campaigns (`campaignType=clinical_followup` / `recall_outreach` for clinical purpose) MAY proceed under transactional-exemption flow but still respect TCPA gates.

System-seeded campaigns SHALL ship for: **welcome series** (new-patient onboarding sequence — appointment-confirmation / pre-visit reminders / forms-completion); **birthday** (annual patient-birthday outreach); **holiday seasonal** (configurable per practice — common: Year-End Benefits, Spring Cleaning Reminder, Summer Smile, Holiday Recall); **win-back** (inactive-patient reactivation — handed off to M7.13 ReactivationCampaign); **review-invitation** (cross-module M7.9 per uniform-cadence pattern); **clinical-followup** (post-procedure check-in).

### [6]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-007
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **multi-touch campaign attribution** via `DPIM_CampaignAttribution` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — per outcome event (appointment scheduled, payment received, treatment plan accepted, review submitted, etc.), OSCAR SHALL attribute outcome credit across campaign touches in the conversion path. **Default attribution model SHALL be position-based 40/20/40** (40% first-touch + 40% last-touch + 20% even-split across middle touches) per industry-standard healthcare-referral-attribution research and Step 4 Architectural-decision item #1 (OQ-7.4.4 / OQ-7.5.2 cross-ref). Practice MAY override default model in `DPIM_PracticeSettings.attributionModel` (proposed D2 v3 addition). Alternative models SHALL include: `first_touch` / `last_touch` / `linear` / `time_decay` / `position_based_40_20_40` (default).
source: Step 4 Architectural-decision item #1; OQ-7.4.4 + OQ-7.5.2 carry-forward; industry healthcare-referral-attribution research; HTML m7 §7.10 ("attribution"); cross-module M2.5 D2 v3 update
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-touch attribution engine absent; `DPIM_CampaignAttribution` property doc pending

### [7]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-008
requirement: OSCAR SHALL produce **campaign attribution analytics** per HTML m7 §7.10 — per campaign: send volume, delivery rate, open rate (email), click-through rate, conversion rate per outcome metric (appointment-scheduled-from-recall, payment-received-from-statement, etc.), cost-per-conversion, ROI calculation (revenue contribution / campaign cost). Attribution rolls up per `DPIM_CampaignAttribution` model selection (M7-MKT-EXT-007). Analytics SHALL feed M11 Practice Pulse + Cost Control + Strategy Zone dashboards. Per-campaign + per-template + per-segment slicing SHALL be supported.
source: HTML m7 §7.10; cross-module M11 + M7-TMPL-EXT-008 template analytics
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Attribution-analytics pipeline absent

### [8]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-009
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **campaign approval workflow** — marketing campaign state transitions SHALL be: `draft` → `pending_approval` → `approved` → `scheduled` → `active` → `completed` (or `paused` / `cancelled` at any non-terminal state). Approval SHALL require `office_manager` or `owner` role (per OQ-7.7.4 marketing_admin role consideration); approval SHALL require explicit acknowledgement of: (a) segment computed size; (b) estimated send-cost; (c) TCPA/CAN-SPAM compliance check passed; (d) A2P 10DLC registration verified (for SMS). Approval transitions SHALL be `full-delta` audited via PS-7.
source: HTML m7 §7.10 (state lifecycle implication); cross-module PS-6 + PS-7 + DPIM v3 §10 + OQ-7.7.4 + Layer 3 SDK verdict
phase: P1

### [9]
reqId: M7-MKT-EXT-010

Per-campaign suppression rules SHALL support exclusions: (a) recently-contacted patients (within configurable cooldown window per `purposeCategory`); (b) appointment-imminent patients (don't send marketing during active treatment); (c) financial-hardship-flagged patients (don't send statement-related reminders during active payment plan); (d) family-account suppression coordination (don't double-send to multiple family members for family-shared events).

## 7.11
id: 7.11
name: Surveys & Satisfaction
purpose: The internal-patient-experience-feedback layer — post-visit surveys, NPS, CSAT, custom-builder, detractor drill-down, and trend reporting. Per HTML m7 §7.11, M7.11 surveys are explicitly **internal** — survey responses route to `office_manager` for service-recovery on negative ratings (PERMITTED per Decision #2) but **SHALL NOT be linked as a filter predicate to M7.9 public-review invitation list** (PROHIBITED per Decision #2 + M7-REP-EXT-004 SDK gate). Survey trend reporting feeds M11 Analytics + PS-4 Sentinel Behavioral dimension per HTML m7 §7.11 integ. **M7-SURV-EXT-009 is Decision #2 Layer 3 anchor (architectural isolation) completing the four-anchor defense-in-depth: EXT-004 (Layer 1 compile) + EXT-017 (Layer 2 runtime) + SURV-EXT-009 (Layer 3 isolation) + MKT-EXT-002 (Layer 4 segment-filter).**
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Survey` (v3-new; D8 P.8.8 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_SurveyResponse` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**)
- `DPIM_NPSResponse` (v3-new; D8 P.8.9 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_CSATResponse` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3 — survey-invitation send); `DPIM_PracticeRiskScore` (Platform v3-new — PS-4 Sentinel Behavioral dim feed); `DPIM_ActionCard` (M11 — detractor-routing)
externalIntegrations: None directly (internal survey infrastructure). Cross-module with PS-4 Sentinel for Behavioral dimension.

NPS scoring SHALL classify respondents per industry-standard NPS convention: `promoter` (9–10), `passive` (7–8), `detractor` (0–6). NPS Score = (% promoters) − (% detractors), computed practice-wide + per-provider + per-time-window. NPS trends SHALL surface in M11 Practice Pulse.

## 7.12
id: 7.12
name: Recall Outreach
purpose: The operational layer that turns M3 recall-cadence rules (`DPIM_RecallSchedule`) into multi-channel patient-facing outreach with self-schedule links. Per HTML m7 §7.12, M7.12 owns: multi-channel cadence operationalizing recall from M3, personalized messaging, self-schedule link, conversion analytics. **Recall outreach is transactional under TCPA**, not marketing — appointment-reminder + recall messages fall under TCPA's transactional / informational categorization for healthcare communications, distinct from marketing campaigns (M7.10). Different consent rules apply: transactional comms operate under broad treatment-purpose consent (HIPAA TPO + TCPA exempted categories) rather than per-channel-per-purpose marketing consent. **M7.12 owns NO primary DPIM class** — second sub-module in the orchestration-sub-module architectural pattern (M7.8 PORTAL, M7.12 RECALL, M7.14 FAM) per Step 4 Architectural-decision item #10.
dpimClasses: - M7.12 does NOT own a primary class — orchestration-sub-module pattern per Architectural-decision item #10
- Cross-module consumed: `DPIM_RecallSchedule` (M3 v3 — recall workflow); `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3); `DPIM_MessageTemplate` (M7.7 — recall templates); `DPIM_Appointment` (M3 — self-schedule outcome)
externalIntegrations: - M7.3 vendor matrix (Twilio + SendGrid + Voice + Push)
- i4 Patient Portal (self-schedule surface)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-RECALL-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL **operationalize M3 recall cadence** per HTML m7 §7.12 — when `DPIM_RecallSchedule` (M3) `dueDate` enters practice-configured outreach window (default 14 days before due), OSCAR SHALL trigger recall-outreach orchestration via M7.3 NOTIF. Recall-outreach SHALL NOT proceed if patient has an upcoming scheduled appointment that satisfies the recall (e.g., scheduled prophy within 30 days of recall due covers the recall). Recall workflow SHALL link `DPIM_Notification.correlationId` back to source `DPIM_RecallSchedule.id` for outcome tracking.
source: HTML m7 §7.12 ("Multi-channel cadence operationalizing recall from m3"); cross-module M3 RecallSchedule + M7.3 + M3.X (Schedule check)
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: M3 recall orchestration absent; M7-orchestration handoff pending

### [1]
reqId: M7-RECALL-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL classify recall outreach as **transactional/healthcare-operational under TCPA + HIPAA Treatment, Payment, Operations (TPO)** — recall comms operate under broad treatment-purpose consent rather than per-channel-per-purpose marketing consent. TCPA quiet-hours + frequency caps (M7-NOTIF-EXT-007/008) STILL APPLY; recall outreach is **exempt from A2P 10DLC marketing-registration requirement** per Twilio Trust Hub transactional-exemption flow. Per-channel patient communication preferences (M1 v3-new `DPIM_CommunicationPreference`) STILL apply — patient SHALL retain channel-opt-out rights even for transactional comms.
source: TCPA 47 USC 227 (transactional/healthcare exemptions per FCC 2012 Healthcare Order); HIPAA 45 CFR 164.501 (TPO); FCC 2012 Healthcare Order on healthcare-related calls; cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-007/008 + M7-NOTIF-EXT-012 + M1 CommunicationPreference
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Transactional-vs-marketing classification at orchestration level absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-RECALL-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **multi-channel cadence with fall-through** per HTML m7 §7.12 — recall outreach SHALL initiate on patient's preferred channel (per `DPIM_CommunicationPreference`), default SMS; on no-response after configured window (default 5 days), fall through to email; on no-response after another window (default 5 days), fall through to voice during TCPA-permitted hours. Each fall-through send SHALL be a separate `DPIM_Notification` correlated to the original `DPIM_RecallSchedule`. Cadence parameters SHALL be practice-configurable per `DPIM_PracticeSettings.recallCadence` (proposed D2 v3 addition).
source: HTML m7 §7.12 ("Multi-channel cadence"); cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-005 channel fall-through pattern + M1 CommunicationPreference + M2.5 D2 v3 update
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Recall cadence engine absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-RECALL-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL render **personalized recall messages** per HTML m7 §7.12 — recall templates (M7.7) SHALL substitute patient-specific tokens: `{{patient.firstName}}`, `{{recall.dueMonth}}`, `{{recall.recallType}}` (e.g., "6-month cleaning" / "perio maintenance" derived from `DPIM_RecallSchedule.recallType`), `{{practice.name}}`, `{{practice.address.short}}`, `{{provider.lastName}}` (last-seen provider), and `{{selfScheduleLink}}` (per M7-RECALL-EXT-005). Template selection SHALL match patient preferred language (M7-TMPL-EXT-007).
source: HTML m7 §7.12 ("personalized messaging"); cross-module M7-TMPL-EXT-004 token rendering + M7-TMPL-EXT-007 language variants
phase: P1
status: Not Started

### [4]
reqId: M7-RECALL-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL generate **self-schedule links** per HTML m7 §7.12 — each recall notification SHALL include a tokenized short-link routing to i4 Patient Portal self-schedule surface (cross-module M3 self-schedule workflow). Tokenized link SHALL: (a) authenticate the patient automatically on click (passwordless magic-link with short TTL — default 30 days); (b) pre-populate self-schedule form with recall context (procedure type, preferred provider, location); (c) be single-use (one click → one session — subsequent clicks require re-authentication); (d) NOT use Azure Blob SAS per M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS policy — link routes through OSCAR's authenticated API surface. Click events SHALL emit `dpim.scheduling.recall_self_schedule_link.clicked` for conversion analytics.
source: HTML m7 §7.12 ("self-schedule link"); HTML m7 integ ("i4 Portal"); cross-module M3 self-schedule + i4 Portal + M2-TEN-EXT-008 no-SAS + M5-SHARE-EXT-001 secure-link pattern
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Magic-link auth + self-schedule deep-link absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-RECALL-EXT-006

## 7.13
id: 7.13
name: Reactivation Campaigns
purpose: The inactive-patient re-engagement layer per HTML m7 §7.13 — inactive patient identification (>18 months no visit), tiered outreach, insurance-benefits-expiring messaging (Q4), conversion tracking. Reactivation is **marketing** (not transactional) under TCPA, distinct from M7.12 Recall — full marketing-consent + A2P 10DLC + CAN-SPAM compliance applies. Reactivation campaigns are specialized `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` sub-classes via `DPIM_ReactivationCampaign` (v3-new, property-doc-pending) per Step 4 Architectural-decision item #9 (sub-class hierarchy per HTML m7 §7.13 v3 declaration). Conversion metrics feed M11 + M2-DSO-EXT-008 valuation narrative per OQ-2.1.3 resolution. Materializes the architectural boundary between recall (transactional, treatment-purpose) and reactivation (marketing, win-back).
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_ReactivationCampaign` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — specialized `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` sub-class per Architectural-decision item #9
- Cross-module: `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` (M7.10 parent); `DPIM_PatientSegment` (M7.10 inactive-patient segment definition); `DPIM_Coverage` (M6 — benefits-expiring trigger); `DPIM_Patient.lastEncounterDate` (M1 / M2-PRAC-EXT-010 active-patient computation pattern)
externalIntegrations: - M7.10 marketing campaign infrastructure (TCPA + CAN-SPAM + A2P 10DLC apply)
- M6 RCM (benefits-expiring eligibility check)

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-REACT-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_ReactivationCampaign` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records as specialized `DPIM_MarketingCampaign` sub-class per HTML m7 §7.13 + Architectural-decision item #9. Each reactivation campaign SHALL have UUID v7 (inherited from MarketingCampaign), `inactivityThresholdMonths` (default 18, configurable 12–36), `outreachTiers[]` (BackboneElement[] — ordered list of tier definitions: tier-1 gentle / tier-2 firm / tier-3 last-chance), `tierIntervalDays` (default 30 days between tiers), `benefitsExpiringTrigger` (boolean — auto-trigger Q4 outreach to patients with year-end-expiring benefits per M6 cross-ref), and `state` (inherited from MarketingCampaign lifecycle).
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (ReactivationCampaign v3-new); HTML m7 §7.13; Module Catalog §8.2.13; Architectural-decision item #9
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; property doc pending; sub-class hierarchy needs DPIM Gate registration

### [1]
reqId: M7-REACT-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **inactive patient identification** per HTML m7 §7.13 — practice-wide query SHALL identify patients meeting: (a) `lastEncounterDate` older than `inactivityThresholdMonths` (default 18); (b) patient status = `active` (not `inactive` / `decedent` / `archived`); (c) no upcoming scheduled appointment; (d) opted-in for marketing comms (TCPA consent for marketing channel per `DPIM_TCPAConsent` D8 P.8.5 + not on `DPIM_SuppressionList`); (e) no `DPIM_FinancialAgreement` active payment plan (don't send marketing to patients in financial-hardship workflow). Patient identification SHALL emit `dpim.comm.patient_segment.computed` event (cross-module M7-MKT-EXT-012) when inactive-patient segment is refreshed.
source: HTML m7 §7.13 ("Inactive patient identification (>18 months no visit)"); cross-module M1 lastEncounter + M7.10 segment + M2-PRAC-EXT-010 active-patient definition + D8 TCPAConsent + SuppressionList + M6 FinancialAgreement
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Inactive-patient identification query absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-REACT-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **tiered outreach cadence** per HTML m7 §7.13 — tier-1 (gentle reminder, default ~18 months inactivity): "We miss you — your dental care is important." Tier-2 (firm, default tier-1+30 days no response): "It's been a while since your last visit. Schedule today." Tier-3 (last-chance, default tier-2+30 days no response): "This is our final outreach — confirm continued patient status or transition to inactive." Each tier SHALL use distinct `DPIM_MessageTemplate`. Patients who do not respond to tier-3 SHALL transition to `DPIM_Patient.status=inactive` automatically per OQ-7.12.5 recall-fatigue → reactivation transition logic (Architectural-decision item #8).
source: HTML m7 §7.13 ("tiered outreach"); cross-module M7.7 templates + M1 lifecycle + M7.10 cadence + Architectural-decision item #8
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Tiered-cadence orchestration absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-REACT-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **benefits-expiring outreach** per HTML m7 §7.13 — when `benefitsExpiringTrigger=true`, OSCAR SHALL evaluate patients quarterly (specifically October–December for calendar-year-expiring plans) for: (a) inactive patient meeting M7-REACT-EXT-002 criteria; (b) active `DPIM_Coverage` (M6) with `annualMaximum` (M2-INS-EXT-009) unused / partially-unused; (c) `DPIM_InsurancePlan.benefitYearStart=calendar_year` (M2-INS-EXT-014). Outreach SHALL include personalized benefits-remaining context (`{{coverage.annualMaximumRemaining}}`). Q4 benefits-expiring campaign SHALL be classified as `DPIM_MarketingCampaign.campaignType=reactivation_benefits_expiring` for attribution distinction.
source: HTML m7 §7.13 ("insurance-benefits-expiring messaging (Q4)"); cross-module M2-INS-EXT-009 + M2-INS-EXT-014 + M6 Coverage + M7.7 template tokens
phase: P2
status: Not Started
evidence: Benefits-expiring evaluation query absent

### [4]
reqId: M7-REACT-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL classify reactivation outreach as **marketing under TCPA** (distinct from M7.12 recall transactional classification per OQ-7.12.2 boundary). Reactivation SHALL: (a) require explicit marketing-channel consent per `DPIM_TCPAConsent.{phone_number, channel, purpose=marketing}` triple (M7-NOTIF-EXT-009); (b) require A2P 10DLC marketing-registration verification (M7-NOTIF-EXT-012 + M7-MKT-EXT-005); (c) honor `DPIM_SuppressionList` + `DPIM_OptOut`; (d) include CAN-SPAM unsubscribe link in email (M7-NOTIF-EXT-011). Without explicit marketing consent, patient SHALL NOT receive reactivation outreach — alternative: transition to inactive status per M7-REACT-EXT-003 tier-3 outcome without further outreach.
source: TCPA 47 USC 227; CAN-SPAM 15 USC 7701; A2P 10DLC; cross-module M7-NOTIF-EXT-009/011/012 + M7-MKT-EXT-005 + D8 TCPAConsent + SuppressionList + OptOut
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Marketing-vs-transactional gating at reactivation orchestration absent

### [5]
reqId: M7-REACT-EXT-006

Conversion metrics SHALL leverage `DPIM_CampaignAttribution` (M7-MKT-EXT-007) position-based 40/20/40 model. Reactivated patient lifetime-revenue contribution (cross-module M6) SHALL feed M11 Practice Pulse + M2-DSO-EXT-008 valuation narrative. Q4 benefits-expiring campaigns SHALL be analyzed distinctly from general win-back for attribution accuracy.
source: HTML m7 §7.13 ("conversion tracking"); cross-module M7-MKT-EXT-007 attribution + M11 + M2-DSO-EXT-008 + M6 revenue

Subscribers (M7.1 CommLog co-emission, M11 reactivation analytics, M1 patient-status auto-transition on tier-3 outcome, M2-DSO-EXT-008 valuation narrative feed) consume tokenized envelopes.

## 7.14
id: 7.14
name: Family Communication
purpose: The multi-recipient routing layer for family-account communications — parent/guardian notifications for minor patients, multi-recipient routing where one event triggers communications to multiple linked family members, and combined family-appointment digests consolidating multiple-family-member events into a single message. Per HTML m7 §7.14, cross-module with M1.4 Family Linking, M1.3 ResponsibleParty (HIPAA designated representative authorization), and M7.8 Portal family-view. **M7.14 owns NO primary DPIM class** — third sub-module in the orchestration-sub-module architectural pattern (M7.8 PORTAL + M7.12 RECALL + M7.14 FAM) per Step 4 Architectural-decision item #10.
dpimClasses: - M7.14 does NOT own a primary class — orchestration-sub-module pattern (third confirmation)
- Cross-module consumed: `DPIM_Family` + `DPIM_FamilyLink` (M1.4); `DPIM_Patient` (M1 — minor patients, age computation from `DOB`); `DPIM_ResponsibleParty` (M1.3 — HIPAA designated representative); `DPIM_PatientPortalAccount` (M1 — family-view access); `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (M1 v3-new — per-family-member channel preferences); `DPIM_ConsentRecord` (M8 NO-DATA — adult-child 18+ authorization); `DPIM_Notification` (M7.3 — actual sends)
externalIntegrations: None directly. Internal-only family-routing orchestration.

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-FAM-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **parent/guardian routing for minor patients** per HTML m7 §7.14 — when an event occurs for a patient under age 18 (computed from `DPIM_Patient.dob`, M1), OSCAR SHALL traverse `DPIM_FamilyLink` (M1.4) to identify linked parent/guardian recipients with `relationship` ∈ {`parent`, `legal_guardian`}. Each parent/guardian SHALL receive event-driven notifications (appointment reminders, post-op instructions, statements, recall outreach). HIPAA designated-representative authorization per `DPIM_ResponsibleParty` (M1.3) SHALL be verified — only authorized representatives receive PHI-containing communications.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.502(g) personal representatives; HTML m7 §7.14 ("Parent / guardian notifications for minor patients"); cross-module M1.4 + M1.3 + M1 DOB
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Family-traversal orchestration absent; M1.3/M1.4 cross-module references

### [1]
reqId: M7-FAM-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL handle **adult-child 18+ authorization transition** per HIPAA 45 CFR 164.502(g) — at patient's 18th birthday, parent loses default access to records. OSCAR SHALL: (a) auto-detect patient transition to age 18 nightly; (b) notify parent that access will lapse (90-day pre-birthday + on-birthday reminders); (c) invite adult-child to grant continued parent access via portal `DPIM_ConsentRecord` (M8 NO-DATA); (d) on 18th birthday with no authorization in place, terminate parent's family-account view + parent's recipient routing for the now-adult child's events; (e) preserve historical family-routing audit trail. *Cross-references OQ-7.8.1 from Batch 5.*
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 164.502(g) personal representatives; HTML m7 §7.14 family-routing implication; cross-module M1.4 + M1.3 + M8 ConsentRecord (NO-DATA) + M7-PORTAL-EXT-005 family-view
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Auto-transition workflow absent; M8 dependency

### [2]
reqId: M7-FAM-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **multi-recipient routing** per HTML m7 §7.14 — one event MAY produce notifications to multiple recipients linked by family relationship. Routing fan-out SHALL: (a) deduplicate when multiple family members share the same contact channel (e.g., two parents share one home phone — send once); (b) respect per-recipient `DPIM_CommunicationPreference` (M1 v3-new) — each recipient receives the message via their preferred channel; (c) tokenize personalization per recipient (token `{{recipient.firstName}}` resolves per-recipient, NOT per-patient); (d) emit one `DPIM_Notification` per (event × recipient × channel) tuple for accurate cross-module M7.10 attribution.
source: HTML m7 §7.14 ("multi-recipient routing"); cross-module M1.4 + M1 CommunicationPreference v3-new + M7.7 + M7.3
phase: P1

## 7.15
id: 7.15
name: Complaint / Grievance
purpose: The patient-complaint intake, routing, resolution, and compliance-escalation layer per HTML m7 §7.15. Owns: intake workflow, routing to office manager, resolution tracking, compliance escalation if applicable. DPIM v3-new classes `DPIM_Complaint` (D8 P.8.10 v3-aligned) + `DPIM_Grievance` (D8 property-doc-pending) distinguish operational complaints from formal grievances triggering external regulatory pathways (state dental boards, HHS OCR HIPAA complaint pathway, FTC consumer-protection pathways). Cross-module with M11 IntegrityAlerts surfacing → M12 fraud investigation when complaint pattern suggests fraud (M12.7 User Behavior Analytics). Per Architectural-decision item #11 (Complaint vs Grievance bifurcation pattern) and item #12 (practice-counsel privilege boundary).

**CAT-code namespace note:** M7-COMP-* (Complaint) shares CAT-code letters with M5-COMP-* (Compliance per M5.11 FMX / Imaging Compliance). Req IDs are module-namespaced (M7-COMP-* vs M5-COMP-*) — no actual collision. Cross-references in this markdown MUST include the module prefix to disambiguate.
dpimClasses: - `DPIM_Complaint` (v3-new; D8 P.8.10 v3-aligned)
- `DPIM_Grievance` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) — formal grievance triggering regulatory pathway
- `DPIM_AuditEntry` (Governance — every complaint event PS-7 WORM-audited)
- Cross-module: `DPIM_CommLog` (M7.1 — complaint intake logged); `DPIM_ActionCard` (M11 — manager routing); `DPIM_BreachIncident` (Governance v3-new — HIPAA-breach-related complaints)
externalIntegrations: - **State dental boards** — formal grievance escalation pathways (per-state — California Dental Board, Texas State Board of Dental Examiners, etc.)
- **HHS OCR** — HIPAA Privacy/Security Rule complaint pathway (45 CFR 160.306)
- **FTC Consumer Sentinel** — consumer-protection complaint pathway (for billing / advertising / review-gating violations)
- **State Attorney General offices** — state-level consumer-protection pathways
- **CMS / Medicare/Medicaid OIG** — fraud-related complaint pathways when patient files cross-cutting complaints

## requirements

### [0]
reqId: M7-COMP-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Complaint` (v3-new; D8 P.8.10 v3-aligned) records per intake. Each complaint SHALL have UUID v7, `patient` Reference (M1, **PHI boundary**; optional — non-patient complainants supported via free-text `complainantIdentity`), `intakeChannel` (`phone` / `portal` / `email` / `in_person` / `letter` / `referral_from_external` — e.g., state dental board forwarded inquiry), `intakeStaff` Reference (M10), `complaintCategory` (`clinical_quality` / `billing` / `wait_time` / `staff_conduct` / `facility` / `scheduling` / `privacy_hipaa` / `discrimination` / `other`), `severity` (`low` / `medium` / `high` / `urgent`), `narrative` (PHI), `state` (`received` / `under_review` / `resolved` / `escalated_to_grievance` / `closed`), `resolutionSummary` (PHI, on close), `intakeAt`, `resolvedAt`.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Complaint v3-new); D8 P.8.10 v3-aligned; HTML m7 §7.15; Module Catalog §8.2.15
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: v3-new class; D8 P.8.10 documented; no `comm` schema on main

### [1]
reqId: M7-COMP-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **multi-channel complaint intake** per HTML m7 §7.15 — patients MAY submit complaints via: (a) phone (M7.6 CallRecord with `disposition=complaint_received` triggers `DPIM_Complaint` creation per M7-CALL-EXT-008); (b) portal (M7.8 — dedicated complaint-submission form); (c) email (inbound email parsing per M7-THR-EXT-003 routes to complaint queue when M7.2 thread is escalated); (d) in-person (staff-entered intake form); (e) letter (paper-mail OCR or manual staff entry); (f) referral from external (state board / HHS OCR / lawyer's office forwarded inquiry). Each intake channel SHALL produce structured `DPIM_Complaint` record + linked `DPIM_CommLog` entry per M7-LOG-EXT-001 universal logging.
source: HTML m7 §7.15 ("Intake workflow"); cross-module M7.6 + M7.8 + M7.2 + M7.1
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Multi-channel intake orchestration absent

### [2]
reqId: M7-COMP-EXT-003
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **routing to office manager** per HTML m7 §7.15 — every `DPIM_Complaint.state=received` SHALL produce M11 `DPIM_ActionCard` ("Complaint received from {patient_name | anonymous} — category: {category}, severity: {severity}") routed to `office_manager` persona within practice-configured SLA (default 4 business hours; urgent complaints same-business-day). Cross-tenant routing for DSO-managed practices SHALL escalate to DSO-level compliance officer (`dso_compliance_officer` role per OQ-7.15.3) for `severity=urgent` + `category ∈ {privacy_hipaa, discrimination}`. Routing failure (no available `office_manager` recipient) SHALL escalate to `owner` role.
source: HTML m7 §7.15 ("routing to office manager"); cross-module M11 ActionCard + M10 office_manager role + M2.1 DSO escalation
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Complaint-routing orchestration absent

### [3]
reqId: M7-COMP-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **resolution tracking** per HTML m7 §7.15 — complaint state machine: `received` → `under_review` (manager has triaged) → `resolved` (manager + patient agree on outcome) → `closed` (resolution implemented, audit complete). Alternative path: `received` → `under_review` → `escalated_to_grievance` (creates linked `DPIM_Grievance` per M7-COMP-EXT-005). Resolution SHALL capture: outcome category (`apology_offered` / `refund_issued` / `service_redo` / `policy_changed` / `no_action_required` / `external_escalation`), `resolutionSummary` text, `resolutionStaff` Reference (M10). Resolution SLA SHALL be practice-configured (default 7 business days for `severity=medium`; 1 business day for `severity=urgent`). SLA breach SHALL escalate to `owner` role automatically.
source: HTML m7 §7.15 ("resolution tracking"); cross-module M2.5 D2 v3 SLA configuration
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: Resolution-tracking state machine absent

### [4]
reqId: M7-COMP-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain `DPIM_Grievance` (v3-new, **property-doc-pending**) records as formal grievance escalation — when `DPIM_Complaint.state=escalated_to_grievance`, OSCAR SHALL create a linked `DPIM_Grievance` with: `parentComplaint` Reference (`DPIM_Complaint.id`), `grievanceCategory` (`hipaa_privacy_security` / `state_dental_board` / `ftc_consumer_protection` / `state_ag_consumer` / `cms_oig_fraud` / `other_regulatory`), `regulatoryBody` (name + jurisdiction), `filingDate`, `filingDeadline` (per regulatory body — HIPAA OCR is 180 days from discovery per 45 CFR 160.306; state dental boards vary), `assignedCounsel` (optional — practice-counsel reference for legal involvement), `regulatoryResponse` (when received), `state` (`pre_filing` / `filed` / `under_investigation` / `resolved` / `dismissed`). **Materializes Architectural-decision item #11 (Complaint vs Grievance bifurcation pattern — first bifurcated-entity pattern in M7).**
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Grievance v3-new); HTML m7 §7.15 ("compliance escalation if applicable"); HIPAA 45 CFR 160.306; state dental board grievance procedures; Architectural-decision item #11
phase: P1
status: Not Started

### [5]
reqId: M7-COMP-EXT-006
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **compliance escalation pathways** per HTML m7 §7.15 — when `DPIM_Complaint.category ∈ {privacy_hipaa, discrimination, billing}` AND severity is `high` or `urgent`, OSCAR SHALL surface escalation-recommendation ActionCard to `owner` + practice-counsel role with regulatory pathway options: (a) HIPAA-Privacy-Security → HHS OCR 45 CFR 160.306 (180-day filing window); (b) Clinical-quality → state dental board grievance procedure; (c) Billing/advertising → FTC Consumer Sentinel + state AG; (d) Fraud-indicator-present → CMS OIG when Medicare/Medicaid involvement. Escalation is **practice-decision**, not automatic — OSCAR SHALL present options but SHALL NOT auto-file external complaints. HIPAA-related complaints SHALL additionally check if `DPIM_BreachIncident` (Governance v3-new) should be opened per M2-TEN-EXT-011 trigger conditions.
source: HIPAA 45 CFR 160.306 OCR complaint; state dental board procedures; FTC Consumer Sentinel; CMS OIG fraud pathway; cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-011 BreachIncident + practice-counsel role
phase: P1

evidence: Compliance-pathway recommendation engine absent

reqId: M7-DPIM-EXT-003

M7 introduces **7 new AI-artifact types** requiring `DPIM_AIInferenceRun` reference per derivation: (a) AI-suggested replies in 7.2 Conversation Threads (M7-THR-EXT-007); (b) AI sentiment classification in 7.9 Reputation (M7-REP-EXT-010); (c) AI topic extraction in 7.9 Reputation (M7-REP-EXT-010); (d) AI-suggested responses in 7.9 Reputation (M7-REP-EXT-012); (e) AI call transcription in 7.6 Call Records (M7-CALL-EXT-006); (f) AI-suggested call disposition in 7.6 Call Records (M7-CALL-EXT-008); (g) AI voicemail-to-ActionCard in 7.6 Call Records (M7-CALL-EXT-009). All seven artifacts SHALL pass through PS-2 Agent Orchestrator PHI boundary per M5-AI-EXT-011 + M5-AI-EXT-012 pattern.
source: DPIM v3 §1.7 AI provenance first-class; OQ-DPIM-M5-3 cross-module retrofit footprint expansion; cross-module M5-AI-EXT-001/011/012 pattern
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: AI provenance pattern retrofit broad — M5 + M7 = 13+ AI-artifact types across 2 modules; future M3/M11/M4/M12 expansion anticipated

### [3]
reqId: M7-DPIM-EXT-004
requirement: OSCAR SHALL implement **TCPA per-channel per-phone-number consent pattern** per D8 P.8.5 (`DPIM_TCPAConsent` v3-aligned). The consent record SHALL be a triple-keyed entity: `{phoneNumber, channel, purposeCategory}` — consent granted for SMS-appointment-reminders does NOT authorize SMS-marketing or Voice-appointment-reminders. M7.3 NOTIF SHALL evaluate the consent triple at every outbound send per M7-NOTIF-EXT-009. M1 owns consent capture; M7.3 owns enforcement. STOP keyword opt-out (M7-NOTIF-EXT-010) SHALL update `DPIM_OptOut` + `DPIM_SuppressionList` records and immediately invalidate the consent triple for the relevant (phone_number, channel) tuple.
source: DPIM D8 P.8.5 TCPAConsent v3-aligned; TCPA 47 USC 227(b); FCC TCPA Order; cross-module M1 + M7-NOTIF-EXT-009/010
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: TCPAConsent triple-keyed evaluation absent at SDK validation gate

### [4]
reqId: M7-DPIM-EXT-005
requirement: OSCAR SHALL maintain **FHIR R4 + FHIR Dental Data Exchange IG mapping discipline** per DPIM v3 §1.8 — every Communication-domain class SHALL declare FHIR R4 resource mapping. Specific mappings: `DPIM_InboundReferral` ↔ `ServiceRequest` + `Patient` + `DiagnosticReport` + `ImagingStudy` per FHIR Dental DEX IG (cross-module M7-REFIN-EXT-003); `DPIM_OutboundReferral` ↔ same Bundle structure outbound (M7-REFOUT-EXT-004); `DPIM_PatientPortalAccount` ↔ patient portal access bridges to Cures Act §4002 `DPIM_DataExportRequest` (cross-module M2-TEN-EXT-012 + M7-PORTAL-EXT-008).
source: DPIM v3 §1.8 FHIR R4 mapping discipline; FHIR Dental Data Exchange IG; 21st Century Cures Act §4002; cross-module M7-REFIN-EXT-003 + M7-REFOUT-EXT-004 + M7-PORTAL-EXT-008 + M2-TEN-EXT-012
phase: P1
status: Not Started
evidence: FHIR mapping incomplete for v3-new Communication classes; cross-module Cures Act fulfillment partial

### [0]
reqId: M7-INTG-EXT-001
requirement: OSCAR SHALL register all M7 external integrations in `DPIM_Integration` (Governance v3-new) per M2-INTG-EXT-001 + M5-INTG-EXT-001 pattern. M7-specific entries (~13 vendors): Twilio (SMS + Voice + A2P 10DLC); SendGrid (email); VoIP vendors (Mango Voice, Weave, RingCentral, NexHealth); Reputation platforms (Google My Business, Facebook Graph, Healthgrades, BirdEye); Direct Messaging HISP (vendor TBD per Architectural-decision item #13); Cloud-fax (vendor TBD); Azure OpenAI Whisper; Azure OpenAI gpt-4o-mini. Each integration SHALL track BAA status, last-health-check, vendor capability matrix, per-tenant credential references.
source: DPIM v3 LOCKED §4 (Integration v3-new); HTML m7 deps.external; cross-module M2-INTG-EXT-001 + M5-INTG-EXT-001 patterns + Governance domain

### [1]
reqId: M7-INTG-EXT-002
requirement: OSCAR SHALL integrate with **Twilio** for SMS + Voice per M7-NOTIF-EXT-002 + M7-CALL-EXT-002. Integration SHALL: (a) authenticate via Key Vault-stored Twilio API credentials (IF-4); (b) submit outbound SMS via Twilio Programmable Messaging API; (c) submit outbound voice via Twilio Voice + TTS API; (d) receive inbound SMS webhooks for M7-THR-EXT-002 thread matching + M7-NOTIF-EXT-010 STOP keyword detection; (e) maintain **A2P 10DLC registration** via Twilio Trust Hub per M7-NOTIF-EXT-012; (f) track Twilio Message SID + Voice Call SID for `DPIM_Notification.vendorMessageId` audit. Twilio BAA SHALL be tracked via `DPIM_BusinessAssociateAgreement` (M2-TEN-EXT-009).
source: M7-NOTIF-EXT-002/010/012 + M7-CALL-EXT-002; A2P 10DLC; CTIA Short Code Guidelines; HIPAA 45 CFR 164.504(e) BAA; cross-module IF-4 + M2-TEN-EXT-009
phase: P1
status: Not Started
